# Phase 3 core — Projects, Files and Import Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make the app usable for getting a real schedule in: a working Projects page with the new project attributes, a project files page whose schedule rules the database enforces, and single-file import of P6 XER, MS Project XML, Excel and CSV that keeps the original file, the mapping and an import report for Phase 4.

**Architecture:** Three pgTAP-tested migrations add the columns, triggers, views and five RPCs; each lands with the adapter code that adapts to it. The shared repository contract then grows slice by slice on both adapters. A pure TypeScript parser in `src/import` (ESLint-bounded, golden-tested in Node, run in a module Web Worker) builds the `ImportModel` and the report. The UI tasks build the Projects page, the files page and the stepped import dialog on hooks.

**Tech Stack:** the Phase 2 stack plus `fast-xml-parser` 5, `papaparse` 5, SheetJS CE `xlsx` 0.20.3 (vendored), `fflate` 0.8 and `@tanstack/react-table` 8.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-05-phase3-core-design.md` ("the spec"). It copies from `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-03-phase3-projects-files-library-design.md` ("P3 full"), which a task cites only where it needs a detail. Each task names its spec sections; values there are requirements even when the task doesn't repeat them.

## Global Constraints

- CLAUDE.md holds, and so do the Phase 2 plan's Global Constraints (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-phase2-supabase.md` lines 13–58) except its dependency bullet (replaced below) and "P2 migrations may be edited in place" (they are never edited now).
- Every task ends with `npm run check`, `npm run test:db`, `npm run test:integration`, `npm run test:e2e` and `npm run test:e2e:supabase` green (spec D36); a task changes every caller its change breaks.
- Docker isn't on the Git Bash PATH: every Bash block that runs the Supabase CLI (`db:*`, `test:db`, `test:integration`, `test:e2e:supabase`) or `docker` starts with `export PATH="/c/Program Files/Docker/Docker/resources/bin:$PATH"`. The local stack runs for every task.
- After a migration or a seed change, run `npm run db:reset` before `test:integration` and `test:e2e:supabase`.
- Both e2e suites use port 4174: run them one after the other, never in parallel.
- Migrations: exactly three new ones, created with `npx supabase migration new p3_core_projects`, `… p3_core_schedules`, `… p3_core_imports`; `npm run db:types` in the same commit as each.
- Every migration ends by revoking `execute` on each function it creates (or replaces) from `public, anon, authenticated`, then grants `execute` to `authenticated` only on `delete_project`, `set_current_schedule`, `update_schedule_details`, `set_schedules_archived`, `delete_schedules`.
- SQL: `p_` parameters; definer and trigger functions `security definer set search_path = ''` with qualified names; invoker functions also `set search_path = ''`; user errors `raise exception using errcode = 'check_violation', hint = 'app', message = '<copy>'`; permission errors `errcode = 'insufficient_privilege'`; a project an invoker RPC can't see raises `errcode = 'no_data_found', hint = 'app', message = 'Project not found'`.
- Invoker RPCs check the caller's role themselves against `app.my_project_ids('editor')` (`'owner'` for `delete_schedules` and `delete_project`) and raise `insufficient_privilege` when the affected row count differs from the id count; array RPCs run R9 count and one project → R9 all found (a select under RLS) → role check → write.
- Dependencies, the closed list: `fast-xml-parser@^5.3.5`, `papaparse@^5` + `@types/papaparse@^5` (dev), `xlsx` from `vendor/xlsx-0.20.3.tgz`, `fflate@^0.8`, `@tanstack/react-table@^8`. Nothing else; no ExcelJS, no Comlink.
- Layer rules (S7, ESLint from Task 1): `src/import/**` imports no React, `@/hooks`, `@/components`, `@/features`, `@/pages` or `@/data/*` other than `import type` from `@/data/types`; only `src/hooks/**` imports `@/import/client` (others `import type` only); `xlsx`, `papaparse`, `fast-xml-parser`, `fflate` are imported statically only inside `src/import/**`, elsewhere in `src/` through `import()`.
- Copy is verbatim from the spec; adapter copy ends with a period ("This code is already used in this project."), form copy doesn't ("This code is already used in this project").
- Mock store: `MOCK_DB_VERSION` and `MockDb['version']` become `3` in Task 3 and stay `3`; the key stays `mock-db.v1`.
- Test data: the spec's fictional seed (§4.5); seed emails `@example.com`, pgTAP emails `@test.dev`; local seed password `localdemo2026`.
- Real exports: nothing under `real-exports/` is read before Task 24, and nothing there is ever copied or committed; fixtures are our own files (spec D2, §5.11).
- Lint stays at `0 errors, 13 warnings`: new `.tsx` files export components and types only; constants and helpers live in `.ts` files.
- Parser code never calls `new Date(string)`; wall-clock values are `'YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm'` strings; unit tests run in `America/Los_Angeles`.
- Test counts: each task's Expected lines give the new totals. Baseline at `1ef5385`: `npm run check` 70 files / 410 tests, `Contrast OK: 37 checks passed`; `test:db` 7 files / 106; `test:integration` 6 files / 45; `test:e2e` 15 passed; `test:e2e:supabase` 4 passed.

## Review Focus

1. **Windows exports: CRLF line ends, a trailing blank line and a UTF-8 BOM in XER and CSV files.** Expected: read exactly as LF files; the BOM never becomes part of `ERMHDR` or the first header. Pinned in Task 10 (tokenizer) and Task 12 (CSV reader).
2. **Excel-saved tables with empty trailing columns and fully empty rows** (`Kod;Ad;;;`, `;;;;`). Expected: empty columns aren't offered as headers, and rows of only empty cells are ignored without an issue and aren't counted as read. Pinned in Task 12.
3. **Double clicks and a held Enter on commit buttons** ("Import", "Save", "Delete project", "Delete", "Make current"). Expected: one request, one toast. Pinned in Tasks 19, 21 and 23.
4. **Closing the import dialog, or choosing another file, while the worker is still reading.** Expected: the worker is terminated or the stale answer dropped; no late toast, step change or error. Pinned in Tasks 14 and 22.
5. **Projects and schedules without optional values** (no client, city, status date, coordinates, notes; P2-era schedules without a code). Expected: "—" in cells, never "null", nulls last in sorts; search and filters never throw. Pinned in Tasks 18 and 20.

### Task 1: Dependencies, import rules, licence notices and V1–V7

**Spec:** §2.4 (Setup row), §3 (layer rules, new dependencies, licence notices), §2.2 S7, S10, §8 V1–V7, §9 (Config, CLAUDE.md). P3 full §3 "Licence notices".

**Files:**
- Create: `vendor/xlsx-0.20.3.tgz` (downloaded), `scripts/third-party-licenses.mjs`, `scripts/third-party-licenses.test.mjs`, `scripts/import-boundaries.test.mjs`, `integration/p3-assumptions.itest.ts`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-05-phase3-core-verification.md`
- Modify: `package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `eslint.config.js`, `vite.config.ts`, `.gitignore`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `CLAUDE.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`scripts/third-party-licenses.mjs`, ESM, uses `scripts/is-main-module.mjs`):
  ```js
  export function licenseOf(pkg);           // pkg.license string · pkg.license.type · pkg.licenses[].type joined with ' OR ' · 'UNKNOWN'
  export function collectPackages(tree);    // the `npm ls --omit=dev --all --json --long` tree without its root; one entry per name@version (the one with a path wins); sorted by name, then version
  export function formatLicenses(packages, readLicenseFiles); // readLicenseFiles(dir) → Array<[fileName, text]>
  // CLI: node scripts/third-party-licenses.mjs [outDir=dist] runs `npm ls --omit=dev --all --json --long` (a non-zero exit with JSON on stdout still counts),
  //   reads each package's files matching /^(licen[cs]e|notice|copying|third[-_]?party)/i, writes <outDir>/third-party-licenses.txt
  //   and prints "Wrote {n} packages to {path}".
  ```
  File format: first line `Third-party licences`, a blank line, then per package a line of 64 `=`, `{name} {version}`, `License: {license}`, a blank line and, per file, `--- {file} ---` and its text.
- Produces (`vite.config.ts`): an inline plugin `thirdPartyLicenses()` with `apply: 'build'` that runs `process.execPath scripts/third-party-licenses.mjs <resolved outDir>` in `closeBundle`, so `dist`, `dist-e2e` and `dist-supabase` all ship `third-party-licenses.txt` (served at `/third-party-licenses.txt`). Vitest never runs it.
- Produces (`eslint.config.js`): the S7 rules as five blocks; each later block that touches a file repeats the adapter rule, because a flat-config block replaces `no-restricted-imports` instead of merging it:
  1. `src/**/*.{ts,tsx}` ignoring `src/data/**`, `src/test/**`, `src/hooks/**`, `src/import/**`: P2's adapter patterns, plus pattern group `['@/import', '@/import/*']` ("Only hooks use the import parser, through @/import/client."), plus `paths` for `xlsx`, `papaparse`, `fast-xml-parser`, `fflate` ("Parser libraries are imported statically only inside src/import; elsewhere use import().").
  2. `src/hooks/**/*.{ts,tsx}`: the adapter patterns, pattern group `['@/import', '@/import/*', '!@/import/client']` with `allowTypeImports: true`, and the four `paths`.
  3. `src/data/**/*.{ts,tsx}`: the four `paths` only.
  4. `src/import/**/*.{ts,tsx}`: pattern group `['react', 'react/*', 'react-dom', 'react-dom/*', '@/hooks', '@/hooks/*', '@/components', '@/components/*', '@/features', '@/features/*', '@/pages', '@/pages/*', '@/data', '@/data/*', '!@/data/types']` ("src/import is pure TypeScript: no React, hooks, UI or adapters.").
  5. `src/import/client.ts`: block 4's group plus `{ group: ['./*', '!./protocol'], allowTypeImports: true, message: 'client.ts statically imports only ./protocol; it loads ./in-thread with import().' }`.
- Produces: `.gitignore` line `/real-exports/` (with the comment `# The owner's real schedule exports: read by path in Task 24 only, never committed (spec D2)`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing tests**

`scripts/third-party-licenses.test.mjs`:

```js
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { collectPackages, formatLicenses, licenseOf } from './third-party-licenses.mjs';

const tree = {
  name: 'scheduler-app',
  version: '0.1.0',
  path: '/app',
  dependencies: {
    'react-dom': {
      name: 'react-dom', version: '19.3.1', license: 'MIT', path: '/app/node_modules/react-dom',
      dependencies: {
        scheduler: { name: 'scheduler', version: '0.27.0', license: 'MIT', path: '/app/node_modules/scheduler' },
        react: { version: '19.3.1' },
      },
    },
    react: { name: 'react', version: '19.3.1', license: 'MIT', path: '/app/node_modules/react' },
    xlsx: { name: 'xlsx', version: '0.20.3', license: 'Apache-2.0', path: '/app/node_modules/xlsx' },
    old: { name: 'old', version: '1.0.0', licenses: [{ type: 'MIT' }, { type: 'BSD-2-Clause' }], path: '/app/node_modules/old' },
  },
};

describe('third-party-licenses', () => {
  it('lists each production package once, by name', () => {
    expect(collectPackages(tree).map((p) => `${p.name}@${p.version}`)).toEqual([
      'old@1.0.0', 'react@19.3.1', 'react-dom@19.3.1', 'scheduler@0.27.0', 'xlsx@0.20.3',
    ]);
  });

  it('reads licence types in their old and new forms', () => {
    expect(licenseOf({ license: 'MIT' })).toBe('MIT');
    expect(licenseOf({ license: { type: 'ISC' } })).toBe('ISC');
    expect(licenseOf({ licenses: [{ type: 'MIT' }, { type: 'BSD-2-Clause' }] })).toBe('MIT OR BSD-2-Clause');
    expect(licenseOf({})).toBe('UNKNOWN');
  });

  it('writes every package with its licence and its licence and NOTICE texts', () => {
    const files = {
      '/app/node_modules/xlsx': [['LICENSE', 'Apache text'], ['NOTICE', 'SheetJS notice']],
      '/app/node_modules/react': [['LICENSE', 'MIT text']],
    };
    const text = formatLicenses(collectPackages(tree), (dir) => files[dir] ?? []);
    expect(text.startsWith('Third-party licences\n')).toBe(true);
    expect(text).toContain('xlsx 0.20.3\nLicense: Apache-2.0\n');
    expect(text).toContain('--- NOTICE ---\nSheetJS notice');
    expect(text).toContain('scheduler 0.27.0\nLicense: MIT\n');
    expect(text.indexOf('react 19.3.1')).toBeLessThan(text.indexOf('xlsx 0.20.3'));
  });
});
```

`scripts/import-boundaries.test.mjs`:

```js
// @vitest-environment node
import { ESLint } from 'eslint';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

const eslint = new ESLint();
async function restrictedImports(filePath, code) {
  const [result] = await eslint.lintText(`${code}\nexport {};\n`, { filePath });
  return result.messages.filter((m) => m.ruleId === 'no-restricted-imports').length;
}

describe('import boundaries (S7)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['src/import/a.ts', "import { useState } from 'react';", 1],
    ['src/import/a.ts', "import { useNow } from '@/hooks/useNow';", 1],
    ['src/import/a.ts', "import { seed } from '@/data/mock/seed';", 1],
    ['src/import/a.ts', "import type { ImportReport } from '@/data/types';", 0],
    ['src/import/a.ts', "import { foldForMatch } from '@/lib/search';", 0],
    ['src/import/table/csv.ts', "import Papa from 'papaparse';", 0],
    ['src/import/client.ts', "import { analyze } from './index';", 1],
    ['src/import/client.ts', "import type { WorkerRequest } from './protocol';", 0],
    ['src/features/a.tsx', "import { analyze } from '@/import/index';", 1],
    ['src/features/a.tsx', "import { createImportClient } from '@/import/client';", 1],
    ['src/features/a.tsx', "import Papa from 'papaparse';", 1],
    ['src/features/a.tsx', "import { seed } from '@/data/mock/seed';", 1],
    ['src/hooks/a.ts', "import { createImportClient } from '@/import/client';", 0],
    ['src/hooks/a.ts', "import type { InspectOutcome } from '@/import/protocol';", 0],
    ['src/hooks/a.ts', "import { analyze } from '@/import/index';", 1],
    ['src/data/supabase/a.ts', "import { gzipSync } from 'fflate';", 1],
  ])('%s: %s gives %i restricted-import errors', async (filePath, code, errors) => {
    expect(await restrictedImports(filePath, code)).toBe(errors);
  });
});
```

`integration/p3-assumptions.itest.ts` (V2–V5 in Node, V7 on the local stack):

```ts
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { XMLParser } from 'fast-xml-parser';
import Papa from 'papaparse';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import * as XLSX from 'xlsx';
import { readLocalStack } from './local-stack';

function workbook(date1904: boolean): Uint8Array {
  const book = XLSX.utils.book_new();
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(book, XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([['Start'], [46096.5]]), 'Plan');
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(book, XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([[9]]), 'Other');
  book.Workbook = { WBProps: { date1904 } };
  return new Uint8Array(XLSX.write(book, { bookType: 'xlsx', type: 'array' }) as ArrayBuffer);
}

describe('phase 3 core assumptions', () => {
  it('V2: SheetJS writes and reads both date systems with the same serials', () => {
    for (const date1904 of [false, true]) {
      const read = XLSX.read(workbook(date1904), { type: 'array', dense: true, cellDates: false, cellFormula: false });
      expect(Boolean(read.Workbook?.WBProps?.date1904)).toBe(date1904);
      expect(XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(read.Sheets.Plan!, { header: 1, raw: true })).toEqual([['Start'], [46096.5]]);
    }
  });

  it('V2: SheetJS reads only the sheets and rows it is asked for', () => {
    const read = XLSX.read(workbook(false), { type: 'array', dense: true, sheets: ['Plan'], sheetRows: 1 });
    expect(read.SheetNames).toEqual(['Plan', 'Other']);
    expect(XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(read.Sheets.Plan!, { header: 1 })).toEqual([['Start']]);
    expect(read.Sheets.Other).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('V3: fast-xml-parser leaves entities as written and values as text', () => {
    const parser = new XMLParser({
      parseTagValue: false, processEntities: false, removeNSPrefix: true, ignoreAttributes: true,
      isArray: (name: string) => name === 'Task',
    });
    const doc = parser.parse(
      '<?xml version="1.0"?><Project xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/project"><Task><Name>Kazı &amp; Dolgu &#x130;</Name><OutlineNumber>1.10</OutlineNumber></Task></Project>',
    ) as { Project: { Task: unknown[] } };
    expect(doc.Project.Task).toEqual([{ Name: 'Kazı &amp; Dolgu &#x130;', OutlineNumber: '1.10' }]);
  });

  it('V4: Papa Parse splits a decoded string on the delimiter it is given', () => {
    expect(Papa.parse<string[]>('Kod;Süre\nA1;2,5\n', { delimiter: ';', skipEmptyLines: true }).data).toEqual([
      ['Kod', 'Süre'], ['A1', '2,5'],
    ]);
  });

  it('V5: TextDecoder knows the encodings the parser offers, with fatal', () => {
    expect(new TextDecoder('windows-1254', { fatal: true }).decode(new Uint8Array([0xdd, 0xfe, 0xf0]))).toBe('İşğ');
    expect(new TextDecoder('windows-1252', { fatal: true }).decode(new Uint8Array([0x8e]))).toBe('Ž');
    expect(new TextDecoder('utf-16le', { fatal: true }).decode(new Uint8Array([0x30, 0x01]))).toBe('İ');
    expect(new TextDecoder('utf-16be', { fatal: true }).decode(new Uint8Array([0x01, 0x30]))).toBe('İ');
    expect(() => new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: true }).decode(new Uint8Array([0xfd]))).toThrow();
  });

  it('V7: storage.download returns the bytes of an object the reader may select', async () => {
    const stack = readLocalStack();
    const options = { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false } };
    const admin = createClient(stack.apiUrl, stack.secretKey, options);
    const email = `v7.${Date.now()}@example.com`;
    await admin.auth.admin.createUser({ email, password: 'letters12345', email_confirm: true });
    const client = createClient(stack.apiUrl, stack.publishableKey, options);
    await client.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password: 'letters12345' });
    const { data: workspace } = await client.rpc('create_team_workspace', { p_name: `V7 ${Date.now() % 100000}` });
    const path = `${(workspace as { id: string }).id}/${crypto.randomUUID()}/${crypto.randomUUID()}.xer.gz`;
    const bytes = new Uint8Array([0x1f, 0x8b, 8, 0, 1, 2, 3]);
    expect((await client.storage.from('schedule-imports').upload(path, new Blob([bytes]))).error).toBeNull();
    const { data, error } = await client.storage.from('schedule-imports').download(path);
    expect(error).toBeNull();
    expect(new Uint8Array(await data!.arrayBuffer())).toEqual(bytes);
  });
});
```

- Produces (database), names exact:

  - `public.delete_project(p_project_id uuid) returns table (bucket text, path text)`, invoker, `language plpgsql set search_path = ''`, in this order: no visible project → P0002 `Project not found` (hint `app`); not in `app.my_project_ids('owner')` → `insufficient_privilege`; `return query` the rows `('schedule-imports', source_file_path)` of its schedules that have one and `('project-covers', cover_image_path)` when set; `delete from public.projects` that id (cascades to schedules and their shares; tasks keep their rows with `project_id` null); a deleted row count other than 1 → `insufficient_privilege`.

```sql
\ir _helpers.psql
select plan(26);

-- columns and checks, as the table owner

select throws_ok($$ update public.projects set status = 'paused' where code = 'P1' $$, '23514', null, 'statuses come from the list');
select throws_ok($$ update public.projects set stage = 'design' where code = 'P1' $$, '23514', null, 'stages come from the list');
select throws_ok($$ update public.projects set sector = 'mixed' where code = 'P1' $$, '23514', null, 'sectors come from the list');
select throws_ok($$ update public.projects set project_type = 'space' where code = 'P1' $$, '23514', null, 'project types come from the list');
select lives_ok($$ update public.projects set status = 'on_hold', stage = 'closeout', sector = 'public_private', project_type = 'energy' where code = 'P1' $$, 'the new values are stored');
select lives_ok($$ update public.projects set project_type = 'transportation' where code = 'P1' $$, 'transportation is a project type');
select lives_ok($$ update public.projects set project_type = 'water' where code = 'P1' $$, 'water is a project type');
select throws_ok($$ update public.projects set location_lat = 41.0151 where code = 'P1' $$, '23514',
  'new row for relation "projects" violates check constraint "projects_location_pair_check"', 'a latitude needs a longitude');
select throws_ok($$ update public.projects set location_lat = 91, location_lng = 0 where code = 'P1' $$, '23514', null, 'latitudes stay within -90 and 90');
select throws_ok($$ update public.projects set location_lat = 0, location_lng = 181 where code = 'P1' $$, '23514', null, 'longitudes stay within -180 and 180');
select lives_ok($$ update public.projects set location_lat = 41.0151, location_lng = -28.9795 where code = 'P1' $$, 'a coordinate pair is stored');
select results_eq($$ select location_lat, location_lng from public.projects where code = 'P1' $$,
  $$ values (41.015100::numeric, -28.979500::numeric) $$, 'coordinates keep six decimals');

-- R11: status_date is not in the trigger's column list
update public.projects set updated_at = '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z' where code = 'P1';
select tests.authenticate_as(tests.user_id('ed@test.dev'));
update public.projects set status_date = '2026-03-01' where code = 'P1';
select is((select updated_at from public.projects where code = 'P1'), '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z'::timestamptz, 'R11: a status date write leaves updated_at alone');
update public.projects set status = 'completed' where code = 'P1';
select is((select updated_at from public.projects where code = 'P1'), now(), 'R11: a status write moves updated_at');
select tests.clear_authentication();
update public.projects set updated_at = '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z' where code = 'P1';
select tests.authenticate_as(tests.user_id('ed@test.dev'));
update public.projects set location_lat = 40, location_lng = 29 where code = 'P1';
select is((select updated_at from public.projects where code = 'P1'), now(), 'R11: a coordinate write moves updated_at');

-- R10, R12: delete_project
select throws_ok($$ select * from public.delete_project(tests.project_id('D1')) $$, '42501', null, 'R10: editors cannot delete projects');
select tests.authenticate_as(tests.user_id('vi@test.dev'));
select throws_ok($$ select * from public.delete_project(tests.project_id('D1')) $$, '42501', null, 'R10: nor can viewers');
select tests.authenticate_as(tests.user_id('out@test.dev'));
select throws_ok($$ select * from public.delete_project(tests.project_id('D1')) $$, '42501', null, 'R10: nor a share recipient, who sees the project');
select tests.authenticate_as(tests.user_id('sam@test.dev'));
select throws_ok($$ select * from public.delete_project(tests.project_id('D1')) $$, 'P0002', 'Project not found', 'a project the caller cannot see is not found');
select throws_ok($$ select * from public.delete_project(gen_random_uuid()) $$, 'P0002', 'Project not found', 'nor is an unknown one');
select tests.authenticate_as(tests.user_id('olive@test.dev'));
select results_eq($$ select bucket, path from public.delete_project(tests.project_id('D1')) order by bucket $$,
  $$ values ('project-covers'::text, 'covers/d1.jpg'::text), ('schedule-imports', 'imports/d1.xer') $$,
  'R12: owners delete a project and get its stored objects back');
select tests.clear_authentication();
select is_empty($$ select 1 from public.projects where code = 'D1' $$, 'R12: the project is gone');
select is_empty($$ select 1 from public.schedules where name like 'Doomed%' $$, 'R12: with its schedules');
select is_empty($$ select 1 from public.schedule_shares where shared_with = tests.user_id('out@test.dev') $$, 'R12: and their shares');
select results_eq($$ select project_id is null from public.tasks where title = 'Check the doomed plan' $$, $$ values (true) $$, 'tasks stay, without the project');

select * from finish();
rollback;
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2's migration.
- Produces (database; trigger names fix the firing order `schedules_apply_baseline` → `schedules_fill_status_date` → `schedules_prepare_new` on insert):
  - `schedules` columns, indexes and checks of §4.6 verbatim: `code` + `schedules_project_code_uq (project_id, lower(code)) where code is not null`; `notes`; `is_current` + `schedules_one_current_uq (project_id) where is_current`; `baseline` + `schedules_one_primary_uq (project_id) where baseline = 'primary'`; `update_number` + `schedules_project_update_uq (project_id, update_number)`; `archived_at` + `check (not (is_current and archived_at is not null))`; `activity_count`.
  - `schedules_set_updated_at` recreated `before update of name, code, notes, status_date, baseline, update_number, archived_at`.
  - `app.prepare_new_schedule()` (`schedules_prepare_new`, before insert): when `(select auth.uid())` is not null and `new.project_id not in (select app.my_project_ids('editor'))` → `insufficient_privilege`; then `perform … from public.projects where id = new.project_id for update`; `new.is_current := not exists (select 1 from public.schedules where project_id = new.project_id)`; for a user call, `nullif(trim(new.code), '') is null` → R8 "Enter a schedule code.".
  - `app.guard_schedule_update()` (`schedules_guard_update`, before update), user calls only: R2 `new.is_current and new.archived_at is not null` → "Make another schedule current before archiving this one."; R4 `not old.is_current and new.is_current and new.archived_at is not null` → "Restore this schedule before making it current."; R8 `old.code is not null and new.code is null` → "Enter a schedule code.".
  - `app.apply_baseline()` (`schedules_apply_baseline`, before insert or update of baseline), every caller: a new `'primary'` (insert, or update from another value) first runs `update public.schedules set baseline = 'baseline' where project_id = new.project_id and baseline = 'primary' and id <> new.id`; `'baseline'` from `'none'` (or on insert) becomes `'primary'` when no other schedule of the project is primary.
  - `app.check_current_after_delete()` (`schedules_check_current_after_delete`, after delete for each statement, `referencing old table as deleted_rows`): for any project of `deleted_rows` that still exists and still has schedules but none current → R3 "Make another schedule current before deleting this one.".
  - `app.sync_project_status_date()` (`schedules_sync_project_status_date`, after insert or update of is_current, status_date): when `new.is_current`, `update public.projects set status_date = new.status_date where id = new.project_id and status_date is distinct from new.status_date`.
  - `app.guard_project_status_date()` (`projects_guard_status_date`, before update of status_date on projects): a current schedule whose status date differs from `new.status_date` → R6 "The status date follows the current schedule.".
  - `app.backfill_current_schedules()`, called once by the migration (§4.6 order: `status_date desc nulls last, created_at desc, id desc`).
  - `public.set_current_schedule(p_schedule_id uuid) returns void`, definer: unknown id → R9 "Some schedules are no longer available. Refresh and try again."; project not in `app.my_project_ids('editor')` → `insufficient_privilege`; archived → R4; lock the project's schedule rows `for update`; already current → return; else clear the old current, then set the new one (two statements: the unique index is checked per row).
  - `public.update_schedule_details(p_schedule_id uuid, p_name text, p_code text, p_status_date date, p_notes text, p_baseline text, p_update_number int) returns void`, invoker: not visible → R9 copy; role check (editor); `nullif(trim(p_code), '') is null` → R8 copy; one `update … set name = trim(p_name), code = trim(p_code), status_date = p_status_date, notes = nullif(trim(p_notes), ''), baseline = p_baseline, update_number = p_update_number` (the column checks refuse a bad baseline, name or notes); row count ≠ 1 → `insufficient_privilege`.
  - `public.set_schedules_archived(p_schedule_ids uuid[], p_archived boolean) returns void`, invoker: an empty array returns at once; more than 500 distinct ids → "Select 500 schedules or fewer."; found rows in more than one project → "Select schedules from one project."; fewer found than distinct ids → R9 copy; role check (editor); `update … set archived_at = case when p_archived then coalesce(archived_at, now()) end where id = any(p_schedule_ids)`; count check.
  - `public.delete_schedules(p_schedule_ids uuid[]) returns setof text`, invoker: the same R9 checks, role check (owner), delete, return the deleted rows' non-null `source_file_path`s; count check.
  - `public.project_summaries` recreated: `p.*`, `schedule_count` (`archived_at is null`), `total_schedule_count` (all), `current_schedule_id`, `current_schedule_name`. View `public.schedule_items` (`security_invoker = true`): `id, project_id, name, code, notes, status_date, source, source_file_name, is_current, baseline, update_number, archived_at, activity_count, created_at, updated_at` listed explicitly. Both views: select to `authenticated`, all to `service_role`.
  - Grants: `revoke insert on public.schedules from authenticated`; `grant insert (project_id, name, status_date, source, source_file_name, source_file_path, code, notes, baseline, update_number)`; `grant update (name, status_date, code, notes, baseline, update_number, archived_at)`.
- Produces (TypeScript):
  ```ts
  // src/data/types.ts — Schedule gains (after name):
  code: string | null;
  // src/data/repository.ts
  export interface NewScheduleInput { projectId: ID; name: string; code: string }
  export interface ImportScheduleInput { projectId: ID; name: string; code: string; file: File }
  // src/data/mock/schedule-rules.ts (Tasks 7–8 add the other rules here)
  export const SCHEDULE_CODE_PATTERN: RegExp;                 // /^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,24}$/
  export function scheduleCodeKey(code: string): string;      // trimmed, toLowerCase()
  export function checkNewScheduleCode(schedules: readonly Schedule[], projectId: ID, code: string): string; // the trimmed code, or throws
  // src/features/files/codes.ts
  export function isCodeTaken(taken: readonly string[], code: string): boolean;           // trimmed, case-insensitive
  export function suggestNewScheduleCode(taken: readonly string[]): string;               // first free S01…S99, then S100…
  export function suggestImportCode(taken: readonly string[], source: { sourceProjectCode?: string | null; fileName: string }): string;
  export function suggestCodeFor(taken: readonly string[], schedule: { source: ScheduleSource; sourceFileName: string | null }): string;
  // src/features/schedules/schedule-schema.ts
  export function makeNewScheduleSchema(taken: readonly string[]): ZodObject;    // { name, code, projectId }
  export function makeImportScheduleSchema(taken: readonly string[]): ZodObject; // { file, name, code, projectId }
  export const newScheduleSchema;     // makeNewScheduleSchema([])
  export const importScheduleSchema;  // makeImportScheduleSchema([])
  export function filesPath(workspaceId: ID, projectId: ID): string;  // `/w/${workspaceId}/p/${projectId}/files`
  ```
- `checkNewScheduleCode` (both adapters agree): empty after trimming → `validation` "Enter a schedule code."; not matching the pattern → `validation` "Some values aren't valid. Check the form and try again." (Supabase's plain 23514 copy); a code of the project equal after `scheduleCodeKey` → `conflict` "This code is already used in this project.". The mock runs it after the project and role checks.
- `suggestImportCode`: `sourceProjectCode`, else the file name without its extension (`scheduleNameFromFile`), every character outside `[A-Za-z0-9_.-]` turned into `-`, cut to 24; empty or taken → `suggestNewScheduleCode(taken)`. `suggestCodeFor` uses the import rule with the file name for `source === 'import'`, else the S rule.
- Code field (both dialogs): label "Code" after "Name", `aria-required`, hint "Letters, numbers, ., - or _ · up to 24 characters"; zod copy "Enter a schedule code" · "Use letters, numbers, ., - or _" · "Use 24 characters or fewer" · "This code is already used in this project" (in that order; the pattern uses `*` so a 25-character code reports only the length). A `conflict` from the repository puts its message under "Code" and focuses it; other failures keep the P2 toast. Until Task 20 the dialogs pass `[]` as the taken codes, so the server's R8 check answers.
- New schedule: Code defaults to `suggestNewScheduleCode([])`; after "Schedule created" the app opens `filesPath(...)` of the schedule's project (spec D16). P2 import dialog: `FIELD_ORDER = ['file', 'name', 'code', 'projectId']`; choosing a file fills an empty Code with `suggestImportCode([], { fileName })`; it still lands on the schedule page.
- Seed codes (§4.5): Main schedule `MS-01`; 003 Baseline `BL-01`, Update 1 `UP-01`, Update 2 `UP-02`; 004 Baseline `BL-01`, Recovery plan `RP-01`; 005 Baseline `BL-01`; T-01 Baseline rev. 3 `BL-03`, Look-ahead W42 `LA-42`. `integration/seed-loader.ts` inserts `code`. `MOCK_DB_VERSION` and `MockDb['version']` become `3`.
- Supabase: `createSchedule` and `importSchedule` insert `code: input.code.trim()`; `mapSchedule` reads `code`. `projectFrom`'s parameter also omits `current_schedule_id` and `current_schedule_name` (the recreated view's new columns), and the row literal in `mappers.test.ts` gains `current_schedule_id: null, current_schedule_name: null` (type only). `errors.ts` maps 23505 on `schedules_project_code_uq` → `conflict` "This code is already used in this project.", on `schedules_project_update_uq` → `conflict` "This update number is already used in this project.", on `schedules_one_primary_uq` or `schedules_one_current_uq` → `conflict` "Another change was saved at the same time. Refresh and try again.".

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { isCodeTaken, suggestCodeFor, suggestImportCode, suggestNewScheduleCode } from '@/features/files/codes';

describe('schedule code suggestions', () => {
  it('suggests the first free S code, ignoring case', () => {
    expect(suggestNewScheduleCode([])).toBe('S01');
    expect(suggestNewScheduleCode(['s01', 'S02', 'MS-01'])).toBe('S03');
    expect(suggestNewScheduleCode(Array.from({ length: 99 }, (_, i) => `S${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`))).toBe('S100');
  });

  it('suggests an import code from the project short name or the file name', () => {
    expect(suggestImportCode([], { sourceProjectCode: 'NTH-A', fileName: 'north.xer' })).toBe('NTH-A');
    expect(suggestImportCode([], { fileName: 'Güncelleme 3 (rev).xml' })).toBe('G-ncelleme-3--rev-');
    expect(suggestImportCode([], { fileName: `${'x'.repeat(30)}.csv` })).toBe('x'.repeat(24));
    expect(suggestImportCode(['nth-a'], { sourceProjectCode: 'NTH-A', fileName: 'north.xer' })).toBe('S01');
  });

  it('suggests the import rule for a P2-era import and the S rule otherwise', () => {
    expect(suggestCodeFor(['S01'], { source: 'import', sourceFileName: 'Rev7.XER' })).toBe('Rev7');
    expect(suggestCodeFor(['S01'], { source: 'blank', sourceFileName: null })).toBe('S02');
    expect(isCodeTaken(['MS-01'], ' ms-01 ')).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

### Task 4: Migration `p3_core_imports` and the interim import path

**Spec:** §4.6 (`p3_core_imports` row, "Existing rows"), §4.7 (`prepare_new_schedule` path rule, `backfill_import_formats`, `schedule_items`), §4.8, §4.9, §2.4 (interim import path), §7 Database (`06_storage`, `10_imports`) and Integration, §8 V7, §9 (`integration/storage.itest.ts`).

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/<timestamp>_p3_core_imports.sql`, `supabase/tests/10_imports.test.sql`
- Modify: `supabase/tests/01_schema.test.sql`, `supabase/tests/02_privileges.test.sql`, `supabase/tests/06_storage.test.sql`, `supabase/tests/08_projects.test.sql` (the setup's import row gains `source_format`), `src/data/supabase/database.types.ts` (generated), `src/data/supabase/SupabaseRepository.ts`, `integration/storage.itest.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3's triggers and views.
- Produces (database):
  - `schedules` columns of §4.6 verbatim: `source_format`, `source_file_size`, `source_file_sha256`, `source_project_code`, `parser_version`, `import_mapping`, `import_report`; unique index `schedules_source_file_path_uq (source_file_path) where source_file_path is not null`; after the backfill, `constraint schedules_source_format_presence_check check ((source = 'import') = (source_format is not null))`.
  - `app.backfill_import_formats()`: sets `source_format` of imports without one from the lower-cased extension of `source_file_name` (`xer` → `xer`, `xml` → `mspdi`, `csv` → `csv`); any import still without a format raises `check_violation` with the message `Schedules with an import format the migration can't tell: ` followed by `{id} {source_file_name}` per row, joined with `, `.
  - `app.prepare_new_schedule()` replaced: as Task 3, plus for a user call with a non-null `source_file_path`, the path must equal `{workspace_id}/{project_id}/{uuid}.{suffix}` of the row's project with suffix `xer.gz`, `xml.gz`, `csv.gz` or `xlsx` for `source_format` `xer`, `mspdi`, `csv` or `xlsx`; otherwise `check_violation` (no `hint`) "The stored file path doesn't match the schedule.".
  - `schedule_items` recreated with `source_format`, `has_import_report` (`import_report is not null`) and `has_import_mapping` (`import_mapping is not null`); same grants.
  - `grant insert (source_format, source_file_size, source_file_sha256, source_project_code, parser_version, import_mapping, import_report, activity_count) on public.schedules to authenticated`.
  - `schedule_imports_delete` replaced (§4.9): `bucket_id = 'schedule-imports' and ((storage.foldername(name))[1] in (select id::text from app.my_workspace_ids('owner') as id) or ((storage.foldername(name))[1] in (select id::text from app.my_workspace_ids('editor') as id) and owner_id = (select auth.uid())::text and not exists (select 1 from public.schedules s where s.source_file_path = objects.name)))` — qualify the object's name as `objects.name`: `public.schedules` has its own `name` column.
- Produces (Supabase adapter, the interim path of §2.4): `importSchedule` takes the format from the file's extension (`.xer` → `xer`, `.xml` → `mspdi`, `.csv` → `csv`; any other → `validation` "Use a .xer, .xml or .csv file."), gzips the file with `gzipSync` from `await import('fflate')`, uploads the bytes as `application/gzip` to `{workspace_id}/{project_id}/{uuid}.{xer|xml|csv}.gz`, and inserts `source_format` and `source_file_size: file.size` with P2's other columns. No mapping, report, hash or parser version yet (a P2-era-like row). The mock is unchanged here (Task 7 adds `sourceFormat`).

```sql
  values (tests.project_id('P1'), 'XML', 'I-3', 'import', 'mspdi', 'a.xml', tests.import_path('P1', 'xml.gz')) $$, 'an MSPDI gzipped');
```

`integration/storage.itest.ts` — import `beforeAll` (already), `gunzipSync` from `fflate` and `makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture` from `./supabase-fixture`; add:

```ts
describe('schedule-imports deletes and the interim import (spec §2.4, §4.9)', () => {
  let projectId = '';
  const importPath = () => `${workspaceId}/${projectId}/${crypto.randomUUID()}.xer.gz`;
  const gz = () => new Blob([new Uint8Array([0x1f, 0x8b, 8, 0])]);
  const exists = async (path: string) => {
    const folder = path.slice(0, path.lastIndexOf('/'));
    return (await admin.storage.from('schedule-imports').list(folder)).data!.some((f) => path.endsWith(`/${f.name}`));
  };
  const reference = (client: SupabaseClient, path: string, code: string) =>
    client.from('schedules').insert({
      project_id: projectId, name: code, code, source: 'import', source_format: 'xer',
      source_file_name: `${code}.xer`, source_file_path: path, source_file_size: 4,
    });

  beforeAll(async () => {
    const { data } = await clients.olive.from('projects').insert({ workspace_id: workspaceId, code: 'SI1', name: 'Storage imports' }).select('id').single();
    projectId = (data as { id: string }).id;
  });

  it('lets an editor delete only an unreferenced object they uploaded', async () => {
    const [own, ref, theirs] = [importPath(), importPath(), importPath()];
    await clients.ed.storage.from('schedule-imports').upload(own, gz());
    await clients.ed.storage.from('schedule-imports').upload(ref, gz());
    await clients.olive.storage.from('schedule-imports').upload(theirs, gz());
    expect((await reference(clients.ed, ref, 'REF-1')).error).toBeNull();
    await clients.ed.storage.from('schedule-imports').remove([own, ref, theirs]);
    expect([await exists(own), await exists(ref), await exists(theirs)]).toEqual([false, true, true]);
  });

  it('lets owners delete any object of their workspace', async () => {
    const ref = importPath();
    await clients.ed.storage.from('schedule-imports').upload(ref, gz());
    expect((await reference(clients.ed, ref, 'REF-2')).error).toBeNull();
    await clients.olive.storage.from('schedule-imports').remove([ref]);
    expect(await exists(ref)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('stores an interim import gzipped under its project, with its format', async () => {
    const f = await makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture();
    const schedule = await f.repository.importSchedule({
      projectId: f.projectIds['002']!, name: 'Rev 8', code: 'R8', file: new File(['ERMHDR\t20.12'], 'rev8.XER'),
    });
    const { data } = await admin.from('schedules').select('source_format, source_file_size, source_file_path').eq('id', schedule.id).single();
    const row = data as { source_format: string; source_file_size: number; source_file_path: string };
    expect(row).toMatchObject({ source_format: 'xer', source_file_size: 12 });
    expect(row.source_file_path).toMatch(new RegExp(`^${f.workspaces.personal}/${f.projectIds['002']}/[0-9a-f-]{36}\\.xer\\.gz$`));
    const { data: blob } = await admin.storage.from('schedule-imports').download(row.source_file_path);
    expect(new TextDecoder().decode(gunzipSync(new Uint8Array(await blob!.arrayBuffer())))).toBe('ERMHDR\t20.12');
  });
});
```

### Task 5: Project attributes, `getProject` and `updateProject`

**Spec:** §4.1 (`ProjectStatus` … `ProjectLocation`; `ProjectSummary` waits for Task 7), §4.2 (`CoverChange`, `UpdateProjectInput`, `getProject`, `updateProject`; hidden rows), §4.3 R10, R11, §4.4 (`useProject`, `useUpdateProject`), §4.5 (seed project columns of the table: status, stage, type, sector, client, city, coordinates; country "Türkiye"), §4.10 ("Writes RLS only filters", `getProject`, `updateProject`, mappers), §7 Repository contract and Integration (cover replacement), D34.

**Files:**
- Create: `integration/projects.itest.ts`
- Modify: `src/data/types.ts`, `src/data/repository.ts`, `src/data/mock/seed.ts`, `src/data/mock/MockRepository.ts`, `src/data/supabase/SupabaseRepository.ts`, `src/data/supabase/mappers.ts`, `integration/seed-loader.ts`, `integration/supabase.contract.itest.ts`, `src/test/fakes.ts`, `src/test/contract/repository.contract.ts`, `src/hooks/query-keys.ts`, `src/hooks/useProjects.ts`, `src/features/projects/project-schema.ts` (`toNewProjectInput` only)
- Test: `src/data/supabase/mappers.test.ts`, `src/hooks/data-hooks.test.tsx`, `src/features/projects/project-schema.test.ts` (expected object), any test that builds a `Project` by hand (the new fields), the contract suite

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`src/data/types.ts`, §4.1 verbatim): `ProjectStatus`, `ProjectStage`, `ProjectType` (eight values), `ProjectSector`; `ProjectAttributes { status; stage; projectType; sector; startDate; plannedFinishDate }`; `ProjectLocation { address; city; country; latitude; longitude }`.
- Produces (`src/data/repository.ts`, §4.2 verbatim): `CoverChange`, `UpdateProjectInput`, and on `DataRepository`:
  ```ts
  getProject(projectId: ID): Promise<ProjectSummary>;          // not_found "Project not found"
  updateProject(input: UpdateProjectInput): Promise<Project>;  // conflict "This ID is already used in this workspace"; viewers forbidden
  ```
- Produces (`src/test/fakes.ts`): `makeNewProjectInput` defaults `attributes: { status: 'active', stage: null, projectType: null, sector: null, startDate: null, plannedFinishDate: null }` and `location: { address: null, city: null, country: null, latitude: null, longitude: null }`; `makeUpdateProjectInput(project: Project, overrides?: Partial<UpdateProjectInput>): UpdateProjectInput` (the project's own values, `cover: 'keep'`, `statusDate: project.statusDate`).
- Produces (hooks): `queryKeys.project(workspaceId: ID | undefined, projectId: ID | undefined)` → `['projects', workspaceId, 'detail', projectId]`; `useProject(workspaceId: ID | undefined, projectId: ID | undefined): UseQueryResult<ProjectSummary>` (idle while either is undefined); `useUpdateProject(): UseMutationResult<Project, Error, { workspaceId: ID; input: UpdateProjectInput }>` — a `File` cover goes through `prepareCoverImage` first; on settle invalidates `projects(workspaceId)`, `sharedWithMe()`, `workspaces()`.
- Produces (mappers): `projectFrom` reads `status`, `stage`, `sector`, `location_lat`, `location_lng` (`Number()`; null stays null); `toProjectInsert` writes them; `toProjectUpdate(input: UpdateProjectInput, coverPath: string | null | undefined): ProjectUpdate` trims like `toProjectInsert`, has no `status_date` key when `input.statusDate` is `undefined` and no `cover_image_path` key when `coverPath` is `undefined`.
- Mock rules: `checkProjectValues(attributes, location)` shared by `createProject` and `updateProject`, after the role check — a status, stage, type or sector outside its list, coordinates that aren't both set or both null, or out of range → `validation` "Some values aren't valid. Check the form and try again."; coordinates are stored rounded to 6 decimals (as `numeric(8,6)` / `numeric(9,6)` do). `getProject`: unknown → `not_found` "Project not found" (the mock doesn't filter by membership, D34). `updateProject` order: unknown → `not_found`; not an editor of the project's workspace → `forbidden`; value checks; another project of the workspace with the same trimmed, case-insensitive code → `conflict`; then the write (cover: `File` → data URL, `'remove'` → `null`, `'keep'` → unchanged; `statusDate` only when present; `updatedAt` now). R6 joins in Task 7.
- Supabase: `getProject` reads `project_summaries` by id with `.single()` (PGRST116 → `not_found` "Project not found") and signs the cover. `updateProject` reads `id, workspace_id, cover_image_path` from `projects` (`maybeSingle`; none → `not_found`); a `File` cover uploads to `{workspace_id}/{uuid}.jpg|.png` as `createProject` does; `update(toProjectUpdate(…)).eq('id', …).select('*').maybeSingle()` — an error removes the new upload; no row → `forbidden` (the row was visible); after success a changed cover removes the old object (best effort). `integration/seed-loader.ts` inserts the new columns.
- Seed (§4.5): 001 active · construction · building · private · Riverside Holding · Istanbul · 41.0151, 28.9795; 002 active · preconstruction · transportation · public · City Transit Office · Ankara · 39.9334, 32.8597; 003 active · construction · industrial · private · Aegean Ports · Izmir · 38.4192, 27.1287; 004 on_hold · construction · building · public · Northfield University · Istanbul · none; 005 completed · completed · infrastructure · public · Regional Roads Office · Bursa · 40.1828, 29.0663; 006 active · pursuit · residential · private · Millstone Homes · no city · none; T-01 active · construction · infrastructure · public · Tunnel Operations Agency · Istanbul · 41.0422, 29.0083; every country "Türkiye".
- `toNewProjectInput` sends the defaults (`status: 'active'`, `stage`, `sector`, `latitude`, `longitude` null) until Task 16 adds the fields.
- Not in this task: R6's refusal in `updateProject` and `ProjectSummary.currentSchedule` (Task 7). Its cases must not change the status date of a project that has schedules.

```ts
    });
```

```ts
    }

    const f = await makeFixture();
    const before = await f.repository.getProject(f.projectIds['006']!);
    const updated = await f.repository.updateProject(
      makeUpdateProjectInput(before, {
        code: '006B', name: '  Old Mill Lofts ', description: 'Phase two',
        attributes: { status: 'completed', stage: 'closeout', projectType: 'water', sector: 'public', startDate: '2026-01-05', plannedFinishDate: '2026-12-20' },
        client: { name: 'Millstone Homes', contactName: 'Ayşe Demir', contactEmail: 'ayse@example.com' },
        location: { address: 'Mill Road 4', city: 'Eskişehir', country: 'Türkiye', latitude: 39.7767, longitude: 30.5206 },
      }),
    );
    expect(updated).toMatchObject({
      code: '006B', name: 'Old Mill Lofts', description: 'Phase two',
      attributes: { status: 'completed', stage: 'closeout', projectType: 'water', sector: 'public' },
      client: { contactName: 'Ayşe Demir' },
      location: { city: 'Eskişehir', latitude: 39.7767, longitude: 30.5206 },
    });
    expect(Date.parse(updated.updatedAt)).toBeGreaterThan(Date.parse(before.updatedAt));
    expect((await f.repository.listRecentProjects(f.workspaces.personal, 1))[0]!.code).toBe('006B');
    expect(await f.repository.getProject(before.id)).toMatchObject({ name: 'Old Mill Lofts', location: { address: 'Mill Road 4' } });
  });

  it('keeps the status date when the input leaves it out and refuses a taken code', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const project = await f.repository.getProject(f.projectIds['006']!);
    const input = makeUpdateProjectInput(project, { name: 'Old Mill' });
    delete input.statusDate;
    expect(await f.repository.updateProject(input)).toMatchObject({ name: 'Old Mill', statusDate: project.statusDate });
    expect(await f.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(project, { statusDate: '2026-01-15' }))).toMatchObject({ statusDate: '2026-01-15' });
    await expect(f.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(project, { code: ' 001 ' }))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'conflict', message: 'This ID is already used in this workspace',
    });
  });

  it('replaces, keeps and removes the cover', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const project = await f.repository.getProject(f.projectIds['002']!);
    const jpeg = new File([new Uint8Array([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff])], 'cover.jpg', { type: 'image/jpeg' });
    const withCover = await f.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(project, { cover: jpeg }));
    expect(withCover.coverImageUrl).toEqual(expect.any(String));
    expect((await f.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(withCover, { name: 'Metro Line 2' }))).coverImageUrl).toEqual(expect.any(String));
    expect((await f.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(withCover, { cover: 'remove' }))).coverImageUrl).toBeNull();
  });

  it('refuses updates from viewers and of unknown projects', async () => {
    const viewer = await makeFixture({ teamRole: 'viewer' });
    const tunnel = await viewer.repository.getProject(viewer.projectIds['T-01']!);
    await expect(viewer.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(tunnel, { name: 'Nope' }))).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'forbidden' });
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const riverside = await f.repository.getProject(f.projectIds['001']!);
    for (const id of unknownIds()) {
      await expect(f.repository.updateProject({ ...makeUpdateProjectInput(riverside), projectId: id })).rejects.toMatchObject({
        code: 'not_found', message: 'Project not found',
      });
    }
  });
});
```

```ts
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Database } from '@/data/supabase/database.types';
import { makeNewProjectInput, makeUpdateProjectInput } from '@/test/fakes';
import { readLocalStack } from './local-stack';
import { makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture } from './supabase-fixture';

const stack = readLocalStack();
const admin = createClient<Database>(stack.apiUrl, stack.secretKey, { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false } });
const jpeg = (name: string) => new File([new Uint8Array([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 0xe0])], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' });

async function coverPath(projectId: string): Promise<string> {
  const { data, error } = await admin.from('projects').select('cover_image_path').eq('id', projectId).single();
  if (error || !data.cover_image_path) throw new Error(`No cover for ${projectId}`);
  return data.cover_image_path;
}
const stored = async (bucket: string, path: string) => (await admin.storage.from(bucket).download(path)).error === null;

describe('projects on the local stack', () => {
  it('deletes the old cover object when a project gets a new one', async () => {
    const f = await makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture();
    const created = await f.repository.createProject(makeNewProjectInput(f.workspaces.personal, { code: 'CV1', coverImage: jpeg('a.jpg') }));
    const first = await coverPath(created.id);
    await f.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(created, { cover: jpeg('b.jpg') }));
    const second = await coverPath(created.id);
    expect(second).not.toBe(first);
    expect(await stored('project-covers', second)).toBe(true);
    expect(await stored('project-covers', first)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
it('removes the stored originals and the cover of a deleted project, P2-era imports included', async () => {
  const f = await makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture();
  const project = await f.repository.createProject(makeNewProjectInput(f.workspaces.personal, { code: 'DL1', coverImage: jpeg('c.jpg') }));
  const imported = await f.repository.importSchedule({ projectId: project.id, name: 'Rev 1', code: 'R1', file: new File(['ERMHDR'], 'rev1.xer') });
  const oldPath = `${f.workspaces.personal}/${project.id}/${crypto.randomUUID()}.xer`;
  await admin.storage.from('schedule-imports').upload(oldPath, new Blob(['ERMHDR']));
  await admin.from('schedules').insert({
    project_id: project.id, name: 'P2 era', source: 'import', source_format: 'xer', source_file_name: 'old.xer', source_file_path: oldPath,
  });
  const { data } = await admin.from('schedules').select('source_file_path').eq('id', imported.id).single();
  const paths = [data!.source_file_path!, oldPath];
  const cover = await coverPath(project.id);
  expect(await Promise.all(paths.map((p) => stored('schedule-imports', p)))).toEqual([true, true]);
  await f.repository.deleteProject(project.id);
  expect(await Promise.all(paths.map((p) => stored('schedule-imports', p)))).toEqual([false, false]);
  expect(await stored('project-covers', cover)).toBe(false);
});
```

- Produces (mappers): `mapScheduleItem(row: ScheduleItemRow): Schedule` replaces `mapSchedule` (`need()` for the non-null columns; `baseline` → `BaselineFlag`; `source_format` → `ImportFormat | null`); `mapProjectSummary` adds `totalScheduleCount` and `currentSchedule` (both `current_schedule_*` set → object, else `null`).
- Mock details (order): unknown schedule → `validation` "Some schedules are no longer available. Refresh and try again."; not an editor → `forbidden`; name trimmed 1–80, notes trimmed (empty → `null`) ≤ 2000, baseline in the list, update number an integer 1–999 or `null`, code matching the pattern — else `validation` "Some values aren't valid. Check the form and try again."; empty code → R8 "Enter a schedule code."; a code or update number another schedule of the project holds → R8 / R7 `conflict`; then R5 (a new primary demotes the old one, which gets `updatedAt` now; `'baseline'` from `'none'` without a primary becomes `'primary'`), R11 (`updatedAt` now), R6 when the schedule is current. `setCurrentSchedule`: unknown → R9 copy; not an editor → `forbidden`; R4; R6. `createSchedule` / `importSchedule`: `isCurrent` when the project has no schedule (R1), R6 sync, `baseline: 'none'`, `activityCount: 0`, `hasImport…: false`, `sourceFormat` `null` for blank and, for an import, from the extension (`.xer` → `xer`, `.xml` → `mspdi`, `.csv` → `csv`, `.xlsx` → `xlsx`). `updateProject` adds R6: a present `statusDate` that differs from the current schedule's → `validation` "The status date follows the current schedule.".
- Supabase: `listSchedules` reads `schedule_items` `.eq('project_id', …).order('status_date', { ascending: false, nullsFirst: false }).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).order('id')`; `setCurrentSchedule` and `updateScheduleDetails` call their RPCs; `createSchedule`, `importSchedule` and `updateScheduleDetails` read the row from `schedule_items` afterwards.
- Seed (§4.5): per project, schedules `{ name, code, statusDaysBefore, isCurrent, baseline, updateNumber, notes, archivedDaysAgo }` — 001 Main schedule (MS-01, current, 2); 003 Baseline (BL-01, primary, 60), Update 1 (UP-01, update 1, 30), Update 2 (UP-02, update 2, current, 7, "Includes the revised crane plan."); 004 Baseline (BL-01, primary, 120), Recovery plan (RP-01, current, 14, "Agreed with the client on site."), Recovery plan draft (RP-00, archived 10 days before seed, 30); 005 Baseline (BL-01, current, primary, no status date); T-01 Baseline rev. 3 (BL-03, primary, 90), Look-ahead W42 (LA-42, current, 3). `integration/seed-loader.ts` inserts these columns and each project's current schedule first, so `prepare_new_schedule` makes it current.

```ts
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('schedules of a project', () => {
  const details = (s: Schedule) => ({
    scheduleId: s.id, name: s.name, code: s.code ?? '', statusDate: s.statusDate, notes: s.notes, baseline: s.baseline, updateNumber: s.updateNumber,
  });

  it('lists schedules by status date, newest first and nulls last, then by creation', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    expect((await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['004']!)).map((s) => [s.code, s.isCurrent, s.baseline, s.archivedAt !== null])).toEqual([
      ['RP-01', true, 'none', false],
      ['RP-00', false, 'none', true],
      ['BL-01', false, 'primary', false],
    ]);
    const harbor = f.projectIds['003']!;
    await f.repository.createSchedule({ projectId: harbor, name: 'Update 3', code: 'UP-03' });
    expect((await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor)).map((s) => s.code)).toEqual(['UP-03', 'UP-02', 'UP-01', 'BL-01']);
    const bridge = f.projectIds['005']!;
    const dated = await f.repository.createSchedule({ projectId: bridge, name: 'Dated', code: 'D-1' });
    await f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(dated), statusDate: '2026-01-01' });
    expect((await f.repository.listSchedules(bridge)).map((s) => s.code)).toEqual(['D-1', 'BL-01']);
    for (const id of unknownIds()) expect(await f.repository.listSchedules(id)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('makes the first schedule of a project current and counts archived schedules apart', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const mill = f.projectIds['006']!;
    const first = await f.repository.createSchedule({ projectId: mill, name: 'First', code: 'S01' });
    const imported = await f.repository.importSchedule({ projectId: mill, name: 'Rev', code: 'R1', file: new File(['x'], 'rev.XML') });
    expect(first).toMatchObject({
      isCurrent: true, baseline: 'none', updateNumber: null, notes: null, archivedAt: null, activityCount: 0,
      sourceFormat: null, hasImportReport: false, hasImportMapping: false,
    });
    expect(imported).toMatchObject({ isCurrent: false, sourceFormat: 'mspdi', hasImportReport: false });
    expect(
      (await f.repository.listProjects(f.workspaces.personal)).map((p) => [p.code, p.scheduleCount, p.totalScheduleCount, p.currentSchedule?.name ?? null]),
    ).toEqual([
      ['006', 2, 2, 'First'],
      ['001', 1, 1, 'Main schedule'],
      ['002', 0, 0, null],
      ['003', 3, 3, 'Update 2'],
      ['004', 2, 3, 'Recovery plan'],
      ['005', 1, 1, 'Baseline'],
    ]);
  });

  it('makes another schedule current; the project follows its status date and keeps its updatedAt', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const harbor = f.projectIds['003']!;
    const before = await f.repository.getProject(harbor);
    const update1 = (await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor)).find((s) => s.code === 'UP-01')!;
    await f.repository.setCurrentSchedule(update1.id);
    const after = await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor);
    expect(after.filter((s) => s.isCurrent).map((s) => s.code)).toEqual(['UP-01']);
    expect(after.find((s) => s.code === 'UP-01')!.updatedAt).toBe(update1.updatedAt);
    expect(await f.repository.getProject(harbor)).toMatchObject({
      statusDate: update1.statusDate, currentSchedule: { id: update1.id, name: 'Update 1' }, updatedAt: before.updatedAt,
    });
  });

  it('refuses to make an archived or unknown schedule current', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const draft = (await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['004']!)).find((s) => s.code === 'RP-00')!;
    await expect(f.repository.setCurrentSchedule(draft.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'validation', message: 'Restore this schedule before making it current.',
    });
    for (const id of unknownIds()) {
      await expect(f.repository.setCurrentSchedule(id)).rejects.toMatchObject({
        code: 'validation', message: 'Some schedules are no longer available. Refresh and try again.',
      });
    }
  });

  it('saves details, turns the first baseline into the primary one and demotes an old primary', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const [main] = await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['001']!);
    const saved = await f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({
      ...details(main!), name: ' Main ', code: ' MS-02 ', notes: '  Weekly look-ahead  ', baseline: 'baseline', updateNumber: 4,
    });
    expect(saved).toMatchObject({ name: 'Main', code: 'MS-02', notes: 'Weekly look-ahead', baseline: 'primary', updateNumber: 4 });
    expect(Date.parse(saved.updatedAt)).toBeGreaterThan(Date.parse(main!.updatedAt));
    const harbor = f.projectIds['003']!;
    const before = await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor);
    await f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(before.find((s) => s.code === 'UP-01')!), baseline: 'primary' });
    const after = await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor);
    expect(after.map((s) => [s.code, s.baseline])).toEqual([['UP-02', 'none'], ['UP-01', 'primary'], ['BL-01', 'baseline']]);
    const updatedAt = (list: Schedule[]) => Date.parse(list.find((s) => s.code === 'BL-01')!.updatedAt);
    expect(updatedAt(after)).toBeGreaterThan(updatedAt(before));
  });

  it('refuses a taken code or update number and an empty code', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const update2 = (await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['003']!)).find((s) => s.code === 'UP-02')!;
    await expect(f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(update2), code: 'up-01' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'conflict', message: 'This code is already used in this project.',
    });
    await expect(f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(update2), updateNumber: 1 })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'conflict', message: 'This update number is already used in this project.',
    });
    await expect(f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(update2), code: '  ' })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'validation', message: 'Enter a schedule code.',
    });
    await expect(f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(update2), updateNumber: 1000 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'validation' });
  });

  it("keeps the project's status date with its current schedule's (R6, R11)", async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const harbor = await f.repository.getProject(f.projectIds['003']!);
    await expect(f.repository.updateProject(makeUpdateProjectInput(harbor, { statusDate: '2026-01-01' }))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'validation', message: 'The status date follows the current schedule.',
    });
    const update2 = (await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor.id)).find((s) => s.isCurrent)!;
    await f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(update2), statusDate: '2026-02-02' });
    expect(await f.repository.getProject(harbor.id)).toMatchObject({ statusDate: '2026-02-02', updatedAt: harbor.updatedAt });
    const input = makeUpdateProjectInput(harbor, { name: 'Harbor Hub' });
    delete input.statusDate;
    expect(await f.repository.updateProject(input)).toMatchObject({ name: 'Harbor Hub', statusDate: '2026-02-02' });
  });

  it('keeps viewers from changing schedules', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture({ teamRole: 'viewer' });
    const [lookAhead, baseline] = await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['T-01']!);
    await expect(f.repository.setCurrentSchedule(baseline!.id)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'forbidden' });
    await expect(f.repository.updateScheduleDetails({ ...details(lookAhead!), name: 'X' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'forbidden' });
  });
});
```

### Task 8: Archive and delete schedules

**Spec:** §4.2 (`setSchedulesArchived`, `deleteSchedules`), §4.3 R2, R3, R9–R11, §4.4 (schedule mutations), §4.10 (`setSchedulesArchived`, `deleteSchedules`), §7 Repository contract and Integration (`deleteSchedules` removes the objects, P2-era imports included).

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/data/repository.ts`, `src/data/mock/schedule-rules.ts`, `src/data/mock/MockRepository.ts`, `src/data/supabase/SupabaseRepository.ts`, `src/hooks/useSchedules.ts`, `src/test/contract/repository.contract.ts`, `integration/projects.itest.ts`
- Test: `src/hooks/data-hooks.test.tsx`, the contract suite, `integration/projects.itest.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7's types, `compareSchedules`, `currentScheduleOf`.
- Produces (`DataRepository`, §4.2): `setSchedulesArchived(input: { scheduleIds: ID[]; archived: boolean }): Promise<void>`, `deleteSchedules(scheduleIds: ID[]): Promise<void>`.
- Produces (`schedule-rules.ts`): `resolveScheduleSet(schedules: readonly Schedule[], scheduleIds: readonly ID[]): { projectId: ID; found: Schedule[] } | null` — `null` for an empty list; distinct ids; R9 in order: more than 500 → "Select 500 schedules or fewer."; found ones in more than one project → "Select schedules from one project."; fewer found than ids → "Some schedules are no longer available. Refresh and try again."; `checkDeleteKeepsCurrent(schedules, projectId, deletedIds)` — R3 on the state after the whole delete.
- Mock order (both methods): `resolveScheduleSet` → role (`editor` to archive, `owner` to delete) → `forbidden` → the write in one `commit`: archive applies R2 to every id first ("Make another schedule current before archiving this one."), keeps an earlier `archivedAt` (`archivedAt ?? now`), restore sets `null`; every named schedule gets `updatedAt` now (R11). Delete applies R3, then removes the schedules and their shares.
- Supabase: `rpc('set_schedules_archived', …)`; `rpc('delete_schedules', …)`, then `storage.from('schedule-imports').remove(paths)` when any, errors ignored. Ids that aren't UUIDs go through `asUuid`.
- Hooks (`useSchedules.ts`): `useSetSchedulesArchived(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, { scheduleIds: ID[]; archived: boolean }>`, `useDeleteSchedules(): UseMutationResult<void, Error, ID[]>`, invalidating as Task 7's mutations.

```ts
  it('archives and restores schedules, never the current one', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const harbor = f.projectIds['003']!;

    const [update2, update1] = await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['003']!);
    const [recovery] = await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['004']!);
    const tooMany = Array.from({ length: 501 }, () => crypto.randomUUID());
    await expect(f.repository.setSchedulesArchived({ scheduleIds: tooMany, archived: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'validation', message: 'Select 500 schedules or fewer.',
    });
    await expect(f.repository.setSchedulesArchived({ scheduleIds: [update1!.id, recovery!.id], archived: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'validation', message: 'Select schedules from one project.',
    });
    await expect(f.repository.deleteSchedules([update1!.id, crypto.randomUUID()])).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'validation', message: 'Some schedules are no longer available. Refresh and try again.',
    });
    await expect(f.repository.setSchedulesArchived({ scheduleIds: [update1!.id, update2!.id], archived: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'validation', message: 'Make another schedule current before archiving this one.',
    });
    expect((await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['003']!)).every((s) => s.archivedAt === null)).toBe(true);
    await expect(f.repository.setSchedulesArchived({ scheduleIds: [], archived: true })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('deletes schedules as sets, never the current one while others stay', async () => {
    const f = await makeFixture();
    const harbor = f.projectIds['003']!;
    const [update2, update1, baseline] = await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor);
    const keepCurrent = { code: 'validation', message: 'Make another schedule current before deleting this one.' };
    await expect(f.repository.deleteSchedules([update2!.id])).rejects.toMatchObject(keepCurrent);
    await expect(f.repository.deleteSchedules([update2!.id, update1!.id])).rejects.toMatchObject(keepCurrent);
    await f.repository.deleteSchedules([baseline!.id]);
    expect((await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor)).map((s) => s.code)).toEqual(['UP-02', 'UP-01']);
    await f.repository.deleteSchedules([update1!.id, update2!.id]);
    expect(await f.repository.listSchedules(harbor)).toEqual([]);
    expect(await f.repository.getProject(harbor)).toMatchObject({ scheduleCount: 0, totalScheduleCount: 0, currentSchedule: null });
    const [, rev3] = await f.repository.listSchedules(f.projectIds['T-01']!);
    await f.repository.deleteSchedules([rev3!.id]);
    expect((await f.repository.listSharedWithMe()).map((r) => r.scheduleName)).toEqual(['Look-ahead W42']);
  });

  it('lets editors archive and only owners delete', async () => {
    const editor = await makeFixture({ teamRole: 'editor' });
    const [, rev3] = await editor.repository.listSchedules(editor.projectIds['T-01']!);
    await expect(editor.repository.deleteSchedules([rev3!.id])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'forbidden' });
    await expect(editor.repository.setSchedulesArchived({ scheduleIds: [rev3!.id], archived: true })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    const viewer = await makeFixture({ teamRole: 'viewer' });
    const [, viewerRev3] = await viewer.repository.listSchedules(viewer.projectIds['T-01']!);
    await expect(viewer.repository.setSchedulesArchived({ scheduleIds: [viewerRev3!.id], archived: true })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'forbidden' });
    await expect(viewer.repository.deleteSchedules([viewerRev3!.id])).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'forbidden' });
  });
});
```
