### Task 9: Parser foundations

**Spec:** §3 (layer rules; `src/import` file list), §4.1 (the parser types block), §5.2 (detection table, `FORMAT_FROM_CONTENT`), §5.4 (encoding, numbers, durations, dates, matching), §5.8 (the `ImportModel` block), §5.9 (Outcomes), §5.11 (compound files, `<!DOCTYPE`, `safe-record.ts`), §5.12 (issue catalog), §7 Parser row (detection, encoding, numbers, durations, dates, `foldForMatch`). P3 full §5.2, §5.4.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/search.ts`, `src/lib/search.test.ts`, `src/import/version.ts`, `src/import/model.ts`, `src/import/read-result.ts`, `src/import/issues.ts`, `src/import/issues.test.ts`, `src/import/outcomes.ts`, `src/import/safe-record.ts`, `src/import/detect.ts`, `src/import/detect.test.ts`, `src/import/encoding.ts`, `src/import/encoding.test.ts`, `src/import/numbers.ts`, `src/import/numbers.test.ts`, `src/import/durations.ts`, `src/import/durations.test.ts`, `src/import/dates.ts`, `src/import/dates.test.ts`, `src/import/testing.ts`
- Modify: `src/data/types.ts` (the parser types block)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`src/data/types.ts`): the second code block of spec §4.1 verbatim, `TextEncodingName` … `ImportReport`, at the end of the file.
- Produces (`src/import/model.ts`): the §5.8 block verbatim (`WallClock`, `Period`, `Window`, `ImportConstraintType`, `ImportModel`), plus `ImportActivity`, `ImportCalendar`, `ImportWbs`, `ImportRelationship` (element types of its arrays) and `emptyModel(format: ImportFormat): ImportModel`.
- Produces (`src/import/read-result.ts`), the shape every reader returns:
  ```ts
  export interface ReadResult {
    model: ImportModel;
    issues: ImportIssue[];                                  // the reader's issues in file order
    counts: ImportCount[];                                  // calendar, wbs, activity, relationship — always all four, in that order
    formatVersion: string | null;
    sourceProject: ImportReport['sourceProject'];
    units: ImportReport['units'];
    externalLinks: ImportReport['externalLinks'];
    notImported: ImportReport['notImported'];
    elements: Array<{ element: string; items: number }>;   // present source elements, named as in OUTCOME_CATALOG
    statusDate: { date: ISODate | null; time: string | null; source: 'file' | 'earliest-start' | 'none' };
  }
  ```
- Produces (`src/import/issues.ts`):
  ```ts
  export type IssueCode = /* the 64 codes of §5.12 */;
  export const ISSUE_CATALOG: Readonly<Record<IssueCode, { severity: ImportIssueSeverity; group: string; message: string }>>; // §5.12 verbatim, {placeholders} kept
  export function cutText(text: string, max?: number): string;   // longer than max (200) → first max-1 characters + '…'
  export function makeIssue(code: IssueCode, params?: Record<string, string | number>,
    at?: { location?: string | null; original?: string | null; used?: string | null }): ImportIssue;
  ```
  Numbers in `params` are written with `toLocaleString('en-US')`; dates in messages are `YYYY-MM-DD` and times `HH:mm`, joined by a space; every text field goes through `cutText`.
- Produces (`src/import/outcomes.ts`): `OUTCOME_CATALOG: Record<ImportFormat, ReadonlyArray<{ element: string; outcome: ImportOutcome }>>` (our wording, §10), and `OUTCOME_LABELS: Record<ImportOutcome, string>` = "Used for dates", "Kept for display and export", "Not read; still in the original file". Catalog — xer: used `Activities`, `Relationships`, `Calendars`, `Durations`, `Actual dates`, `Constraints`; kept `WBS`, `Early and late dates`, `Total and free float`, `Scheduling settings`; not read `Expected finish dates`, `Resources`, `Resource assignments`, `Activity codes`, `User-defined fields`, `Notebook topics`, `Other tables`. mspdi: used `Tasks`, `Links`, `Calendars`, `Durations`, `Actual dates`, `Constraints`, `Manual starts`; kept `Summary tasks`, `Early and late dates`, `Total and free slack`, `Task notes`, `Scheduling settings`; not read `Deadlines`, `Resources`, `Assignments`, `Baselines`, `Costs`, `Custom fields`, `Outline codes`, `Hyperlinks`, `Recurring task definitions`, `Leveling and priority`, `Timephased data`, `Split segments`, `Earned value`. xlsx and csv: used `Activity code`, `Activity name`, `Duration`, `Start`, `Finish`, `Actual start`, `Actual finish`, `Activity type`, `Calendar`, `Constraint`, `Constraint date`, `% complete`, `Predecessors`; kept `Task ID`, `WBS`, `Notes`; not read `Unmapped columns`.
- Produces (`src/import/safe-record.ts`): `createRecord<T>(): Record<string, T>` (`Object.create(null)`).
- Produces (`src/import/version.ts`): `PARSER_VERSION = '1.0.0'`.
- Produces (`src/import/detect.ts`):
  ```ts
  export type Detection =
    | { kind: 'format'; format: ImportFormat; fromContent: boolean; bomLength: number }
    | { kind: 'refused'; message: string };
  export function extensionOf(fileName: string): string;     // lower-cased, without the dot; '' when none
  export function formatOfExtension(extension: string): ImportFormat | null; // xer · xml → mspdi · xlsx · csv
  export function detectFormat(bytes: Uint8Array, fileName: string): Detection;
  ```
  The §5.2 table in its order, on the bytes after any byte-order mark. XML: the first element after `<?…?>` declarations and comments, read from the first 64 KB; a `<!DOCTYPE` before it refuses as "any other XML". A zip whose local or central directory names include `xl/workbook.xml` is xlsx. Compound files: entry names come from the directory sector chain (header sector shift at offset 30, first directory sector at 48, FAT sectors from the header's DIFAT at 76; at most 1,000 sectors); `EncryptionInfo` → protected; a name matching `/^ {3}\d{3}$/` or a file named `.mpp` → MPP; any other → `.xls`. CSV: a `.csv` file whose first 4,096 bytes after the BOM hold no `0x00` (a UTF-16 BOM allows them). An empty file (0 bytes or only a BOM) is the format of its extension (`fromContent: false`); `.mpp` and `.xls` keep their refusals. `fromContent` is true when the extension names another format or none.
- Produces (`src/import/encoding.ts`):
  ```ts
  export interface DetectedEncoding { name: TextEncodingName; detectedBy: 'bom' | 'utf8' | 'byte-score'; bomLength: number; invalidBytes: number }
  export const ENCODING_LABELS: Record<TextEncodingName, string>; // 'UTF-8', 'UTF-16 LE', 'UTF-16 BE', 'Turkish (Windows-1254)', 'Western European (Windows-1252)'
  export function detectEncoding(bytes: Uint8Array): DetectedEncoding;     // §5.4 rule; 1252 wins only with more 0x8E/0x9E than 0xD0 0xDD 0xDE 0xF0 0xFD 0xFE
  export function decodeText(bytes: Uint8Array, encoding: TextEncodingName, bomLength?: number): string; // non-fatal; invalid bytes → U+FFFD
  export function encodingIssues(detected: DetectedEncoding): ImportIssue[]; // ENCODING_GUESSED ({encoding} = its label) or ENCODING_INVALID_BYTES ({n})
  ```
- Produces (`src/import/numbers.ts`): `parseNumber(text: string, decimal: ',' | '.'): number | null` (`null` for an empty cell, `NaN` for an unreadable one; `%` dropped; the other separator removed as grouping); `guessDecimalSeparator(samples: readonly string[]): ',' | '.'` (each sample's last `,` or `.` votes; `,` only with more votes than `.`).
- Produces (`src/import/durations.ts`): `type DurationResult = { kind: 'empty' } | { kind: 'invalid' } | { kind: 'ok'; minutes: number; elapsed: boolean; estimated: boolean }`; `parseDuration(text: string, options: { unit: 'days' | 'hours'; hoursPerDay: number; decimal: ',' | '.' }): DurationResult`. Working units: day = `hoursPerDay` h, week = 5 days, month = 20 days. Elapsed units (`e` before the unit): day 1,440 min, week 7 × 1,440, month 30 × 1,440, hour 60, minute 1.
- Produces (`src/import/dates.ts`):
  ```ts
  export function parseXerDate(text: string): WallClock | null | 'invalid';      // yyyy-M-d[ H[:mm[:ss]]]
  export function parseMspdiDate(text: string): WallClock | null | 'invalid';    // yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss
  export interface TableDate { date: ISODate; time: string | null }              // time 'HH:mm'
  export function parseTableDate(text: string, format: ImportDateFormat): TableDate | null | 'invalid';
  export function excelSerialToWallClock(serial: number, date1904: boolean): WallClock;
  export function guessDateFormat(samples: readonly string[]): ImportDateFormat; // the first of YYYY-MM-DD, DD.MM.YYYY, MM/DD/YYYY, DD/MM/YYYY that reads every non-empty sample; else DD.MM.YYYY
  ```
  Regex and arithmetic only; weekday names are skipped, not checked; month names (English and Turkish, full or three letters) compare with `foldForMatch`.
- Produces (`src/lib/search.ts`): `foldForMatch(text: string): string`, `matchesSearch(text: string | null, query: string): boolean` (a blank query matches everything, `null` text nothing else).
- Produces (`src/import/testing.ts`, test-only): `bytesOf(text: string): Uint8Array` (UTF-8), `concatBytes(...parts: Array<Uint8Array | number[]>): Uint8Array`, `zip(entries: Record<string, string | Uint8Array>): Uint8Array` (`fflate` `zipSync`), `compoundFile(names: string[]): Uint8Array` — 1,536 bytes: a 512-byte header (signature `D0 CF 11 E0 A1 B1 1A E1`, minor version `0x3E` at 24, major version 3 at 26, `0xFFFE` at 28, sector shift 9 at 30, mini sector shift 6 at 32, one FAT sector at 44, first directory sector 1 at 48, mini stream cutoff 4096 at 56, `ENDOFCHAIN` at 60 and 68, DIFAT[0] = 0 at 76, the other DIFAT entries `0xFFFFFFFF`); sector 0 the FAT (`0xFFFFFFFD`, `0xFFFFFFFE`, then free); sector 1 the directory: "Root Entry" (type 5) and one stream entry (type 2) per name, each 128 bytes with the UTF-16LE name, its byte length with the terminator at 64 and the type at 66.

`src/import/detect.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { detectFormat } from '@/import/detect';
import { bytesOf, compoundFile, concatBytes, zip } from '@/import/testing';

const UNSUPPORTED = "This file type can't be imported. Use a P6 XER, a Microsoft Project XML, an Excel .xlsx or a CSV file.";
const NOT_MSPDI = "This XML file isn't a Microsoft Project XML file.";
const MPP = "MPP files can't be imported. In Microsoft Project, save the file as XML and import that.";

describe('detectFormat', () => {
  it.each([
    ['ERMHDR\t20.12\t2026-11-01', 'plan.xer', 'xer', false],
    ['ERMHDR\t20.12', 'plan.csv', 'xer', true],
    ['<?xml version="1.0"?>\n<!-- x --><Project xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/project"><Name>A</Name></Project>', 'plan.xml', 'mspdi', false],
    ['<Project xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/project"/>', 'plan.xer', 'mspdi', true],
    ['Kod;Ad\nA1;Kazı', 'list.csv', 'csv', false],
  ])('reads %j named %s as %s', (content, name, format, fromContent) => {
    expect(detectFormat(bytesOf(content), name)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'format', format, fromContent });
  });

  it('reads a workbook archive as xlsx, skips a byte-order mark and takes an empty file by its extension', () => {
    expect(detectFormat(zip({ '[Content_Types].xml': '<Types/>', 'xl/workbook.xml': '<workbook/>' }), 'plan.xlsx')).toMatchObject({ kind: 'format', format: 'xlsx', fromContent: false });
    expect(detectFormat(concatBytes([0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf], bytesOf('ERMHDR\t20.12')), 'p.xer')).toMatchObject({ kind: 'format', format: 'xer', bomLength: 3 });
    expect(detectFormat(new Uint8Array(), 'empty.xer')).toMatchObject({ kind: 'format', format: 'xer', fromContent: false });
  });

  it('refuses what it cannot import, each with its message', () => {
    const refused = (bytes: Uint8Array, name: string) => detectFormat(bytes, name);
    expect(refused(bytesOf('<?xml version="1.0"?><APIBusinessObjects/>'), 'p6.xml')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: 'This is a Primavera P6 XML file. In P6, export the project as XER and import that.' });
    expect(refused(bytesOf('<?xml version="1.0"?><Workbook/>'), 'x.xml')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: NOT_MSPDI });
    expect(refused(bytesOf('<?xml version="1.0"?><!DOCTYPE Project [<!ENTITY a "b">]><Project xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/project"/>'), 'x.xml')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: NOT_MSPDI });
    expect(refused(zip({ 'word/document.xml': 'x' }), 'x.xlsx')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: "This file isn't a readable Excel workbook." });
    expect(refused(compoundFile(['EncryptionInfo', 'EncryptedPackage']), 'x.xlsx')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: 'This workbook is protected with a password. Remove the password in Excel, save it and import it again.' });
    expect(refused(compoundFile(['   114', 'Props']), 'plan.dat')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: MPP });
    expect(refused(compoundFile(['Workbook']), 'plan.mpp')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: MPP });
    expect(refused(compoundFile(['Workbook']), 'old.xls')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: "Older Excel workbooks (.xls) can't be imported. In Excel, save the file as .xlsx and import that." });
    expect(refused(bytesOf('SQLite format 3\0'), 'x.xer')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: 'This is a database file, not a schedule export. Export the project as XER from P6 or as XML from Microsoft Project.' });
    expect(refused(bytesOf('just text'), 'notes.xer')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: UNSUPPORTED });
    expect(refused(new Uint8Array([0x41, 0, 0x42]), 'bin.csv')).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: UNSUPPORTED });
  });
});
```

`src/import/encoding.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { decodeText, detectEncoding, encodingIssues } from '@/import/encoding';
import { bytesOf, concatBytes } from '@/import/testing';

describe('encoding', () => {
  it('lets a byte-order mark decide', () => {
    expect(detectEncoding(new Uint8Array([0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf, 0x41]))).toMatchObject({ name: 'utf-8', detectedBy: 'bom', bomLength: 3 });
    expect(detectEncoding(new Uint8Array([0xff, 0xfe, 0x41, 0]))).toMatchObject({ name: 'utf-16le', detectedBy: 'bom', bomLength: 2 });
    expect(detectEncoding(new Uint8Array([0xfe, 0xff, 0, 0x41]))).toMatchObject({ name: 'utf-16be', detectedBy: 'bom', bomLength: 2 });
  });

  it('reads valid UTF-8 as UTF-8', () => {
    expect(detectEncoding(bytesOf('Kazı işleri'))).toEqual({ name: 'utf-8', detectedBy: 'utf8', bomLength: 0, invalidBytes: 0 });
    expect(encodingIssues(detectEncoding(bytesOf('Kazı')))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('keeps mostly valid UTF-8 and counts the invalid bytes', () => {
    const bytes = concatBytes(bytesOf('ş'.repeat(100)), [0xfd]);
    expect(detectEncoding(bytes)).toMatchObject({ name: 'utf-8', detectedBy: 'utf8', invalidBytes: 1 });
    expect(decodeText(bytes, 'utf-8').endsWith('�')).toBe(true);
    expect(encodingIssues(detectEncoding(bytes))).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'ENCODING_INVALID_BYTES', message: "1 bytes aren't valid UTF-8 and were replaced with �. If letters look wrong, choose another encoding." }),
    ]);
  });

  it('scores Turkish letters against Windows-1252 letters, defaulting to Windows-1254', () => {
    expect(detectEncoding(new Uint8Array([0x4b, 0x61, 0x7a, 0xfd]))).toMatchObject({ name: 'windows-1254', detectedBy: 'byte-score' });
    expect(detectEncoding(new Uint8Array([0x8e, 0x9e, 0x41]))).toMatchObject({ name: 'windows-1252', detectedBy: 'byte-score' });
    expect(detectEncoding(new Uint8Array([0xe9, 0x41]))).toMatchObject({ name: 'windows-1254', detectedBy: 'byte-score' });
    expect(encodingIssues(detectEncoding(new Uint8Array([0xfd])))).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'ENCODING_GUESSED', message: 'Text encoding detected as Turkish (Windows-1254). If letters look wrong, choose another encoding.' }),
    ]);
  });

  it('decodes with the encoding the user chose', () => {
    expect(decodeText(new Uint8Array([0xdd, 0xfe]), 'windows-1254')).toBe('İş');
    expect(decodeText(new Uint8Array([0xdd, 0xfe]), 'windows-1252')).toBe('Ýþ');
    expect(decodeText(new Uint8Array([0xef, 0xbb, 0xbf, 0x41]), 'utf-8', 3)).toBe('A');
  });
});
```

```ts
describe('numbers', () => {
  it('reads decimal commas and periods, grouping and percent signs', () => {
    expect(parseNumber('2,5', ',')).toBe(2.5);
    expect(parseNumber('1.234,5', ',')).toBe(1234.5);
    expect(parseNumber('1,234.5', '.')).toBe(1234.5);
    expect(parseNumber(' 50% ', '.')).toBe(50);
    expect(parseNumber('-3', '.')).toBe(-3);
    expect(parseNumber('', ',')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseNumber('abc', '.')).toBeNaN();
  });

  it('guesses the decimal separator from samples', () => {
    expect(guessDecimalSeparator(['2,5', '10', '1.234,5'])).toBe(',');
    expect(guessDecimalSeparator(['2.5', '1,234.5'])).toBe('.');
    expect(guessDecimalSeparator(['10', '20'])).toBe('.');
  });
});
```

`src/import/durations.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseDuration } from '@/import/durations';

const days = { unit: 'days', hoursPerDay: 8, decimal: '.' } as const;
const minutes = (text: string, options: Parameters<typeof parseDuration>[1] = days) => {
  const result = parseDuration(text, options);
  return result.kind === 'ok' ? result.minutes : result.kind;
};

describe('durations', () => {
  it('reads English and Turkish units, bare numbers in the chosen unit and hours per day', () => {
    expect(['5 days', '2 hrs', '30m', '1 wk', '1 mo', '3 gün', '2 saat', '1 hafta', '1 ay', '45 dk', '4'].map((t) => minutes(t))).toEqual([
      2400, 120, 30, 2400, 9600, 1440, 120, 2400, 9600, 45, 1920,
    ]);
    expect(minutes('2,5 g', { ...days, decimal: ',' })).toBe(1200);
    expect(minutes('4', { ...days, unit: 'hours' })).toBe(240);
    expect(minutes('3 d', { ...days, hoursPerDay: 10 })).toBe(1800);
  });

  it('marks estimates and elapsed time, and refuses the rest', () => {
    expect(parseDuration('5 days?', days)).toEqual({ kind: 'ok', minutes: 2400, elapsed: false, estimated: true });
    expect(parseDuration('2 edays', days)).toEqual({ kind: 'ok', minutes: 2880, elapsed: true, estimated: false });
    expect(parseDuration('3 egün', days)).toEqual({ kind: 'ok', minutes: 4320, elapsed: true, estimated: false });
    expect(parseDuration('', days)).toEqual({ kind: 'empty' });
    expect(parseDuration('soon', days)).toEqual({ kind: 'invalid' });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { excelSerialToWallClock, guessDateFormat, parseMspdiDate, parseTableDate, parseXerDate } from '@/import/dates';

describe('dates', () => {
  it('reads XER and MSPDI timestamps as wall-clock text', () => {
    expect(['2026-3-5 8:00', '2026-03-05', '2026-03-05 17', '2026-03-05 08:30:15'].map(parseXerDate)).toEqual([
      '2026-03-05T08:00', '2026-03-05T00:00', '2026-03-05T17:00', '2026-03-05T08:30',
    ]);
    expect(parseXerDate('')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseXerDate('2026-02-30 08:00')).toBe('invalid');
    expect(parseMspdiDate('2026-03-05T08:00:00')).toBe('2026-03-05T08:00');
    expect(parseMspdiDate('05/03/2026')).toBe('invalid');
  });

  it('reads table dates with weekdays, month names, two-digit years and times', () => {
    expect(parseTableDate('15.03.2026', 'DD.MM.YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-15', time: null });
    expect(parseTableDate('5/3/26', 'DD/MM/YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-05', time: null });
    expect(parseTableDate('3/16/26 8:00 AM', 'MM/DD/YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-16', time: '08:00' });
    expect(parseTableDate('Mon 3/16/26 5:00 PM', 'MM/DD/YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-16', time: '17:00' });
    expect(parseTableDate('Pazartesi 16.03.2026 08:00', 'DD.MM.YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-16', time: '08:00' });
    expect(parseTableDate('15 Mart 2026', 'DD.MM.YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-15', time: null });
    expect(parseTableDate('15 Şub 2026', 'DD.MM.YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-02-15', time: null });
    expect(parseTableDate('March 15, 2026', 'MM/DD/YYYY')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-15', time: null });
    expect(parseTableDate('2026-03-15', 'YYYY-MM-DD')).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-15', time: null });
    expect(parseTableDate('31.02.2026', 'DD.MM.YYYY')).toBe('invalid');
    expect(parseTableDate('', 'DD.MM.YYYY')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('converts Excel serials in both date systems', () => {
    expect(excelSerialToWallClock(46096, false)).toBe('2026-03-15T00:00');
    expect(excelSerialToWallClock(46096.5, false)).toBe('2026-03-15T12:00');
    expect(excelSerialToWallClock(44634, true)).toBe('2026-03-15T00:00');
    expect(excelSerialToWallClock(59, false)).toBe('1900-02-28T00:00');
    expect(excelSerialToWallClock(61, false)).toBe('1900-03-01T00:00');
  });

  it('guesses the date format that reads every sample', () => {
    expect(guessDateFormat(['15.03.2026', '1.4.2026'])).toBe('DD.MM.YYYY');
    expect(guessDateFormat(['Mon 3/16/26 8:00 AM', '3/2/26'])).toBe('MM/DD/YYYY');
    expect(guessDateFormat(['15/03/2026'])).toBe('DD/MM/YYYY');
    expect(guessDateFormat(['2026-03-15'])).toBe('YYYY-MM-DD');
    expect(guessDateFormat(['soon'])).toBe('DD.MM.YYYY');
  });
});
```

### Task 10: XER reader

**Spec:** §5.5 (all), §5.10 (limits are Task 13's), §5.11 (`Object.create(null)` records), §7 Parser row (XER cases, tokenizer, `clndr_data`, calendar problems and inheritance). P3 full §5.5.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/import/xer/tokenize.ts`, `src/import/xer/tokenize.test.ts`, `src/import/xer/calendar-data.ts`, `src/import/xer/calendar-data.test.ts`, `src/import/xer/read.ts`, `src/import/xer/read.test.ts`, `src/import/__fixtures__/two-projects.xer`
- Modify: `vite.config.ts` (the `?bytes` loader), `src/vite-env.d.ts` (`declare module '*?bytes' { const bytes: Uint8Array; export default bytes; }`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's `ReadResult`, `makeIssue`, `createRecord`, `parseXerDate`, `decodeText`, `ImportModel`.
- Produces (`vite.config.ts`): an inline plugin `fixtureBytes()` (`enforce: 'pre'`) whose `load(id)` answers ids ending in `?bytes` with `export default Uint8Array.from(atob("<base64>"), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));` read with `node:fs/promises`, so tests in `src/` import fixtures as bytes under both Vitest configs.
- Produces (`xer/tokenize.ts`):
  ```ts
  export interface XerTable { name: string; fields: string[]; rows: Array<Record<string, string>>; firstLine: number }
  export interface XerFile { version: string | null; tables: Map<string, XerTable> }
  export function tokenizeXer(text: string): XerFile | { error: 'unreadable' };
  ```
  Lines split on `\r\n` or `\n`; `ERMHDR` (tab-separated; field 1 is the version) must come first; `%T` starts a table, `%F` names its fields, `%R` adds a record (rows from `createRecord`, unknown fields kept by name, missing ones absent); a line whose first tab-separated token is empty, or that doesn't start with `%`, continues the previous record's last field after a `\n` (without its leading tab); `%E` ends the file; blank lines are ignored; `%R` before `%F` → `{ error: 'unreadable' }`.
- Produces (`xer/calendar-data.ts`): `parseCalendarData(clndrData: string): { week: Period[][]; exceptions: Array<{ date: ISODate; periods: Period[] }> } | null` — the nested `(0||key(attr|value…)(children))` structure with entries separated by line breaks or `0x7F`; `DaysOfWeek` children `1`–`7` (1 = Sunday) each with periods `(s|08:00|f|12:00)` in 24-hour or `h:mm AM` form; `Exceptions` children `(d|<days since 1899-12-30>)` with their periods (none = non-working); `null` when the structure can't be read.
- Produces (`xer/read.ts`):
  ```ts
  export interface XerProjectInfo { id: string; code: string | null; name: string | null; exportFlag: boolean }
  export function listXerProjects(file: XerFile): XerProjectInfo[];   // PROJECT rows in file order; name = the project's root WBS node name
  export function readXer(file: XerFile, sourceProjectId: string): ReadResult;
  ```
  Pins beyond §5.5: calendar ids, WBS ids and activity `keys.taskId` are the source ids as text; `CA_Base` → `global`, `CA_Project` → `project`, `CA_Rsrc` → `resource`; the project reads every calendar its tasks or its default use (with their base chain) and every resource calendar; the root WBS node isn't counted; `lag_hr_cnt` and the hour counts become minutes (× 60); a link counts for the project of its successor or its predecessor, and one to the other project is skipped (`XER_CROSS_PROJECT_LINK`) and counted under that project's short name; `notImported` lists every table this reader doesn't read, in file order, with its whole row count; `settings` in this order when the field exists: `progress_mode` (`sched_retained_logic` = Y → `retained_logic`; else `sched_progress_override` = Y → `progress_override`; else `actual_dates`), then `sched_calendar_on_relationship_lag`, `act_this_per_link_flag`, `add_act_remain_flag`, `cost_qty_recalc_flag`, `reset_planned_flag` as written (V9 confirms the field names on real files). `mustFinishBy` comes from `plan_end_date`, the stated start and finish from `plan_start_date` and `scd_end_date`. An activity or relationship is adjusted when an issue says it was changed (`XER_RESOURCE_TASK`, `CALENDAR_MISSING`, `LINK_TYPE_UNKNOWN`, `TEXT_TRUNCATED`); each item counts once. Issue locations read `TASK line {n}`, `TASKPRED line {n}` or `CALENDAR line {n}`.
- The fixture `src/import/__fixtures__/two-projects.xer` (our own, UTF-8 without BOM, CRLF line ends, tab-separated, ending in `%E` and a blank line). Tables in this order with these rows (columns not listed are left out):
  - `ERMHDR	20.12	2026-11-01	Project	admin	Planner	dbxDatabaseNoName	Project Management	TRY`
  - `CURRTYPE`: one row.
  - `PROJECT` (`proj_id, proj_short_name, export_flag, last_recalc_date, plan_start_date, scd_end_date, plan_end_date, clndr_id, sched_retained_logic, sched_progress_override, sched_calendar_on_relationship_lag, act_this_per_link_flag, add_act_remain_flag, cost_qty_recalc_flag, reset_planned_flag`): `101, NTH-A, Y, 2026-10-15 08:00, 2026-09-01 08:00, 2026-12-18 17:00, 2026-12-31 00:00, 1, Y, N, rcal_Successor, Y, N, N, N`; `102, NTH-B, N, 2026-10-15 17:00, 2026-10-01 08:00, 2026-11-30 17:00, (empty), 1, Y, N, rcal_Predecessor, Y, N, N, N`.
  - `CALENDAR` (`clndr_id, clndr_name, clndr_type, base_clndr_id, proj_id, day_hr_cnt, week_hr_cnt, month_hr_cnt, year_hr_cnt, clndr_data`): `1, Standard 5 Day, CA_Base` — Mon–Fri 08:00–12:00 and 13:00–17:00, a non-working exception `d|46324` (2026-10-29), 8/40/172/2000, its `clndr_data` written with `0x7F` separators; `2, Night Shift, CA_Project, base 1, proj 101` — Mon–Fri 13:00–22:00, states `day_hr_cnt` 8, no exceptions of its own, `clndr_data` on one line; `3, Crane, CA_Rsrc` — Mon–Sat 07:00–15:00.
  - `PROJWBS` (`wbs_id, proj_id, parent_wbs_id, seq_num, proj_node_flag, wbs_short_name, wbs_name`): `1001, 101, —, 0, Y, NTH-A, North Plant`; `1002, 101, 1001, 10, N, CIV, Civil works`; `1003, 101, 1001, 20, N, MEC, Mechanical`; `2001, 102, —, 0, Y, NTH-B, North Annex`; `2002, 102, 2001, 10, N, FIT, Fit-out`.
  - `RSRC`: two rows.
  - `TASK` (`task_id, proj_id, wbs_id, clndr_id, task_code, task_name, task_type, status_code, phys_complete_pct, complete_pct_type, target_drtn_hr_cnt, remain_drtn_hr_cnt, act_start_date, act_end_date, early_start_date, early_end_date, late_start_date, late_end_date, total_float_hr_cnt, free_float_hr_cnt, cstr_type, cstr_date, expect_end_date, target_start_date, target_end_date`): `1, 101, 1002, 1, A1000, Mobilisation, TT_Mile, TK_Complete, 100, CP_Drtn, 0, 0, 2026-09-01 08:00, 2026-09-01 08:00`; `2, 101, 1002, 1, A1010, Kazı işleri, TT_Task, TK_Complete, 100, CP_Drtn, 40, 0, 2026-09-01 08:00, 2026-09-07 17:00`; `3, 101, 1002, 1, A1020, Foundations, TT_Task, TK_Active, 50, CP_Phys, 80, 40, 2026-09-08 08:00, —, …, cstr_type CS_MSOA, cstr_date 2026-09-08 08:00`; `4, 101, 1003, 2, A1030, Pump install, TT_Rsrc, TK_NotStart, 0, CP_Drtn, 36, 36`; `5, 101, 1003, 1, A1040, Commissioning, TT_FinMile, TK_NotStart, 0, CP_Drtn, 0, 0, …, expect_end_date 2026-12-18 17:00`; `6, 101, 1002, 1, A1050, Site supervision, TT_LOE, TK_Active, 0, CP_Drtn, 120, 80, 2026-09-01 08:00`; `11, 102, 2002, 1, B2000, Annex start, TT_Mile, TK_NotStart, 0, CP_Drtn, 0, 0`; `12, 102, 2002, 9, B2010, Fit-out works, TT_Task, TK_NotStart, 0, CP_Drtn, 80, 80`; `13, 102, 2002, 1, B2020, Handover, TT_FinMile, TK_NotStart, 0, CP_Drtn, 0, 0`. `src/import/xer/tokenize.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { tokenizeXer, type XerFile } from '@/import/xer/tokenize';

const lines = (...rows: string[]) => rows.join('\n');
const read = (text: string) => tokenizeXer(text) as XerFile;

describe('tokenizeXer', () => {
  it('reads tables by field name into prototype-free records', () => {
    const file = read(lines('ERMHDR\t20.12', '%T\tTASK', '%F\ttask_id\ttask_code\t__proto__\textra', '%R\t1\tA1\tx\ty', '%E'));
    const [row] = file.tables.get('TASK')!.rows;
    expect(file.version).toBe('20.12');
    expect(Object.getPrototypeOf(row)).toBeNull();
    expect({ ...row }).toEqual({ task_id: '1', task_code: 'A1', __proto__: 'x', extra: 'y' });
  });

  it('joins continuation lines and stops at %E', () => {
    const file = read(lines('ERMHDR\t20.12', '%T\tTASKMEMO', '%F\tmemo_id\ttask_memo', '%R\t1\tFirst line', '\tsecond line', 'third line', '%E', '%T\tIGNORED'));
    expect(file.tables.get('TASKMEMO')!.rows[0]!.task_memo).toBe('First line\nsecond line\nthird line');
    expect(file.tables.has('IGNORED')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('reads CRLF files with a trailing blank line like LF files', () => {
    const lf = lines('ERMHDR\t20.12', '%T\tTASK', '%F\ttask_id\ttask_code', '%R\t1\tA1', '%E', '');
    expect(read(lf.replaceAll('\n', '\r\n'))).toEqual(read(lf));
  });

  it('refuses a record before its field names', () => {
    expect(tokenizeXer(lines('ERMHDR\t20.12', '%T\tTASK', '%R\t1\tA1'))).toEqual({ error: 'unreadable' });
  });
});
```

`src/import/xer/calendar-data.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parseCalendarData } from '@/import/xer/calendar-data';

const day = (n: number, periods: string) => `(0||${n}()(${periods}))`;
const period = (s: string, f: string) => `(0||0(s|${s}|f|${f})())`;
const data = (days: string, exceptions = '') =>
  `(0||CalendarData()(\n(0||DaysOfWeek()(\n${days}))\n(0||Exceptions()(${exceptions}))))`;

describe('parseCalendarData', () => {
  it('reads the week, several periods a day, 24-hour and AM/PM times, and exceptions', () => {
    const week = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]
      .map((n) => (n === 1 || n === 7 ? day(n, '') : n === 2 ? day(n, period('8:00 AM', '12:00 PM') + period('13:00', '17:00')) : day(n, period('08:00', '17:00'))))
      .join('\n');
    const parsed = parseCalendarData(data(week, '(0||0(d|46324)())(0||1(d|46325)((0||0(s|08:00|f|12:00)())))'))!;
    expect(parsed.week[0]).toEqual([]);
    expect(parsed.week[1]).toEqual([{ start: 480, finish: 720 }, { start: 780, finish: 1020 }]);
    expect(parsed.week[2]).toEqual([{ start: 480, finish: 1020 }]);
    expect(parsed.exceptions).toEqual([
      { date: '2026-10-29', periods: [] },
      { date: '2026-10-30', periods: [{ start: 480, finish: 720 }] },
    ]);
  });

  it('accepts 0x7F separators and answers null for data it cannot read', () => {
    const week = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7].map((n) => day(n, n === 2 ? period('08:00', '17:00') : '')).join('\x7f');
    expect(parseCalendarData(data(week).replaceAll('\n', '\x7f'))!.week[1]).toEqual([{ start: 480, finish: 1020 }]);
    expect(parseCalendarData('(0||CalendarData()((0||DaysOfWeek(')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`src/import/xer/read.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import fixture from '@/import/__fixtures__/two-projects.xer?bytes';
import { decodeText } from '@/import/encoding';
import { listXerProjects, readXer } from '@/import/xer/read';
import { tokenizeXer, type XerFile } from '@/import/xer/tokenize';

const file = () => tokenizeXer(decodeText(fixture, 'utf-8')) as XerFile;
const codes = (issues: { code: string }[]) => issues.map((i) => i.code).sort();
/** The fixture's text with one CALENDAR row's fields replaced. */
function withCalendar(id: string, patch: Record<string, string>): XerFile {
  const parsed = file();
  const row = parsed.tables.get('CALENDAR')!.rows.find((r) => r.clndr_id === id)!;
  Object.assign(row, patch);
  return parsed;
}

describe('readXer', () => {
  it('lists the projects of the file with their export flags', () => {
    expect(listXerProjects(file())).toEqual([
      { id: '101', code: 'NTH-A', name: 'North Plant', exportFlag: true },
      { id: '102', code: 'NTH-B', name: 'North Annex', exportFlag: false },
    ]);
  });

  it('reads project NTH-A with its counts, issues, links and settings', () => {
    const result = readXer(file(), '101');
    expect(result.counts).toEqual([
      { entity: 'calendar', read: 3, accepted: 3, adjusted: 1, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'wbs', read: 2, accepted: 2, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'activity', read: 6, accepted: 6, adjusted: 1, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'relationship', read: 6, accepted: 5, adjusted: 1, skipped: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual([
      'CALENDAR_HOURS_MISMATCH', 'LINK_TYPE_UNKNOWN', 'XER_CROSS_PROJECT_LINK', 'XER_EXPECTED_FINISH', 'XER_MULTIPLE_PROJECTS', 'XER_RESOURCE_TASK',
    ]);
    const message = (code: string) => result.issues.find((i) => i.code === code)!.message;
    expect(message('XER_MULTIPLE_PROJECTS')).toBe('The file holds 2 projects. This schedule is made from NTH-A.');
    expect(message('XER_CROSS_PROJECT_LINK')).toBe('The link A1040 → B2020 points to another project and was skipped.');
    expect(message('CALENDAR_HOURS_MISMATCH')).toBe('Calendar Night Shift states 8 hours a day, but its work week has 9. 9 is used.');
    expect(message('XER_EXPECTED_FINISH')).toBe('1 activities have an expected finish date. Expected finish isn\'t used, so their remaining durations decide their dates.');
    expect(result.externalLinks).toEqual([{ target: 'NTH-B', links: 1 }]);
    expect(result.notImported).toEqual([
      { table: 'CURRTYPE', rows: 1 }, { table: 'RSRC', rows: 2 }, { table: 'TASKRSRC', rows: 1 }, { table: 'UDFTYPE', rows: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(result.formatVersion).toBe('20.12');
    expect(result.statusDate).toEqual({ date: '2026-10-15', time: '08:00', source: 'file' });
    expect(result.sourceProject).toEqual({
      id: '101', code: 'NTH-A', name: 'North Plant', projectsInFile: 2, rootWbsName: 'North Plant',
      mustFinishBy: '2026-12-31', stated: { start: '2026-09-01', finish: '2026-12-18' },
    });
    const { model } = result;
    expect(model.project).toMatchObject({ code: 'NTH-A', name: 'North Plant', statusDate: '2026-10-15T08:00', defaultCalendarId: '1', mustFinishBy: '2026-12-31T00:00' });
    expect(model.activities.map((a) => [a.code, a.type, a.status, a.calendarId, a.originalDuration, a.remainingDuration])).toEqual([
      ['A1000', 'start_milestone', 'completed', '1', 0, 0],
      ['A1010', 'task', 'completed', '1', 2400, 0],
      ['A1020', 'task', 'in_progress', '1', 4800, 2400],
      ['A1030', 'task', 'not_started', '2', 2160, 2160],
      ['A1040', 'finish_milestone', 'not_started', '1', 0, 0],
      ['A1050', 'level_of_effort', 'in_progress', '1', 7200, 4800],
    ]);
    const a1020 = model.activities.find((a) => a.code === 'A1020')!;
    expect(a1020).toMatchObject({ keys: { taskId: '3', uid: null, guid: null }, wbsId: '1002', constraint: { type: 'start_on_or_after', date: '2026-09-08T08:00' }, percent: { physical: 50, type: 'physical' } });
    expect(model.activities.find((a) => a.code === 'A1030')!.flags.resourceDependent).toBe(true);
    expect(model.relationships).toEqual([
      { predecessorCode: 'A1000', successorCode: 'A1010', type: 'FS', lagMinutes: 0, lagElapsed: false },
      { predecessorCode: 'A1010', successorCode: 'A1020', type: 'SS', lagMinutes: 480, lagElapsed: false },
      { predecessorCode: 'A1020', successorCode: 'A1030', type: 'FS', lagMinutes: 0, lagElapsed: false },
      { predecessorCode: 'A1030', successorCode: 'A1040', type: 'FF', lagMinutes: -240, lagElapsed: false },
      { predecessorCode: 'A1000', successorCode: 'A1050', type: 'FS', lagMinutes: 0, lagElapsed: false },
    ]);
    expect(model.wbs.map((w) => [w.id, w.code, w.name, w.parentId, w.order])).toEqual([['1002', 'CIV', 'Civil works', null, 10], ['1003', 'MEC', 'Mechanical', null, 20]]);
    expect(model.calendars.map((c) => [c.id, c.name, c.kind, c.hoursPerDay])).toEqual([['1', 'Standard 5 Day', 'global', 8], ['2', 'Night Shift', 'project', 9], ['3', 'Crane', 'resource', 8]]);
    expect(model.calendars[0]!.week[1]).toEqual([{ start: 480, finish: 720 }, { start: 780, finish: 1020 }]);
    expect(model.calendars[1]!.exceptions).toEqual([{ date: '2026-10-29', periods: [] }]);
    expect(model.settings).toEqual([
      { key: 'progress_mode', value: 'retained_logic' },
      { key: 'sched_calendar_on_relationship_lag', value: 'rcal_Successor' },
      { key: 'act_this_per_link_flag', value: 'Y' },
      { key: 'add_act_remain_flag', value: 'N' },
      { key: 'cost_qty_recalc_flag', value: 'N' },
      { key: 'reset_planned_flag', value: 'N' },
    ]);
  });

  it('reads project NTH-B on its own, with the project calendar for a missing one', () => {
    const result = readXer(file(), '102');
    expect(result.counts).toEqual([
      { entity: 'calendar', read: 2, accepted: 2, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'wbs', read: 1, accepted: 1, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'activity', read: 3, accepted: 3, adjusted: 1, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'relationship', read: 3, accepted: 2, adjusted: 0, skipped: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['CALENDAR_MISSING', 'XER_CROSS_PROJECT_LINK', 'XER_MULTIPLE_PROJECTS']);
    expect(result.externalLinks).toEqual([{ target: 'NTH-A', links: 1 }]);
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => [a.code, a.calendarId])).toEqual([['B2000', '1'], ['B2010', '1'], ['B2020', '1']]);
    expect(result.sourceProject).toMatchObject({ code: 'NTH-B', mustFinishBy: null });
  });

  it('stops on a calendar it needs but cannot read, or whose base is missing', () => {
    expect(codes(readXer(withCalendar('2', { clndr_data: '(0||CalendarData()(' }), '101').issues)).toContain('CALENDAR_UNREADABLE');
    const missingBase = readXer(withCalendar('2', { base_clndr_id: '77' }), '101');
    expect(missingBase.issues.find((i) => i.code === 'CALENDAR_BASE_MISSING')).toMatchObject({
      severity: 'error', message: "Calendar Night Shift is based on a calendar that isn't in the file. Export the file with that calendar included.",
    });
  });

  it('skips an unreadable calendar nothing uses, and flags a code used twice', () => {
    const skipped = readXer(withCalendar('3', { clndr_data: '(0||CalendarData()(' }), '101');
    expect(skipped.issues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error')).toEqual([]);
    expect(skipped.issues.find((i) => i.code === 'CALENDAR_UNUSED_SKIPPED')!.message).toBe("Calendar Crane couldn't be read completely. Nothing uses it, so it was skipped.");
    expect(skipped.counts[0]).toEqual({ entity: 'calendar', read: 3, accepted: 2, adjusted: 1, skipped: 1 });
    const twice = file();
    twice.tables.get('TASK')!.rows.find((r) => r.task_code === 'A1050')!.task_code = 'A1000';
    expect(readXer(twice, '101').issues.find((i) => i.code === 'DUPLICATE_CODE')!.message).toBe('Activity code A1000 is used more than once.');
  });

  it('reads the same model from CRLF and LF text', () => {
    const lf = decodeText(fixture, 'utf-8').replaceAll('\r\n', '\n');
    expect(readXer(tokenizeXer(lf) as XerFile, '101').model).toEqual(readXer(file(), '101').model);
  });
});
```

(The resource calendar Crane is read for every project and used by no activity, so an unreadable Crane is skipped with a warning.)

### Task 11: MSPDI reader

**Spec:** §5.6 (all), §5.5 "Calendar problems", §5.11 (DOCTYPE), §7 Parser row (MSPDI cases). P3 full §5.6.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/import/mspdi/xml-text.ts`, `src/import/mspdi/xml-text.test.ts`, `src/import/mspdi/calendars.ts`, `src/import/mspdi/calendars.test.ts`, `src/import/mspdi/read.ts`, `src/import/mspdi/read.test.ts`, `src/import/calendars.ts`, `src/import/__fixtures__/project.xml`
- Modify: `src/import/testing.ts` (`mspdiXml`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's modules; `fast-xml-parser`.
- Produces (`src/import/calendars.ts`, shared with Task 12):
  ```ts
  export const STANDARD_CALENDAR_ID = 'gen:standard';
  export const ELAPSED_CALENDAR_ID = 'gen:24h';
  export function standardCalendar(hoursPerDay: number): ImportCalendar;      // "Standard", kind project: Mon–Fri from 08:00, hoursPerDay hours, 1 h break at 12:00 when hoursPerDay > 4
  export function calendarFromName(name: string, hoursPerDay: number): ImportCalendar; // id `gen:name:${foldForMatch(name)}`, kind global, the standard week
  export function elapsedCalendar(): ImportCalendar;                          // "24 hours", kind task, every day 0–1440
  export function workingDay(calendar: ImportCalendar, date: ISODate): Period[]; // the exception's periods, else the weekday's
  ```
- Produces (`mspdi/xml-text.ts`): `decodeXmlText(text: string): { text: string; badCharacters: string[] }` — the five entities, decimal and hex references; U+0000, surrogates and code points above U+10FFFF become U+FFFD and are listed.
- Produces (`mspdi/calendars.ts`): `readMspdiCalendars(calendars: unknown[], span: { start: ISODate; finish: ISODate }): { calendars: Map<string, ImportCalendar>; issues: ImportIssue[]; unreadable: Set<string> }` — `WeekDays` (DayType 1–7 = Sunday–Saturday, DayType 0 legacy exceptions), 2007+ `Exceptions` with `Type` 1 daily, 2 yearly by month day, 3 yearly by position, 4 monthly by day, 5 monthly by position, 6 weekly (`DaysOfWeek` bit 1 = Sunday … 64 = Saturday); any other type → that calendar is unreadable (`CALENDAR_RECURRENCE_UNSUPPORTED`); recurrences expand from `span.start` to 20 years after `span.finish`, at most 10,000 dates (`CALENDAR_RECURRENCE_CAPPED` with the last kept date); `WorkWeeks` → default week only (`MSPDI_WORK_WEEKS`); derived calendars inherit weekdays and exceptions down the chain (cycle-safe, at most 5 levels). V9 confirms the exception type numbers on the owner's file.
- Produces (`mspdi/read.ts`):
  ```ts
  export function mspdiCodeFields(text: string): string[];   // 'UID', 'ID', then each TextN filled and unique on at least 90 % of activities, in number order
  export function readMspdi(text: string, options: { activityCodeField: string }): ReadResult;
  ```
  Pins beyond §5.6: `fast-xml-parser` with `parseTagValue: false`, `processEntities: false`, `removeNSPrefix: true`, `ignoreAttributes: true`, `isArray` for `Task`, `PredecessorLink`, `Calendar`, `WeekDay`, `WorkingTime`, `Exception`, `ExtendedAttribute`; nesting deeper than 100 levels or a parse error → `FILE_UNREADABLE`. Durations `PTnHnMnS` become working minutes; `DurationFormat` and `LagFormat` 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 36, 38, 40, 42, 44 are elapsed; `LagFormat` 19, 20, 51, 52 are percentages; `LinkLag` is tenths of a minute. Activity codes are the chosen field's value (UID by default); the WBS code is the last segment of `OutlineNumber` as text (`1.10` → `10`). A start milestone sits on its calendar's first working minute of that day, a finish milestone on its last. Status date: `StatusDate`, else the earliest task start with `source: 'earliest-start'` (the issue is Task 13's check); `units.minutesPerDay` from `MinutesPerDay` (default 480). Settings, in order when present: `ScheduleFromStart`, `CalendarUID`, `MinutesPerDay`, `MinutesPerWeek`, `DaysPerMonth`, `DefaultStartTime`, `DefaultFinishTime`. Issue locations read `Task UID {n}` or `Calendar UID {n}`; an item is adjusted when `MSPDI_MANUAL_TASK`, `MSPDI_MANUAL_START_DROPPED`, `MSPDI_MILESTONE_WITH_DURATION`, `MSPDI_MILESTONE_AMBIGUOUS`, `MSPDI_ZERO_DURATION`, `ELAPSED_DURATION`, `CODE_FALLBACK`, `TEXT_TRUNCATED`, `XML_BAD_CHARACTER`, `LINK_TYPE_UNKNOWN`, `PERCENT_LAG` or `MSPDI_WORK_WEEKS` names it.
- Produces (`src/import/testing.ts`): `mspdiXml(parts: { project?: string; calendars?: string; tasks: string }): string` — a complete `<Project xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/project">` document with `SaveVersion` 14, `MinutesPerDay` 480, `CalendarUID` 1, the given project elements, the given calendars (default: UID 1 "Standard", Monday–Friday 08:00–12:00 and 13:00–17:00) and the given task elements.
- The fixture `src/import/__fixtures__/project.xml` (our own; UTF-8, `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>`): project `Name` "Depot Upgrade", `SaveVersion` 14, `StartDate` 2026-03-02T08:00:00, `FinishDate` 2026-04-10T17:00:00, `StatusDate` 2026-03-20T17:00:00, `CalendarUID` 1, `MinutesPerDay` 480, `MinutesPerWeek` 2400, `DaysPerMonth` 20, `ScheduleFromStart` 1, `DefaultStartTime` 08:00:00, `DefaultFinishTime` 17:00:00. Calendars: UID 1 "Standard" (base; Sunday and Saturday off; Monday–Friday 08:00–12:00 and 13:00–17:00; a non-working exception on 2026-03-23); UID 2 "Saturday crew" (`IsBaseCalendar` 0, `BaseCalendarUID` 1; only Saturday listed, working 08:00–12:00). Tasks: UID 0 project summary; UID 1 summary "Site works" (OutlineLevel 1, OutlineNumber 1); UID 2 "Kickoff" (`Milestone` 1, `Duration` PT0H0M0S, start and finish 2026-03-02T08:00:00, OutlineNumber 1.1); UID 3 "Kazı &amp; Dolgu" (PT40H0M0S, 2026-03-02T08:00:00 – 2026-03-06T17:00:00, `Notes` "Check &#x130;zmir crane"); UID 4 "Formwork" (`Manual` 1, `ManualStart` 2026-03-09T08:00:00, PT24H0M0S, `ConstraintType` 0); UID 5 "Inspection" (`CalendarUID` 2, PT8H0M0S); UID 6 "Handover" (`Milestone` 1, zero duration, start and finish 2026-04-10T17:00:00); UID 7 summary "Design" (OutlineLevel 1, OutlineNumber 2); UID 8 "Approve drawings" (OutlineLevel 2, OutlineNumber 2.1, PT16H0M0S, `PercentComplete` 100, `ActualStart` 2026-03-02T08:00:00, `ActualFinish` 2026-03-03T17:00:00). Links: 3 ← 2 (Type 1), 4 ← 3 (Type 3, `LinkLag` 4800, `LagFormat` 5), 5 ← 4 (Type 1), 6 ← 5 (Type 1), 6 ← 8 (Type 1).

`src/import/mspdi/calendars.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { XMLParser } from 'fast-xml-parser';
import { readMspdiCalendars } from '@/import/mspdi/calendars';

const parse = (xml: string) =>
  (new XMLParser({ parseTagValue: false, processEntities: false, ignoreAttributes: true, isArray: (n: string) => ['Calendar', 'WeekDay', 'WorkingTime', 'Exception'].includes(n) })
    .parse(`<Calendars>${xml}</Calendars>`) as { Calendars: { Calendar: unknown[] } }).Calendars.Calendar;
const weekDay = (type: number, working: boolean, times = '') =>
  `<WeekDay><DayType>${type}</DayType><DayWorking>${working ? 1 : 0}</DayWorking>${times ? `<WorkingTimes>${times}</WorkingTimes>` : ''}</WeekDay>`;
const time = (from: string, to: string) => `<WorkingTime><FromTime>${from}:00</FromTime><ToTime>${to}:00</ToTime></WorkingTime>`;
const base = `<Calendar><UID>1</UID><Name>Standard</Name><IsBaseCalendar>1</IsBaseCalendar><WeekDays>${weekDay(1, false)}${[2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map((d) => weekDay(d, true, time('08:00', '12:00') + time('13:00', '17:00'))).join('')}${weekDay(7, false)}</WeekDays></Calendar>`;
const span = { start: '2026-03-02', finish: '2026-04-10' };

describe('readMspdiCalendars', () => {
  it('inherits weekdays and exceptions down a derived chain, cycle-safe', () => {
    const derived = `<Calendar><UID>2</UID><Name>Saturday crew</Name><IsBaseCalendar>0</IsBaseCalendar><BaseCalendarUID>1</BaseCalendarUID><WeekDays>${weekDay(7, true, time('08:00', '12:00'))}</WeekDays></Calendar>`;
    const looped = `<Calendar><UID>3</UID><Name>Loop A</Name><IsBaseCalendar>0</IsBaseCalendar><BaseCalendarUID>4</BaseCalendarUID></Calendar><Calendar><UID>4</UID><Name>Loop B</Name><IsBaseCalendar>0</IsBaseCalendar><BaseCalendarUID>3</BaseCalendarUID></Calendar>`;
    const { calendars, unreadable } = readMspdiCalendars(parse(base + derived + looped), span);
    expect(calendars.get('2')!.week[6]).toEqual([{ start: 480, finish: 720 }]);
    expect(calendars.get('2')!.week[1]).toEqual([{ start: 480, finish: 720 }, { start: 780, finish: 1020 }]);
    expect([...unreadable].sort()).toEqual(['3', '4']);
  });

  it('expands weekly and yearly recurrences up to 10,000 dates and reports what it cannot read', () => {
    const exception = (inner: string) => `<Calendar><UID>5</UID><Name>Holidays</Name><IsBaseCalendar>0</IsBaseCalendar><BaseCalendarUID>1</BaseCalendarUID><Exceptions><Exception>${inner}</Exception></Exceptions></Calendar>`;
    const weekly = exception('<EnteredByOccurrences>0</EnteredByOccurrences><TimePeriod><FromDate>2026-03-06T00:00:00</FromDate><ToDate>2026-03-27T23:59:00</ToDate></TimePeriod><Type>6</Type><Period>1</Period><DaysOfWeek>32</DaysOfWeek><DayWorking>0</DayWorking>');
    expect(readMspdiCalendars(parse(base + weekly), span).calendars.get('5')!.exceptions.map((e) => e.date)).toEqual(['2026-03-06', '2026-03-13', '2026-03-20', '2026-03-27']);
    const yearly = exception('<EnteredByOccurrences>0</EnteredByOccurrences><TimePeriod><FromDate>2026-01-01T00:00:00</FromDate><ToDate>2049-12-31T23:59:00</ToDate></TimePeriod><Type>2</Type><Month>0</Month><MonthDay>1</MonthDay><DayWorking>0</DayWorking>');
    expect(readMspdiCalendars(parse(base + yearly), span).calendars.get('5')!.exceptions.map((e) => e.date).slice(0, 2)).toEqual(['2026-01-01', '2027-01-01']);
    const daily = exception('<EnteredByOccurrences>0</EnteredByOccurrences><TimePeriod><FromDate>2026-01-01T00:00:00</FromDate><ToDate>2060-12-31T23:59:00</ToDate></TimePeriod><Type>1</Type><Period>1</Period><DayWorking>0</DayWorking>');
    const capped = readMspdiCalendars(parse(base + daily), span);
    expect(capped.calendars.get('5')!.exceptions).toHaveLength(10000);
    expect(capped.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toEqual(['CALENDAR_RECURRENCE_CAPPED']);
    const odd = readMspdiCalendars(parse(base + exception('<Type>99</Type><DayWorking>0</DayWorking>')), span);
    expect([...odd.unreadable]).toEqual(['5']);
    expect(odd.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toEqual(['CALENDAR_RECURRENCE_UNSUPPORTED']);
  });
});
```

`src/import/mspdi/read.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import fixture from '@/import/__fixtures__/project.xml?bytes';
import { mspdiCodeFields, readMspdi } from '@/import/mspdi/read';
import { mspdiXml } from '@/import/testing';

const text = new TextDecoder().decode(fixture);
const uid = { activityCodeField: 'UID' };
const codes = (issues: { code: string }[]) => issues.map((i) => i.code).sort();
const task = (uidValue: number, inner: string) =>
  `<Task><UID>${uidValue}</UID><ID>${uidValue}</ID><Name>Task ${uidValue}</Name><OutlineLevel>1</OutlineLevel><OutlineNumber>${uidValue}</OutlineNumber>${inner}</Task>`;
const dates = (start: string, finish = start) => `<Start>${start}</Start><Finish>${finish}</Finish>`;

describe('readMspdi', () => {
  it('reads the fixture with its counts, milestones, manual task and escaped text', () => {
    const result = readMspdi(text, uid);
    expect(result.counts).toEqual([
      { entity: 'calendar', read: 2, accepted: 2, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'wbs', read: 2, accepted: 2, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'activity', read: 6, accepted: 6, adjusted: 1, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'relationship', read: 5, accepted: 5, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
    ]);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['MSPDI_MANUAL_TASK']);
    expect(result.issues[0]!.message).toBe('4 is manually scheduled in the file. It starts on or after 2026-03-09 08:00.');
    expect(result.formatVersion).toBe('14');
    expect(result.statusDate).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-20', time: '17:00', source: 'file' });
    expect(result.units).toEqual({ minutesPerDay: 480, calendars: [{ name: 'Standard', hoursPerDay: 8 }, { name: 'Saturday crew', hoursPerDay: 8 }] });
    const { model } = result;
    expect(model.activities.map((a) => [a.code, a.name, a.type, a.status, a.calendarId, a.originalDuration])).toEqual([
      ['2', 'Kickoff', 'start_milestone', 'not_started', '1', 0],
      ['3', 'Kazı & Dolgu', 'task', 'not_started', '1', 2400],
      ['4', 'Formwork', 'task', 'not_started', '1', 1440],
      ['5', 'Inspection', 'task', 'not_started', '2', 480],
      ['6', 'Handover', 'finish_milestone', 'not_started', '1', 0],
      ['8', 'Approve drawings', 'task', 'completed', '1', 960],
    ]);
    expect(model.activities.find((a) => a.code === '3')!.notes).toBe('Check İzmir crane');
    expect(model.activities.find((a) => a.code === '4')).toMatchObject({ constraint: { type: 'start_on_or_after', date: '2026-03-09T08:00' }, flags: { manual: true } });
    expect(model.wbs.map((w) => [w.code, w.name])).toEqual([['1', 'Site works'], ['2', 'Design']]);
    expect(model.relationships.find((r) => r.successorCode === '4')).toEqual({ predecessorCode: '3', successorCode: '4', type: 'SS', lagMinutes: 480, lagElapsed: false });
    expect(model.calendars.find((c) => c.id === '1')!.exceptions).toEqual([{ date: '2026-03-23', periods: [] }]);
  });

  it('derives status from actual dates, then percent complete', () => {
    const tasks = [
      task(1, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration><ActualFinish>2026-03-02T17:00:00</ActualFinish>`),
      task(2, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration><ActualStart>2026-03-02T08:00:00</ActualStart>`),
      task(3, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration><PercentComplete>100</PercentComplete><PhysicalPercentComplete>40</PhysicalPercentComplete>`),
      task(4, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration><PercentComplete>10</PercentComplete>`),
      task(5, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`),
    ].join('');
    const { model } = readMspdi(mspdiXml({ tasks }), uid);
    expect(model.activities.map((a) => a.status)).toEqual(['completed', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'in_progress', 'not_started']);
    expect(model.activities[2]!.percent).toEqual({ duration: 100, physical: 40, type: null });
  });

  it('offers UID, ID and Text fields filled and unique on 90% of activities, with fallbacks', () => {
    const tasks = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) =>
      task(i + 1, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>${i < 9 ? `<ExtendedAttribute><FieldID>188743731</FieldID><Value>K-${i + 1}</Value></ExtendedAttribute>` : ''}`),
    ).join('');
    const xml = mspdiXml({ tasks });
    expect(mspdiCodeFields(xml)).toEqual(['UID', 'ID', 'Text1']);
    const result = readMspdi(xml, { activityCodeField: 'Text1' });
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => a.code).slice(-2)).toEqual(['K-9', '10']);
    expect(result.issues.find((i) => i.code === 'CODE_FALLBACK')!.message).toBe('Task 10 has no usable code in Text1. Its unique ID 10 is used.');
  });

  it('fills a missing status date with the earliest task start', () => {
    const tasks = task(1, `${dates('2026-03-04T08:00:00', '2026-03-04T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`) + task(2, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`);
    expect(readMspdi(mspdiXml({ tasks }), uid).statusDate).toEqual({ date: '2026-03-02', time: '08:00', source: 'earliest-start' });
  });

  it('tells milestones at an unclear time, milestones with a duration and zero durations apart', () => {
    const tasks = [
      task(1, `<Milestone>1</Milestone>${dates('2026-03-02T10:00:00')}<Duration>PT0H0M0S</Duration>`),
      task(2, `<Milestone>1</Milestone>${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`),
      task(3, `<Milestone>0</Milestone>${dates('2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT0H0M0S</Duration>`),
    ].join('');
    const result = readMspdi(mspdiXml({ tasks }), uid);
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => a.type)).toEqual(['start_milestone', 'task', 'finish_milestone']);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['MSPDI_MILESTONE_AMBIGUOUS', 'MSPDI_MILESTONE_WITH_DURATION', 'MSPDI_ZERO_DURATION']);
    expect(result.issues.find((i) => i.code === 'MSPDI_MILESTONE_AMBIGUOUS')!.message).toBe('1 is a milestone at 10:00, neither the start nor the end of its working day. It is imported as a start milestone.');
  });

  it('drops the manual start of a task that has a constraint', () => {
    const tasks = task(1, `<Manual>1</Manual><ManualStart>2026-03-05T08:00:00</ManualStart>${dates('2026-03-05T08:00:00', '2026-03-05T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration><ConstraintType>4</ConstraintType><ConstraintDate>2026-03-04T08:00:00</ConstraintDate>`);
    const result = readMspdi(mspdiXml({ tasks }), uid);
    expect(result.model.activities[0]!.constraint).toEqual({ type: 'start_on_or_after', date: '2026-03-04T08:00' });
    expect(result.issues.map((i) => i.message)).toEqual(['1 is manually scheduled and already has a constraint. Its manual start 2026-03-05 08:00 was dropped.']);
  });

  it('gives elapsed durations the 24-hour calendar once and converts percentage lags', () => {
    const tasks = [
      task(1, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-04T08:00:00')}<Duration>PT48H0M0S</Duration><DurationFormat>8</DurationFormat>`),
      task(2, `${dates('2026-03-04T08:00:00', '2026-03-05T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT16H0M0S</Duration><DurationFormat>8</DurationFormat>`),
      task(3, `${dates('2026-03-06T08:00:00', '2026-03-06T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration><PredecessorLink><PredecessorUID>1</PredecessorUID><Type>1</Type><LinkLag>-25</LinkLag><LagFormat>19</LagFormat></PredecessorLink><PredecessorLink><PredecessorUID>2</PredecessorUID><Type>9</Type><LinkLag>14400</LinkLag><LagFormat>8</LagFormat></PredecessorLink>`),
    ].join('');
    const result = readMspdi(mspdiXml({ tasks }), uid);
    expect(result.model.calendars.filter((c) => c.id === 'gen:24h')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => a.calendarId)).toEqual(['gen:24h', 'gen:24h', '1']);
    expect(result.model.relationships).toEqual([
      { predecessorCode: '1', successorCode: '3', type: 'FS', lagMinutes: -720, lagElapsed: false },
      { predecessorCode: '2', successorCode: '3', type: 'FS', lagMinutes: 1440, lagElapsed: true },
    ]);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['CALENDAR_GENERATED_ELAPSED', 'ELAPSED_DURATION', 'ELAPSED_DURATION', 'ELAPSED_LAG', 'LINK_TYPE_UNKNOWN', 'PERCENT_LAG']);
    expect(result.counts[0]).toEqual({ entity: 'calendar', read: 1, accepted: 1, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 });
  });

  it('skips inactive, external and null tasks, links on summaries and links outside the file', () => {
    const tasks = [
      `<Task><UID>1</UID><ID>1</ID><Name>Phase</Name><Summary>1</Summary><OutlineLevel>1</OutlineLevel><OutlineNumber>1</OutlineNumber><PredecessorLink><PredecessorUID>2</PredecessorUID><Type>1</Type></PredecessorLink></Task>`,
      task(2, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`),
      task(3, `<Active>0</Active>${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`),
      task(4, `<ExternalTask>1</ExternalTask>${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`),
      `<Task><UID>5</UID><ID>5</ID><IsNull>1</IsNull></Task>`,
      task(6, `${dates('2026-03-03T08:00:00', '2026-03-03T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration><PredecessorLink><PredecessorUID>99</PredecessorUID><Type>1</Type><CrossProject>1</CrossProject><CrossProjectName>Other.mpp\\12</CrossProjectName></PredecessorLink>`),
    ].join('');
    const result = readMspdi(mspdiXml({ tasks }), uid);
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => a.code)).toEqual(['2', '6']);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['MSPDI_EXTERNAL_LINK', 'MSPDI_EXTERNAL_TASK', 'MSPDI_INACTIVE_TASK', 'MSPDI_SUMMARY_LINK']);
    expect(result.externalLinks).toEqual([{ target: 'Other.mpp\\12', links: 1 }]);
    expect(result.counts.find((c) => c.entity === 'activity')).toEqual({ entity: 'activity', read: 4, accepted: 2, adjusted: 0, skipped: 2 });
  });

  it('keeps outline numbers as text and reports unreadable XML', () => {
    const tasks = `<Task><UID>1</UID><ID>1</ID><Name>Phase</Name><Summary>1</Summary><OutlineLevel>1</OutlineLevel><OutlineNumber>1.10</OutlineNumber></Task>${task(2, `${dates('2026-03-02T08:00:00', '2026-03-02T17:00:00')}<Duration>PT8H0M0S</Duration>`)}`;
    expect(readMspdi(mspdiXml({ tasks }), uid).model.wbs[0]!.code).toBe('10');
    expect(readMspdi('<Project xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/project"><Tasks><Task>', uid).issues.map((i) => i.code)).toEqual(['FILE_UNREADABLE']);
  });
});
```

(The percentage lag converts against the predecessor's duration: 25 % of 2,880 elapsed minutes is 720 working minutes, `lagElapsed: false`; the unknown `Type` 9 becomes FS; `-25` with `LagFormat` 19 is a percentage, `14400` tenths with format 8 is a 24-hour lag of 1,440 minutes.)

### Task 12: Table reader

**Spec:** §5.7 (all), §5.4 (numbers, durations, dates, matching), §5.10 (xlsx archive row), §5.11 (zip guard), §3 (`scripts/make-xlsx-fixtures.ts`, D42), §7 Parser row (CSV and xlsx, zip guard). P3 full §5.7.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/import/zip-guard.ts`, `src/import/zip-guard.test.ts`, `src/import/table/csv.ts`, `src/import/table/csv.test.ts`, `src/import/table/xlsx.ts`, `src/import/table/xlsx.test.ts`, `src/import/table/auto-map.ts`, `src/import/table/auto-map.test.ts`, `src/import/table/predecessors.ts`, `src/import/table/predecessors.test.ts`, `src/import/table/constraints.ts`, `src/import/table/constraints.test.ts`, `src/import/table/normalize.ts`, `src/import/table/normalize.test.ts`, `scripts/make-xlsx-fixtures.ts`, `src/import/__fixtures__/turkish-1254.csv`, `src/import/__fixtures__/utf8.csv`, `src/import/__fixtures__/msp-table.csv`, `src/import/__fixtures__/dates-1900.xlsx`, `src/import/__fixtures__/dates-1904.xlsx`
- Modify: `package.json` (script `"fixtures:xlsx": "tsx scripts/make-xlsx-fixtures.ts"`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9's modules; Task 11's `calendars.ts`; `papaparse`, `xlsx`, `fflate`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // table/normalize.ts
  export type TableCell = { kind: 'text'; text: string } | { kind: 'number'; value: number; percent: boolean } | { kind: 'date'; serial: number };
  export interface TableSource { rows: Array<Array<TableCell | null>>; sheets: string[]; sheet: string | null; date1904: boolean }
  export interface TablePreview { headers: string[]; rows: Array<{ rowNumber: number; cells: Array<{ text: string; invalid: boolean }> }> }
  export const TABLE_ASSUMPTIONS: TableAssumptions;   // §4.1 fixed values
  export function columnLetter(index: number): string;  // 0 → 'A', 25 → 'Z', 26 → 'AA'
  export function headerRowGuess(source: TableSource): number; // 1-based: the first row with at least two non-empty text cells
  export function defaultTableMapping(source: TableSource, options: { delimiter: ',' | ';' | '\t' | null }): TableMapping;
  export function readTable(source: TableSource, mapping: TableMapping, meta: { format: 'xlsx' | 'csv'; encoding: ImportReport['encoding'] }): ReadResult;
  export function previewTable(source: TableSource, mapping: TableMapping): TablePreview; // first 10 data rows; headers without empty trailing ones
  // table/csv.ts
  export function guessDelimiter(text: string): ',' | ';' | '\t';   // the candidate giving the most rows with a constant field count
  export function csvSource(text: string, delimiter: ',' | ';' | '\t'): TableSource;
  // table/xlsx.ts
  export function xlsxSource(bytes: Uint8Array, sheet: string | null): TableSource | { refused: string };
  // zip-guard.ts
  export interface ZipLimits { totalBytes: number; entryBytes: number; ratio: number }
  export const ZIP_LIMITS: ZipLimits;                 // 200 MB, 100 MB, 100
  export function checkZip(bytes: Uint8Array, limits?: ZipLimits): boolean;
  // table/auto-map.ts
  export const FIELD_ALIASES: Record<ImportField, readonly string[]>;
  export function autoMap(headers: readonly string[]): TableMapping['columns'];
  // table/predecessors.ts
  export type PredecessorToken =
    | { ref: string; type: 'FS' | 'SS' | 'FF' | 'SF'; lag: { minutes: number; elapsed: boolean } | { percent: number } }
    | { invalid: string };
  export function parsePredecessors(cell: string, options: { decimal: ',' | '.'; hoursPerDay: number; unit: 'days' | 'hours' }): PredecessorToken[];
  // table/constraints.ts
  export function parseConstraint(text: string): ImportConstraintType | 'none' | null; // null = unsupported
  ```
- Pins: `defaultTableMapping` takes `headerRow` from `headerRowGuess`, the guessed decimal separator and date format from the data rows, `durationUnit: 'days'`, `hoursPerDay: 8`, `predecessorRef: 'taskId'` when Task ID is mapped (else `'code'`), `skipRowsWithIssues: false`, `sheet` the source's, `assumptions: TABLE_ASSUMPTIONS`. `readTable`: rows whose cells are all empty are ignored (not read, no issue); a row without a code is skipped (`TABLE_MISSING_CODE`); unreadable cells are left empty and adjust the activity, or with `skipRowsWithIssues` skip the row with `TABLE_ROW_SKIPPED` instead; date-only cells take `08:00` for starts and `17:00` for finishes; every distinct calendar name becomes `calendarFromName` (`CALENDAR_GENERATED_FROM_NAME`), a name folding to `standard` is the generated "Standard" (`CALENDAR_GENERATED_STANDARD` always, with `{n}` the hours per day); elapsed durations use `elapsedCalendar()` (`CALENDAR_GENERATED_ELAPSED` once); a WBS cell is a code or a path split on `.`, `/` or `>`, each distinct prefix one element (code = the full prefix, name = its last segment); percentage lags convert against the predecessor's duration (`PERCENT_LAG` with `{value}` in hours, e.g. `-18 h`); locations read `Line {n}` for CSV and `{sheet}!{cell}` for xlsx; generated calendars stay outside the calendar count, so a table's calendar count is all zeros. `FIELD_ALIASES` hold at least §5.7's examples plus `% complete`/`% tamamlanma`, `calendar`/`takvim`, `activity type`/`aktivite tipi`, `constraint`/`kısıt`, `constraint date`/`kısıt tarihi`, `notes`/`notlar`, `wbs`/`iş kırılımı`, `actual start`/`gerçek başlangıç`, `actual finish`/`gerçek bitiş`; headers compare with `foldForMatch`; each column maps once (first match wins). `xlsxSource` runs `checkZip` first and refuses with "This file isn't a readable Excel workbook." for a failed guard, an archive SheetJS can't open, or a missing sheet; it reads with `dense: true`, `cellFormula: false`, `cellDates: false`, `sheets` limited to the chosen sheet (the first when `null`) and `sheetRows: 20051`; number cells with a date format become `date` cells, with a `%` format `percent: true` (multiplied by 100 when read); merged cells take the top-left value.
- `scripts/make-xlsx-fixtures.ts` writes `dates-1900.xlsx` and `dates-1904.xlsx` with SheetJS (`utils.aoa_to_sheet`, `write({ bookType: 'xlsx', type: 'array' })`, `Workbook.WBProps.date1904`): sheet "Plan" with headers `Code`, `Name`, `Duration`, `Start`, `Finish`, `% Complete`, `Predecessors` and rows `X1, Mobilise, 1, 2026-03-02, 2026-03-02, 1, —`; `X2, Excavate, 5, 2026-03-03, 2026-03-09, 0.5, X1`; `X3, Pour, 3, 2026-03-10 08:00, 2026-03-12 17:00, 0, X2FS+1` (dates as date cells with format `yyyy-mm-dd` or `yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm` and serials of their date system; `% Complete` with format `0%`), then a sheet "Notes" with one text cell.
- The CSV fixtures (our own):
  - `turkish-1254.csv` — Windows-1254, `;`, CRLF:

`src/import/zip-guard.test.ts`:

```ts
import { zipSync } from 'fflate';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { checkZip } from '@/import/zip-guard';
import { bytesOf } from '@/import/testing';

describe('checkZip', () => {
  it('accepts an ordinary workbook archive', () => {
    expect(checkZip(zipSync({ 'xl/workbook.xml': bytesOf('<workbook/>') }))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('measures the real inflated bytes: understated sizes, ratios and archives cut short', () => {
    const bomb = zipSync({ 'xl/workbook.xml': bytesOf('<workbook/>'), 'xl/big.xml': bytesOf('a'.repeat(2_000_000)) });
    expect(checkZip(bomb)).toBe(false);
    const lying = zipSync({ 'xl/workbook.xml': bytesOf('a'.repeat(5000)) }, { level: 0 });
    // Claim 10 uncompressed bytes in the local header (offset 22) and the central directory entry.
    const view = new DataView(lying.buffer);
    view.setUint32(22, 10, true);
    const central = lying.findIndex((_, i) => i <= lying.length - 4 && view.getUint32(i, true) === 0x02014b50);
    view.setUint32(central + 24, 10, true);
    expect(checkZip(lying, { totalBytes: 1000, entryBytes: 1000, ratio: 100 })).toBe(false);
    expect(checkZip(zipSync({ 'xl/workbook.xml': bytesOf('<workbook/>'.repeat(50)) }).slice(0, 40))).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`src/import/table/csv.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { csvSource, guessDelimiter } from '@/import/table/csv';

const texts = (rows: Array<Array<{ kind: string; text?: string } | null>>) => rows.map((row) => row.map((cell) => (cell && 'text' in cell ? cell.text : null)));

describe('CSV', () => {
  it('guesses the delimiter that gives the most rows with a constant field count', () => {
    expect(guessDelimiter('Kod;Süre\nA1;2,5\nA2;1,25\n')).toBe(';');
    expect(guessDelimiter('Code,Duration\nA1,2.5\n')).toBe(',');
    expect(guessDelimiter('Code\tDuration\nA1\t2\n')).toBe('\t');
  });

  it('reads CRLF and a trailing blank line like LF, and drops rows of only empty cells', () => {
    const lf = 'Code;Name\nA1;Kazı\n;;\n\n';
    expect(texts(csvSource(lf.replaceAll('\n', '\r\n'), ';').rows)).toEqual(texts(csvSource(lf, ';').rows));
    expect(texts(csvSource(lf, ';').rows)).toEqual([['Code', 'Name'], ['A1', 'Kazı']]);
  });
});
```

`src/import/table/xlsx.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import workbook1900 from '@/import/__fixtures__/dates-1900.xlsx?bytes';
import workbook1904 from '@/import/__fixtures__/dates-1904.xlsx?bytes';
import { xlsxSource } from '@/import/table/xlsx';
import type { TableSource } from '@/import/table/normalize';

describe('xlsx', () => {
  it('reads the chosen sheet with date and percent cells in both date systems', () => {
    const a = xlsxSource(workbook1900, null) as TableSource;
    const b = xlsxSource(workbook1904, 'Plan') as TableSource;
    expect(a).toMatchObject({ sheets: ['Plan', 'Notes'], sheet: 'Plan', date1904: false });
    expect(b.date1904).toBe(true);
    expect(a.rows[1]![3]).toEqual({ kind: 'date', serial: 46083 });
    expect(b.rows[1]![3]).toEqual({ kind: 'date', serial: 44621 });
    expect(a.rows[2]![5]).toEqual({ kind: 'number', value: 0.5, percent: true });
    expect((xlsxSource(workbook1900, 'Notes') as TableSource).rows).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('refuses a damaged workbook with the workbook message', () => {
    expect(xlsxSource(workbook1900.slice(0, workbook1900.length / 2), null)).toEqual({ refused: "This file isn't a readable Excel workbook." });
    expect(xlsxSource(workbook1900, 'Missing')).toEqual({ refused: "This file isn't a readable Excel workbook." });
  });
});
```

(Serial 46083 is 2026-03-02 in the 1900 system and 44621 in the 1904 system.)

`src/import/table/predecessors.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { parsePredecessors } from '@/import/table/predecessors';

const options = { decimal: '.', hoursPerDay: 8, unit: 'days' } as const;

describe('parsePredecessors', () => {
  it('reads refs with types and spaced, word and percentage lags, and marks unreadable tokens', () => {
    expect(parsePredecessors('A100FS+2d; A120SS', options)).toEqual([
      { ref: 'A100', type: 'FS', lag: { minutes: 960, elapsed: false } },
      { ref: 'A120', type: 'SS', lag: { minutes: 0, elapsed: false } },
    ]);
    expect(parsePredecessors('3FS+2 days, 12SS-1 hafta', options)).toEqual([
      { ref: '3', type: 'FS', lag: { minutes: 960, elapsed: false } },
      { ref: '12', type: 'SS', lag: { minutes: -2400, elapsed: false } },
    ]);
    expect(parsePredecessors('7FS-25%', options)).toEqual([{ ref: '7', type: 'FS', lag: { percent: -25 } }]);
    expect(parsePredecessors('T110SS+1 g', { ...options, decimal: ',' })).toEqual([{ ref: 'T110', type: 'SS', lag: { minutes: 480, elapsed: false } }]);
    expect(parsePredecessors('A1 XX+?', options)).toEqual([{ invalid: 'A1 XX+?' }]);
    expect(parsePredecessors('', options)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`src/import/table/normalize.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import msp from '@/import/__fixtures__/msp-table.csv?bytes';
import turkish from '@/import/__fixtures__/turkish-1254.csv?bytes';
import utf8 from '@/import/__fixtures__/utf8.csv?bytes';
import { decodeText, detectEncoding } from '@/import/encoding';
import { csvSource, guessDelimiter } from '@/import/table/csv';
import { defaultTableMapping, previewTable, readTable } from '@/import/table/normalize';

function load(bytes: Uint8Array) {
  const encoding = detectEncoding(bytes);
  const text = decodeText(bytes, encoding.name, encoding.bomLength);
  const delimiter = guessDelimiter(text);
  const source = csvSource(text, delimiter);
  const mapping = defaultTableMapping(source, { delimiter });
  const meta = { format: 'csv' as const, encoding: { name: encoding.name, detectedBy: encoding.detectedBy } };
  return { source, mapping, read: () => readTable(source, mapping, meta), meta };
}
const codes = (issues: { code: string }[]) => issues.map((i) => i.code).sort();

describe('readTable', () => {
  it('reads the Windows-1254 CSV with decimal commas, Turkish headers and calendar names', () => {
    const { mapping, read } = load(turkish);
    expect(mapping).toMatchObject({ delimiter: ';', decimalSeparator: ',', dateFormat: 'DD.MM.YYYY', headerRow: 1, predecessorRef: 'code' });
    const result = read();
    expect(result.counts).toEqual([
      { entity: 'calendar', read: 0, accepted: 0, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'wbs', read: 0, accepted: 0, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'activity', read: 5, accepted: 4, adjusted: 1, skipped: 1 },
      { entity: 'relationship', read: 3, accepted: 3, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
    ]);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['CALENDAR_GENERATED_FROM_NAME', 'CALENDAR_GENERATED_STANDARD', 'TABLE_BAD_NUMBER', 'TABLE_MISSING_CODE']);
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => [a.code, a.name, a.originalDuration, a.calendarId, a.percent.duration])).toEqual([
      ['T100', 'Şantiye kurulumu', 1200, 'gen:name:gunduz', 100],
      ['T110', 'Kazı işleri', 2400, 'gen:name:gunduz', 40],
      ['T120', 'Temel betonu', 1440, 'gen:standard', 0],
      ['T130', 'İskele', null, 'gen:standard', null],
    ]);
    expect(result.model.activities[0]!.planned).toEqual({ start: '2026-03-02T08:00', finish: '2026-03-04T17:00' });
    expect(result.model.relationships[1]).toEqual({ predecessorCode: 'T110', successorCode: 'T120', type: 'SS', lagMinutes: 480, lagElapsed: false });
    expect(result.model.calendars.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['gen:standard', 'gen:name:gunduz']);
  });

  it('reads the UTF-8 CSV with WBS paths, types, constraints and repeated links', () => {
    const result = load(utf8).read();
    expect(result.counts.slice(1)).toEqual([
      { entity: 'wbs', read: 3, accepted: 3, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'activity', read: 5, accepted: 5, adjusted: 1, skipped: 0 },
      { entity: 'relationship', read: 6, accepted: 4, adjusted: 0, skipped: 2 },
    ]);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['CALENDAR_GENERATED_STANDARD', 'CONSTRAINT_UNSUPPORTED', 'LINK_DUPLICATE', 'LINK_SELF']);
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => [a.code, a.type, a.constraint?.type ?? null, a.wbsId])).toEqual([
      ['M1', 'start_milestone', null, '1'], ['A1', 'task', 'start_on_or_after', '1.1'], ['A2', 'task', 'finish_on_or_before', '1.2'],
      ['A3', 'task', null, '1.2'], ['M2', 'finish_milestone', null, '1'],
    ]);
    expect(result.model.wbs.map((w) => [w.id, w.code, w.name, w.parentId])).toEqual([['1', '1', '1', null], ['1.1', '1.1', '1', '1'], ['1.2', '1.2', '2', '1']]);
  });

  it('reads the Microsoft Project–style table with Task IDs, unit durations and weekday dates', () => {
    const { mapping, read } = load(msp);
    expect(mapping).toMatchObject({ delimiter: ',', dateFormat: 'MM/DD/YYYY', predecessorRef: 'taskId' });
    expect(mapping.columns.taskId).toEqual({ index: 0, header: 'ID' });
    const result = read();
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => [a.code, a.originalDuration, a.calendarId])).toEqual([
      ['101', 480, 'gen:standard'], ['102', 2400, 'gen:standard'], ['103', 4800, 'gen:standard'], ['104', 4320, 'gen:24h'], ['105', 240, 'gen:standard'],
    ]);
    expect(result.model.activities[0]!.planned).toEqual({ start: '2026-03-02T08:00', finish: '2026-03-02T17:00' });
    expect(result.model.relationships.map((r) => [r.predecessorCode, r.successorCode, r.type, r.lagMinutes])).toEqual([
      ['101', '102', 'FS', 0], ['102', '103', 'FS', 960], ['103', '104', 'FS', 0], ['104', '105', 'SS', -1080],
    ]);
    expect(codes(result.issues)).toEqual(['CALENDAR_GENERATED_ELAPSED', 'CALENDAR_GENERATED_STANDARD', 'ELAPSED_DURATION', 'PERCENT_LAG', 'TABLE_ESTIMATED_DURATION']);
    expect(result.issues.find((i) => i.code === 'PERCENT_LAG')!.message).toBe('The lag on 104 → 105 is a percentage. It was converted to -18 h.');
  });

  it('skips rows with problems when asked', () => {
    const { source, mapping, meta } = load(turkish);
    const result = readTable(source, { ...mapping, skipRowsWithIssues: true }, meta);
    expect(result.model.activities.map((a) => a.code)).toEqual(['T100', 'T110', 'T120']);
    expect(result.issues.find((i) => i.code === 'TABLE_ROW_SKIPPED')).toMatchObject({ message: 'This row was skipped because “abc” in % Tamamlanma couldn\'t be read.', location: 'Line 5' });
    expect(codes(result.issues)).not.toContain('TABLE_BAD_NUMBER');
  });

  it('previews the first 10 data rows with invalid cells marked and no empty trailing headers', () => {
    const { source, mapping } = load(turkish);
    const preview = previewTable(source, mapping);
    expect(preview.headers).toEqual(['Aktivite Kodu', 'Aktivite Adı', 'Süre', 'Başlangıç', 'Bitiş', 'Öncüller', '% Tamamlanma', 'Takvim']);
    expect(preview.rows.map((r) => r.rowNumber)).toEqual([2, 3, 4, 5, 6]);
    expect(preview.rows[3]!.cells[6]).toEqual({ text: 'abc', invalid: true });
    const wide = csvSource('Kod;Ad;;\nA1;Bir;;\n', ';');
    expect(previewTable(wide, defaultTableMapping(wide, { delimiter: ';' })).headers).toEqual(['Kod', 'Ad']);
  });
});
```
