### Task 13: `analyze`, report and `pack`

**Spec:** §5.1 (steps 2, 4, 6), §5.8 (reader entry point, compatibility rule), §5.9 (all), §5.10 (limits), §5.12 (status-date, format and limit codes), §2.2 S4, S5, §7 Parser row (checks, generated calendars, CSV export escaping, 200-character texts and the stored report under 200 KB, `pack` round trip, goldens and count rules, damaged files, determinism, limits, budget). P3 full §7 Parser row (how damaged copies are made).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/import/index.ts`, `src/import/index.test.ts`, `src/import/checks.ts`, `src/import/checks.test.ts`, `src/import/report.ts`, `src/import/report.test.ts`, `src/import/csv-export.ts`, `src/import/csv-export.test.ts`, `src/import/pack.ts`, `src/import/pack.test.ts`, `src/import/goldens.test.ts`, `src/import/damaged.test.ts`, `src/import/limits.test.ts`, `src/import/budget.test.ts`, `src/import/__fixtures__/cases.ts`, `src/import/__fixtures__/*.report.json` and `*.model.json` (one pair per case, generated), `scripts/update-import-goldens.ts`
- Modify: `src/import/testing.ts` (`generateXer`, `generateCsv`), `package.json` (script `"goldens": "tsx scripts/update-import-goldens.ts"`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 9–12.
- Produces (`src/import/index.ts`, the parser's public entry points; nothing throws — any failure is a report or a refusal):
  ```ts
  export type InspectOutcome =
    | { kind: 'refused'; message: string; fileName: string; size: number; parserVersion: string }
    | { kind: 'schedule'; format: 'xer' | 'mspdi'; fileName: string; size: number; sha256: string; formatFromContent: boolean;
        encoding: ImportReport['encoding']; projects: XerProjectInfo[]; codeFields: string[]; firstActivities: string[]; mapping: ImportMapping }
    | { kind: 'table'; format: 'xlsx' | 'csv'; fileName: string; size: number; sha256: string; formatFromContent: boolean;
        encoding: ImportReport['encoding']; sheets: string[]; mapping: ImportMapping; preview: TablePreview };
  export type AnalyzeOutcome = { kind: 'analyzed'; model: ImportModel; report: ImportReport } | { kind: 'refused'; message: string };
  export interface PackResult { stored: Uint8Array; report: ImportReport }
  export function inspect(bytes: Uint8Array, fileName: string): Promise<InspectOutcome>;
  export function preview(bytes: Uint8Array, fileName: string, table: TableMapping, encoding: TextEncodingName | null): TablePreview;
  export function analyze(bytes: Uint8Array, fileName: string, mapping: ImportMapping, options?: { statusDate?: ISODate | null }): Promise<AnalyzeOutcome>;
  export { pack } from './pack';
  export type { TablePreview } from './table/normalize';
  ```
  `inspect`: XER → `projects` from `listXerProjects` and `mapping.sourceProjectId` = the first project P6 flagged for export, else the first; MSPDI → `codeFields` from `mspdiCodeFields`, `activityCodeField: 'UID'`; `firstActivities` the first five activity names as decoded (XER, CSV; `[]` otherwise); tables → `defaultTableMapping` and its preview. `analyze` (the spec's reader for P4, §5.8 3) decodes with `mapping.encoding`, reads, enforces §5.10's counts (`FILE_TOO_MANY_*`; 20,000 activities, 40,000 relationships, 10,000 WBS elements, 200 calendars; 20,000 table data rows, 200 columns), runs the checks against `options.statusDate` when given (report `statusDate.source: 'user'`), else the file's, and builds the report; `FILE_EMPTY` for an empty file, `FILE_NO_ACTIVITIES` when no activity is accepted, `FILE_UNREADABLE` when a reader can't read through; an xlsx that fails `xlsxSource` → `{ kind: 'refused' }` with its message.
- Produces (`checks.ts`): `runChecks(model: ImportModel, readIssues: readonly ImportIssue[], context: { statusDate: ISODate | null; statusSource: ImportReport['statusDate']['source']; stated: ImportReport['sourceProject']['stated']; dateRange: ImportReport['dateRange'] }): { checks: ImportReport['checks']; issues: ImportIssue[] }` — all nine checks in the §4.1 order with `findings`, zeros included. `actual_after_status_date`: actual start or finish dates after the status date (`ACTUAL_AFTER_STATUS_DATE`, `{start or finish}` = `start` or `finish`); `progress_without_actual_start`: in progress, or a duration or physical % above 0, without an actual start; `complete_without_actual_finish`: completed, or 100 %, without an actual finish; `status_date_fallback`: 1 when `statusSource` is `earliest-start` (`STATUS_DATE_FALLBACK`); `ambiguous_milestones`, `percent_lags`, `links_on_summaries`, `external_links`: the number of reader issues `MSPDI_MILESTONE_AMBIGUOUS`, `PERCENT_LAG`, `MSPDI_SUMMARY_LINK`, `XER_CROSS_PROJECT_LINK` + `MSPDI_EXTERNAL_LINK` (no new issues); `project_dates_differ`: 1 with `PROJECT_DATES_DIFFER` when a stated date exists and differs from `dateRange`.
- Produces (`report.ts`): `buildReport(read: ReadResult, context: { format: ImportFormat; fileName: string; size: number; sha256: string; formatFromContent: boolean; extension: string; encoding: ImportReport['encoding']; table: TableMapping | null; activityCodeField: string | null; statusDate: ISODate | null; durationMs: number }): ImportReport` — issues: `FORMAT_FROM_CONTENT` (`{format}` = "Primavera P6 XER", "Microsoft Project XML", "Excel workbook" or "CSV file"), encoding issues, reader issues, `STATUS_DATE_MISSING` (an XER without `last_recalc_date`), `STATUS_TIME_KEPT` (a file status time other than 00:00), the checks' issues; ordered errors, then warnings, then notes, each kept in arrival order; the first 5,000 kept, `issueTotals` exact. `dateRange`: the accepted activities' earliest `actual.start ?? planned.start` and latest `actual.finish ?? planned.finish`, date parts. `breakdown`, `outcomes` (catalog order, present elements only) and `units` from the read; `table` is the mapping without `columns`.
- Produces (`csv-export.ts`): `reportToCsv(report: ImportReport): string` — header `severity,code,message,location,original,used`, one row per issue, every cell quoted (`"` doubled), a cell starting with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, tab, CR or their full-width forms `＝ ＋ － ＠` gets a leading `'`; `\r\n` row ends; `reportFileName(scheduleName: string, extension: 'csv' | 'json'): string` → `${scheduleName} - import report.${extension}`.
- Produces (`pack.ts`): `pack(bytes: Uint8Array, fileName: string, report: ImportReport): Promise<PackResult>` — rejects a report with errors; `stored` is `gzipSync(bytes, { mtime: 0 })` for xer, mspdi and csv and the bytes themselves for xlsx; the stored report keeps the first 500 issues, then drops notes and then warnings from the end until `new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(report)).length <= 204800`.
- Produces (`__fixtures__/cases.ts`): `export interface GoldenCase { name: string; file: string; mapping: ImportMapping }` and `GOLDEN_CASES` — `two-projects.NTH-A` and `two-projects.NTH-B` (`two-projects.xer`, `encoding: 'utf-8'`, `sourceProjectId` 101 / 102), `project` (`project.xml`, `activityCodeField: 'UID'`), `turkish-1254`, `utf8`, `msp-table` (their `defaultTableMapping`, written out in full), `dates-1900` and `dates-1904` (sheet `Plan`, header row 1, `DD.MM.YYYY`, `.`); goldens are `<name>.report.json` (without `durationMs` and `parserVersion`) and `<name>.model.json`. `scripts/update-import-goldens.ts` runs `analyze` over every case and writes them.
- Produces (`testing.ts`): `generateXer(options: { activities: number; links: number; wbs?: number; calendars?: number; missingCalendars?: boolean; padToBytes?: number }): Uint8Array` (one project, a chain of activities plus extra FS links; `missingCalendars` gives every activity an unknown `clndr_id`, so each gets `CALENDAR_MISSING`; `padToBytes` pads with `TASKMEMO` rows) and `generateCsv(options: { rows: number; columns?: number }): Uint8Array`.

`src/import/report.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import xer from '@/import/__fixtures__/two-projects.xer?bytes';
import msp from '@/import/__fixtures__/msp-table.csv?bytes';
import { analyze, inspect } from '@/import/index';
import { generateXer } from '@/import/testing';

const analyzed = async (bytes: Uint8Array, name: string) => {
  const inspected = await inspect(bytes, name);
  if (inspected.kind === 'refused') throw new Error(inspected.message);
  const outcome = await analyze(bytes, name, inspected.mapping);
  if (outcome.kind !== 'analyzed') throw new Error(outcome.message);
  return outcome.report;
};

describe('the import report', () => {
  it('orders issues errors, warnings, notes and keeps the totals exact while capping the dry run at 5,000', async () => {
    const report = await analyzed(xer, 'north.xer');
    const severities = report.issues.map((i) => i.severity);
    expect(severities).toEqual([...severities].sort((a, b) => ['error', 'warning', 'info'].indexOf(a) - ['error', 'warning', 'info'].indexOf(b)));
    expect(report.issueTotals).toEqual({ error: 0, warning: 4, info: 3 });
    const many = await analyzed(generateXer({ activities: 6000, links: 0, missingCalendars: true }), 'many.xer');
    expect(many.issues).toHaveLength(5000);
    expect(many.issueTotals.info + many.issueTotals.warning).toBeGreaterThan(5000);
  });

  it('carries the summary, date range, units, outcomes and the file', async () => {
    const report = await analyzed(xer, 'north.xer');
    expect(report).toMatchObject({
      schemaVersion: 1, format: 'xer', formatVersion: '20.12', formatFromContent: false,
      file: { name: 'north.xer', size: xer.length },
      statusDate: { date: '2026-10-15', time: '08:00', source: 'file' },
      dateRange: { start: '2026-09-01', finish: '2026-12-18' },
      breakdown: { activitiesStarted: 4, activitiesCompleted: 2, constraints: 1, calendarExceptions: 2 },
    });
    expect(report.outcomes.map((o) => o.element)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['Activities', 'Resources', 'Scheduling settings']));
    expect(report.issues.map((i) => i.code)).toContain('STATUS_TIME_KEPT');
  });

  it('notes the generated calendars and the table options', async () => {
    const report = await analyzed(msp, 'tasks.csv');
    expect(report.issues.find((i) => i.code === 'CALENDAR_GENERATED_STANDARD')!.message).toBe('Calendar Standard was added as the default calendar: Monday to Friday from 08:00, 8 hours a day.');
    expect(report.table).toMatchObject({ delimiter: ',', dateFormat: 'MM/DD/YYYY', predecessorRef: 'taskId', assumptions: { weekUnitDays: 5 } });
    expect(report.table).not.toHaveProperty('columns');
    expect(report.counts[0]).toEqual({ entity: 'calendar', read: 0, accepted: 0, adjusted: 0, skipped: 0 });
  });
});
```

(`activitiesStarted` counts in-progress and completed activities: A1000, A1010, A1020, A1050; `calendarExceptions` counts the exceptions of the accepted calendars, the inherited one included.)

`src/import/pack.test.ts`:

```ts
import { gunzipSync } from 'fflate';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import xer from '@/import/__fixtures__/two-projects.xer?bytes';
import workbook from '@/import/__fixtures__/dates-1900.xlsx?bytes';
import { analyze, inspect, pack } from '@/import/index';
import type { ImportIssue, ImportReport } from '@/data/types';

async function reportOf(bytes: Uint8Array, name: string): Promise<ImportReport> {
  const inspected = await inspect(bytes, name);
  if (inspected.kind === 'refused') throw new Error(inspected.message);
  const outcome = await analyze(bytes, name, inspected.mapping);
  if (outcome.kind !== 'analyzed') throw new Error(outcome.message);
  return outcome.report;
}

describe('pack', () => {
  it('gzips text formats and keeps an xlsx as it is', async () => {
    const packed = await pack(xer, 'north.xer', await reportOf(xer, 'north.xer'));
    expect(gunzipSync(packed.stored)).toEqual(xer);
    expect((await pack(workbook, 'plan.xlsx', await reportOf(workbook, 'plan.xlsx'))).stored).toEqual(workbook);
  });

  it('keeps 500 issues, then drops notes and warnings to stay within 200 KB', async () => {
    const report = await reportOf(xer, 'north.xer');
    const long = (severity: 'warning' | 'info', i: number): ImportIssue => ({
      severity, code: severity === 'info' ? 'TEXT_TRUNCATED' : 'TABLE_BAD_NUMBER',
      message: `${'Şantiye işçiliği ğüşıöç '.repeat(8).slice(0, 199)}…`, location: `Line ${i}`, original: 'İ'.repeat(200), used: 'ş'.repeat(200),
    });
    const big = { ...report, issues: [...Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => long('warning', i)), ...Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => long('info', i))] };
    const stored = (await pack(xer, 'north.xer', big)).report;
    expect(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(stored)).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(204800);
    expect(stored.issues.length).toBeLessThan(500);
    expect(stored.issues.slice(0, 10).every((i) => i.severity === 'warning')).toBe(true);
    expect(stored.issueTotals).toEqual(big.issueTotals);
  });

  it('refuses a report with errors', async () => {
    const report = await reportOf(xer, 'north.xer');
    const withError = { ...report, issues: [{ severity: 'error', code: 'FILE_EMPTY', message: 'The file is empty.', location: null, original: null, used: null } as const] };
    await expect(pack(xer, 'north.xer', withError)).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});
```

`src/import/goldens.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GOLDEN_CASES } from '@/import/__fixtures__/cases';
import { analyze } from '@/import/index';
import type { ImportReport } from '@/data/types';

const files = import.meta.glob<Uint8Array>('./__fixtures__/*.{xer,xml,csv,xlsx}', { query: '?bytes', import: 'default', eager: true });
const goldens = import.meta.glob<unknown>('./__fixtures__/*.json', { import: 'default', eager: true });
const bytesOf = (file: string) => files[`./__fixtures__/${file}`]!;
/** The report without the two fields goldens leave out (S4). */
function stable(report: ImportReport): Partial<ImportReport> {
  const copy: Partial<ImportReport> = { ...report };
  delete copy.durationMs;
  delete copy.parserVersion;
  return copy;
}
async function run(name: string) {
  const c = GOLDEN_CASES.find((x) => x.name === name)!;
  const outcome = await analyze(bytesOf(c.file), c.file, c.mapping);
  if (outcome.kind !== 'analyzed') throw new Error(outcome.message);
  return outcome;
}

describe('golden fixtures (S4)', () => {
  it.each(GOLDEN_CASES.map((c) => c.name))('%s matches its golden report and model', async (name) => {
    const { model, report } = await run(name);
    expect(stable(report)).toEqual(goldens[`./__fixtures__/${name}.report.json`]);
    expect(model).toEqual(goldens[`./__fixtures__/${name}.model.json`]);
  });

  it('keeps the count rules for every case', async () => {
    for (const c of GOLDEN_CASES) {
      const { report } = await run(c.name);
      const changed = report.issues.filter((i) => i.severity !== 'error').length;
      for (const count of report.counts) {
        expect(count.read, `${c.name} ${count.entity}`).toBe(count.accepted + count.skipped);
        expect(count.adjusted).toBeLessThanOrEqual(count.accepted);
      }
      expect(report.counts.reduce((sum, n) => sum + n.adjusted + n.skipped, 0)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(changed);
    }
  });

  it('has no error in any golden, so the compatibility rule has a baseline', async () => {
    for (const c of GOLDEN_CASES) expect((await run(c.name)).report.issueTotals.error, c.name).toBe(0);
  });

  it('is deterministic, and gives one model per chosen XER project', async () => {
    expect((await run('project')).model).toEqual((await run('project')).model);
    const [a, b] = [await run('two-projects.NTH-A'), await run('two-projects.NTH-B')];
    expect([a.model.project.code, b.model.project.code]).toEqual(['NTH-A', 'NTH-B']);
    expect((await run('dates-1900')).model).toEqual((await run('dates-1904')).model);
  });
});
```

`src/import/damaged.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { GOLDEN_CASES } from '@/import/__fixtures__/cases';
import { analyze, inspect } from '@/import/index';

const files = import.meta.glob<Uint8Array>('./__fixtures__/*.{xer,xml,csv,xlsx}', { query: '?bytes', import: 'default', eager: true });
const WORKBOOK = "This file isn't a readable Excel workbook.";

/** Bytes that must stay intact so the copy is still detected as its format (P3 full §7). */
function protectedPrefix(bytes: Uint8Array, file: string): number {
  const text = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(0, 4096));
  if (file.endsWith('.xer')) return text.indexOf('\n') + 1;
  if (file.endsWith('.xml')) return text.indexOf('>', text.indexOf('<Project')) + 1;
  if (file.endsWith('.xlsx')) return 30 + new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset).getUint16(26, true);
  return (bytes[0] === 0xef ? 3 : 0) + 16;
}
function* damaged(bytes: Uint8Array, start: number) {
  for (const share of [0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.9]) yield bytes.slice(0, Math.max(start, Math.floor(bytes.length * share)));
  let seed = 7;
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i += 1) {
    seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) % 2 ** 31;
    const copy = bytes.slice();
    const at = start + (seed % (bytes.length - start));
    copy[at] = copy[at]! ^ 0xff;
    yield copy;
  }
}

describe('damaged files (S4)', () => {
  it.each([...new Set(GOLDEN_CASES.map((c) => c.file))])('%s gives a report or the workbook message for every damaged copy', async (file) => {
    const bytes = files[`./__fixtures__/${file}`]!;
    const mapping = GOLDEN_CASES.find((c) => c.file === file)!.mapping;
    for (const copy of damaged(bytes, protectedPrefix(bytes, file))) {
      const inspected = await inspect(copy, file);
      const outcome = await analyze(copy, file, mapping);
      if (outcome.kind === 'refused' || inspected.kind === 'refused') {
        expect(file.endsWith('.xlsx')).toBe(true);
        expect(outcome.kind === 'refused' ? outcome.message : WORKBOOK).toBe(WORKBOOK);
      } else {
        expect(outcome.report.counts).toHaveLength(4);
      }
    }
  });

  it('always refuses an xlsx cut in half', async () => {
    for (const file of ['dates-1900.xlsx', 'dates-1904.xlsx']) {
      const bytes = files[`./__fixtures__/${file}`]!;
      const half = bytes.slice(0, bytes.length / 2);
      expect(await analyze(half, file, GOLDEN_CASES.find((c) => c.file === file)!.mapping)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: WORKBOOK });
    }
  });
});
```

`src/import/limits.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { analyze, inspect } from '@/import/index';
import { generateCsv, generateXer } from '@/import/testing';

async function errors(bytes: Uint8Array, name: string) {
  const inspected = await inspect(bytes, name);
  if (inspected.kind === 'refused') throw new Error(inspected.message);
  const outcome = await analyze(bytes, name, inspected.mapping);
  if (outcome.kind !== 'analyzed') throw new Error(outcome.message);
  return outcome.report.issues.filter((i) => i.severity === 'error').map((i) => [i.code, i.message]);
}

describe('limits (§5.10)', () => {
  it.each([
    [() => generateXer({ activities: 20001, links: 0 }), 'a.xer', 'FILE_TOO_MANY_ACTIVITIES', 'The file has 20,001 activities. The limit is 20,000.'],
    [() => generateXer({ activities: 2, links: 40001 }), 'b.xer', 'FILE_TOO_MANY_RELATIONSHIPS', 'The file has 40,001 relationships. The limit is 40,000.'],
    [() => generateXer({ activities: 2, links: 0, wbs: 10001 }), 'c.xer', 'FILE_TOO_MANY_WBS', 'The file has 10,001 WBS elements. The limit is 10,000.'],
    [() => generateXer({ activities: 2, links: 0, calendars: 201 }), 'd.xer', 'FILE_TOO_MANY_CALENDARS', 'The file has 201 calendars. The limit is 200.'],
    [() => generateCsv({ rows: 20001 }), 'e.csv', 'FILE_TOO_MANY_ROWS', 'The sheet has more than 20,000 rows.'],
    [() => generateCsv({ rows: 2, columns: 201 }), 'f.csv', 'FILE_TOO_MANY_COLUMNS', 'The sheet has more than 200 columns.'],
  ])('%#: one past the limit gives its error', async (make, name, code, message) => {
    expect(await errors(make(), name)).toEqual([[code, message]]);
  });
});
```

(`generateXer({ calendars })` gives every activity the first calendar and lets the others be used by nothing but the project's scope — they still count toward the limit, which applies to the calendars the file defines; `generateXer({ links: 40001 })` repeats links between the two activities with distinct `task_pred_id`s, so the count is read before duplicates are dropped.)

### Task 14: Worker, protocol, client and `useImportParser`

**Spec:** §5.3 (all), §3 (layer rules for `client.ts` and hooks), §4.4 (`useImportParser`), §7 Worker client row, §8 V6.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/import/protocol.ts`, `src/import/in-thread.ts`, `src/import/worker.ts`, `src/import/client.ts`, `src/import/client.test.ts`, `src/hooks/useImportParser.ts`, `src/hooks/useImportParser.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 13's `inspect`, `preview`, `analyze`, `pack`.
- Produces (`protocol.ts`, types plus no runtime code beyond constants):
  ```ts
  export type WorkerRequest =
    | { id: number; type: 'inspect'; fileName: string; bytes: ArrayBuffer }
    | { id: number; type: 'preview'; table: TableMapping; encoding: TextEncodingName | null }
    | { id: number; type: 'analyze'; mapping: ImportMapping; statusDate: ISODate | null }
    | { id: number; type: 'pack'; report: ImportReport }
    | { id: number; type: 'cancel' };
  export type WorkerResponse = { id: number; ok: true; result: unknown } | { id: number; ok: false; issue: { message: string } };
  export type { InspectOutcome, AnalyzeOutcome, PackResult, TablePreview } from './index';
  export interface ParserSession {                               // holds the file's bytes between requests
    inspect(fileName: string, bytes: Uint8Array): Promise<InspectOutcome>;
    preview(table: TableMapping, encoding: TextEncodingName | null): Promise<TablePreview>;
    analyze(mapping: ImportMapping, statusDate: ISODate | null): Promise<AnalyzeOutcome>;
    pack(report: ImportReport): Promise<PackResult>;
  }
  ```
- Produces (`in-thread.ts`): `createParserSession(): ParserSession` (the one implementation; a request without an inspected file rejects with `{ message: 'no_file' }`). `worker.ts`: `self.onmessage` routes requests to one session and posts `WorkerResponse`s, transferring `pack`'s bytes.
- Produces (`client.ts`, statically importing only `./protocol` and types):
  ```ts
  export const WATCHDOG_MS = 30_000;
  export const TOO_SLOW = 'This file took too long to read. Try a smaller export.';
  export class ImportClientError extends Error { readonly code: 'timeout' | 'cancelled' | 'no_file' | 'failed' }
  export interface WorkerLike { postMessage(message: unknown, transfer?: Transferable[]): void; terminate(): void; onmessage: ((event: { data: unknown }) => void) | null }
  export interface ImportClient {
    inspect(file: File): Promise<InspectOutcome>;
    preview(table: TableMapping, encoding: TextEncodingName | null): Promise<TablePreview>;
    analyze(mapping: ImportMapping, options?: { statusDate?: ISODate | null }): Promise<AnalyzeOutcome>;
    pack(report: ImportReport): Promise<{ stored: Blob; report: ImportReport }>;
    cancel(): void;     // rejects every pending request with 'cancelled' and terminates the worker
    dispose(): void;    // cancel, and no further requests
  }
  export function createImportClient(options?: { createWorker?: () => WorkerLike | null; timeoutMs?: number }): ImportClient;
  ```
  The default `createWorker` returns `new Worker(new URL('./worker.ts', import.meta.url), { type: 'module' })`, or `null` where `Worker` is missing; with `null` the client runs `createParserSession()` from `await import('./in-thread')` in the calling thread. Each request gets the next id; a request open longer than `timeoutMs` terminates the worker and rejects with code `timeout` and message `TOO_SLOW`; after a termination, every request but `inspect` rejects with `no_file` until `inspect` starts a new worker. `pack`'s `stored` is a `Blob` of the bytes, typed `application/gzip` for xer, mspdi and csv and `application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet` for xlsx.
- Produces (`src/hooks/useImportParser.ts`): `ImportClientFactoryContext: Context<() => ImportClient>` (default `() => createImportClient()`); `useImportParser(): ImportClient` — a stable object whose methods create the client on first use (no worker during render) and which `dispose()`s it on unmount; re-exports the types `ImportClient`, `InspectOutcome`, `AnalyzeOutcome`, `TablePreview` for components (S7: components never import `src/import`).

`src/import/client.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import xer from '@/import/__fixtures__/two-projects.xer?bytes';
import { createImportClient, TOO_SLOW, type WorkerLike } from '@/import/client';

function fakeWorker(answer: (request: { id: number; type: string }) => unknown | undefined) {
  const posted: Array<{ id: number; type: string }> = [];
  const worker: WorkerLike & { terminated: boolean } = {
    terminated: false,
    onmessage: null,
    postMessage(message) {
      const request = message as { id: number; type: string };
      posted.push(request);
      const result = answer(request);
      if (result !== undefined) queueMicrotask(() => worker.onmessage?.({ data: { id: request.id, ok: true, result } }));
    },
    terminate() { worker.terminated = true; },
  };
  return { worker, posted };
}
const file = new File([xer], 'north.xer');

describe('import client', () => {
  afterEach(() => vi.useRealTimers());

  it('matches answers to requests by id', async () => {
    const { worker, posted } = fakeWorker((r) => ({ kind: 'refused', message: `answer ${r.id}`, fileName: 'x', size: 1 }));
    const client = createImportClient({ createWorker: () => worker });
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([client.inspect(file), client.inspect(file)]);
    expect([a, b].map((o) => (o.kind === 'refused' ? o.message : ''))).toEqual(posted.map((r) => `answer ${r.id}`));
    expect(new Set(posted.map((r) => r.id)).size).toBe(2);
  });

  it('terminates the worker and reports a request that runs past the watchdog', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    const { worker } = fakeWorker(() => undefined);
    const client = createImportClient({ createWorker: () => worker, timeoutMs: 1000 });
    const pending = client.inspect(file);
    const check = expect(pending).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'timeout', message: TOO_SLOW });
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1001);
    await check;
    expect(worker.terminated).toBe(true);
    await expect(client.analyze({ schemaVersion: 1, format: 'xer', encoding: 'utf-8', sourceProjectId: '101', activityCodeField: null, table: null })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'no_file' });
  });

  it('cancels pending requests and terminates the worker', async () => {
    const { worker } = fakeWorker(() => undefined);
    const client = createImportClient({ createWorker: () => worker });
    const pending = client.inspect(file);
    client.cancel();
    await expect(pending).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'cancelled' });
    expect(worker.terminated).toBe(true);
  });

  it('runs in the calling thread where Worker is missing', async () => {
    const client = createImportClient({ createWorker: () => null });
    const inspected = await client.inspect(file);
    expect(inspected).toMatchObject({ kind: 'schedule', format: 'xer' });
    if (inspected.kind !== 'schedule') return;
    const outcome = await client.analyze(inspected.mapping);
    expect(outcome.kind).toBe('analyzed');
    if (outcome.kind !== 'analyzed') return;
    const packed = await client.pack(outcome.report);
    expect(packed.stored.type).toBe('application/gzip');
  });
});
```

`src/hooks/useImportParser.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { renderHook } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ImportClientFactoryContext, useImportParser, type ImportClient } from '@/hooks/useImportParser';

describe('useImportParser', () => {
  it('creates its client on first use, not during render, and disposes it on unmount', async () => {
    const client = { inspect: vi.fn(async () => ({ kind: 'refused', message: 'x', fileName: 'a', size: 1 })), dispose: vi.fn() } as unknown as ImportClient;
    const factory = vi.fn(() => client);
    const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => <ImportClientFactoryContext value={factory}>{children}</ImportClientFactoryContext>;
    const { result, rerender, unmount } = renderHook(() => useImportParser(), { wrapper });
    rerender();
    expect(factory).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await result.current.inspect(new File(['x'], 'a.xer'));
    await result.current.inspect(new File(['x'], 'b.xer'));
    expect(factory).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    unmount();
    expect(client.dispose).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});
```

  ```ts
  getImportMapping(scheduleId: ID): Promise<ImportMapping | null>; // same
  ```

- Mock: `MockDb.schedules` holds `MockSchedule = Omit<Schedule, 'hasImportReport' | 'hasImportMapping'> & { sourceProjectCode: string | null; importReport: ImportReport | null; importMapping: ImportMapping | null }`, `hasImport…` derived when read; the stored report keeps its first 50 issues; the original `File`s live in a `Map<ID, File>` owned by the `MockRepository` instance (not persisted; `deleteSchedules` and `deleteProject` drop entries). `importSchedule` with a `report`: `sourceFormat = report.format`, `activityCount`, `statusDate` (`null` → the project's), `sourceProjectCode`, mapping and report stored. `getScheduleSourceFile`: blank → `validation` "This schedule has no original file."; an import missing from the map → `validation` "The original file isn't available in demo mode after a reload."; unknown → `not_found` "Schedule not found".
- Supabase: `importSchedule` with `stored` uploads it (content type from `stored.type`) to `{workspace_id}/{project_id}/{uuid}.{suffix}` with suffix `xer.gz`, `xml.gz`, `csv.gz` or `xlsx` from `report.format`, and inserts `code`, `status_date`, `activity_count`, `source_format` (`report.format`), `source_file_size` (`file.size`), `source_file_sha256` (`report.file.sha256`), `source_project_code`, `parser_version` (`report.parserVersion`), `import_mapping`, `import_report`; without `stored` it keeps Task 4's interim branch. `getImportReport` / `getImportMapping`: `schedules.select('import_report')` / `('import_mapping')` by id, `maybeSingle()`, no row → `not_found` "Schedule not found". `getScheduleSourceFile`: reads `source_file_path, source_file_name`; no path → `validation` "This schedule has no original file."; `storage.download(path)`; a `.gz` path is gunzipped with `gunzipSync` from `await import('fflate')`; returns `new File([bytes], source_file_name)`.

A `file` in the options replaces the fixture (read as an XER when its name ends in `.xer`), with the name and code from the options, else the file name.

`integration/imports.itest.ts`:

```ts
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { ImportIssue } from '@/data/types';
import type { Database } from '@/data/supabase/database.types';
import { SupabaseRepository } from '@/data/supabase/SupabaseRepository';
import { makeImportScheduleInput } from '@/test/fakes';
import { readLocalStack } from './local-stack';
import { makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture, signedInClient } from './supabase-fixture';

const stack = readLocalStack();
const admin = createClient<Database>(stack.apiUrl, stack.secretKey, { auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false } });
const pathOf = async (id: string) => (await admin.from('schedules').select('source_file_path').eq('id', id).single()).data!.source_file_path!;
const bytes = async (blob: Blob) => new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());

describe('imports on the local stack', () => {
  it('stores a gzipped XER and an xlsx as it is, and gives both originals back', async () => {
    const f = await makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture();
    const xer = await makeImportScheduleInput(f.projectIds['002']!);
    const xlsx = await makeImportScheduleInput(f.projectIds['002']!, { fixture: 'dates-1900.xlsx' });
    const [a, b] = [await f.repository.importSchedule(xer), await f.repository.importSchedule(xlsx)];
    expect(await pathOf(a.id)).toMatch(/\.xer\.gz$/);
    expect(await pathOf(b.id)).toMatch(/[0-9a-f-]{36}\.xlsx$/);
    expect(await bytes(await f.repository.getScheduleSourceFile(a.id))).toEqual(await bytes(xer.file));
    expect(await bytes(await f.repository.getScheduleSourceFile(b.id))).toEqual(await bytes(xlsx.file));
  });

  it('stores a report near 200 KB of Turkish text within the 256 KB column check', async () => {
    const f = await makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture();
    const input = await makeImportScheduleInput(f.projectIds['002']!);
    const long = (i: number): ImportIssue => ({
      severity: 'warning', code: 'TABLE_BAD_NUMBER', message: `${'Şantiye işçiliği ğüşıöç '.repeat(8).slice(0, 199)}…`,
      location: `Line ${i}`, original: 'İ'.repeat(200), used: 'ş'.repeat(200),
    });
    const { pack } = await import('@/import/index');
    const report = (await pack(new Uint8Array(await input.file.arrayBuffer()), input.file.name, { ...input.report!, issues: Array.from({ length: 500 }, (_, i) => long(i)) })).report;
    expect(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(report)).length).toBeGreaterThan(150_000);
    const schedule = await f.repository.importSchedule({ ...input, report });
    expect((await f.repository.getImportReport(schedule.id))!.issues).toHaveLength(report.issues.length);
  });

  it('keeps a P2-era import readable: no report or mapping, and its uncompressed original', async () => {
    const f = await makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture();
    const metro = f.projectIds['002']!;
    const path = `${f.workspaces.personal}/${metro}/${crypto.randomUUID()}.xer`;
    await admin.storage.from('schedule-imports').upload(path, new Blob(['ERMHDR\t8.0']));
    const { data } = await admin.from('schedules').insert({
      project_id: metro, name: 'P2 era', source: 'import', source_format: 'xer', source_file_name: 'old.xer', source_file_path: path,
    }).select('id').single();
    expect(await f.repository.getImportReport(data!.id)).toBeNull();
    expect(await f.repository.getImportMapping(data!.id)).toBeNull();
    const file = await f.repository.getScheduleSourceFile(data!.id);
    expect([file.name, new TextDecoder().decode(await bytes(file))]).toEqual(['old.xer', 'ERMHDR\t8.0']);
  });

  it('lets a viewer read the report and the mapping', async () => {
    const f = await makeSupabaseRepositoryFixture();
    const schedule = await f.repository.importSchedule(await makeImportScheduleInput(f.projectIds['T-01']!, { code: 'NTH-T' }));
    const viewer = new SupabaseRepository(await signedInClient(f.users.elif!.email, 'letters12345'));
    expect(await viewer.getImportReport(schedule.id)).toMatchObject({ format: 'xer' });
    expect(await viewer.getImportMapping(schedule.id)).toMatchObject({ sourceProjectId: '101' });
  });
});
```

`src/hooks/useImportReport.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { RepositoryError } from '@/data/errors';
import { useDownloadImportReport, useDownloadSourceFile, useImportReport } from '@/hooks/useImportReport';
import { saveFile } from '@/lib/download';
import { createTestDataLayer, makeImportScheduleInput } from '@/test/fakes';
import { createQueryWrapper } from '@/test/render';

vi.mock('@/lib/download', () => ({ saveFile: vi.fn() }));
const toastError = vi.hoisted(() => vi.fn());
vi.mock('sonner', async (original) => ({ ...(await original<typeof import('sonner')>()), toast: { error: toastError, success: vi.fn() } }));

async function imported() {
  const dl = createTestDataLayer();
  const metro = dl.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === '002')!;
  const schedule = await dl.repository.importSchedule(await makeImportScheduleInput(metro.id));
  return { dl, schedule };
}

describe('import report hooks', () => {
  it('reads a stored report once', async () => {
    const { dl, schedule } = await imported();
    const read = vi.spyOn(dl.repository, 'getImportReport');
    const wrapper = createQueryWrapper(dl);
    const first = renderHook(() => useImportReport(schedule.id), { wrapper });
    await waitFor(() => expect(first.result.current.data?.format).toBe('xer'));
    const second = renderHook(() => useImportReport(schedule.id), { wrapper });
    expect(second.result.current.data?.format).toBe('xer');
    expect(read).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('saves the report as CSV and JSON under its file name, and toasts a failure', async () => {
    const { dl, schedule } = await imported();
    const report = (await dl.repository.getImportReport(schedule.id))!;
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useDownloadImportReport(), { wrapper: createQueryWrapper(dl) });
    await act(() => result.current.mutateAsync({ report, scheduleName: 'North Plant', format: 'csv' }));
    await act(() => result.current.mutateAsync({ report, scheduleName: 'North Plant', format: 'json' }));
    expect(vi.mocked(saveFile).mock.calls.map(([blob, name]) => [blob.type, name])).toEqual([
      ['text/csv;charset=utf-8', 'North Plant - import report.csv'],
      ['application/json', 'North Plant - import report.json'],
    ]);
    vi.mocked(saveFile).mockImplementationOnce(() => { throw new Error('blocked'); });
    await act(() => result.current.mutateAsync({ report, scheduleName: 'North Plant', format: 'csv' }).catch(() => undefined));
    expect(toastError).toHaveBeenCalledWith("The report couldn't be downloaded. Try again.");
  });

  it('saves the original under its name, and toasts why it cannot', async () => {
    const { dl, schedule } = await imported();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useDownloadSourceFile(), { wrapper: createQueryWrapper(dl) });
    await act(() => result.current.mutateAsync(schedule.id));
    expect(vi.mocked(saveFile).mock.calls.at(-1)![1]).toBe('two-projects.xer');
    dl.repository.getScheduleSourceFile = () => Promise.reject(new RepositoryError('validation', 'This schedule has no original file.'));
    await act(() => result.current.mutateAsync(schedule.id).catch(() => undefined));
    expect(toastError).toHaveBeenCalledWith('This schedule has no original file.');
  });
});
```

**Files:**
- Create: `src/lib/project-options.ts`, `src/features/projects/coordinates.ts`, `src/features/projects/coordinates.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/features/projects/project-schema.ts`, `src/features/projects/project-schema.test.ts`, `src/features/projects/steps/StepFields.tsx`, `src/features/projects/steps/AttributesStep.tsx`, `src/features/projects/steps/LocationStep.tsx`, `src/features/projects/NewProjectWizard.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's types.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // src/lib/project-options.ts
  export interface Option<T extends string> { value: T; label: string }
  export const STATUS_OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<Option<ProjectStatus>>;      // Active · On hold · Completed · Cancelled
  export const STAGE_OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<Option<ProjectStage>>;        // Pursuit · Preconstruction · Construction · Closeout · Completed
  export const PROJECT_TYPE_OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<Option<ProjectType>>;  // Building · Infrastructure · Industrial · Residential · Energy · Transportation · Water · Other
  export const SECTOR_OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<Option<ProjectSector>>;      // Public · Private · Public–private partnership
  export function isOptionValue<T extends string>(options: ReadonlyArray<Option<T>>, value: unknown): value is T;
  export function optionLabel<T extends string>(options: ReadonlyArray<Option<T>>, value: T | null): string; // "—" for null
  // src/features/projects/coordinates.ts
  export function parseCoordinate(text: string): number | null;      // trimmed; ',' or '.' decimal mark; '' → null; anything else that isn't a plain decimal → NaN
  export function coordinateDecimals(text: string): number;
  export function splitCoordinatePair(text: string): [string, string] | null; // two numbers split by ';', whitespace or ', ', or by ',' when both use '.'
  // StepFields.tsx
  export function SelectField<T extends string>(props: { name: SelectFieldName; label: string; options: ReadonlyArray<Option<T>>; noneLabel?: string }): ReactElement; // noneLabel ("None") stands for ''
  export function FieldHint({ id, children }: { id: string; children: ReactNode }): ReactElement;
  ```
- `project-schema.ts`: `ProjectFormValues` gains `status: ProjectStatus`, `stage: ProjectStage | ''`, `sector: ProjectSector | ''`, `latitude: string`, `longitude: string` (`projectType` takes the eight types); `EMPTY_PROJECT_FORM` adds `status: 'active'`, `stage: ''`, `sector: ''`, `latitude: ''`, `longitude: ''`; `STEP_FIELDS.attributes = ['status', 'stage', 'projectType', 'sector', 'statusDate', 'startDate', 'plannedFinishDate']`, `STEP_FIELDS.location = ['address', 'city', 'country', 'latitude', 'longitude']`; `PROJECT_TYPE_OPTIONS` moves to `project-options.ts`. Coordinates (superRefine, §6.5 copy): one filled and one empty → "Enter both latitude and longitude" on the empty one; not a number or out of range → "Enter a latitude between -90 and 90" / "Enter a longitude between -180 and 180"; more than 6 decimals → "Use up to 6 decimal places". `toNewProjectInput` maps them (`parseCoordinate`; `''` → `null`).
- Attributes rows: "Status" | "Stage"; "Project type" | "Sector"; "Status date"; "Start date" | "Planned finish date". Status has no "None"; Stage, Project type and Sector start with "None". Location: Address; City | Country; Latitude | Longitude with the hint "Decimal degrees, for example 41.0151 and 28.9795. Paste both into Latitude to fill the two fields."; pasting text that `splitCoordinatePair` splits into Latitude fills both fields.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { coordinateDecimals, parseCoordinate, splitCoordinatePair } from '@/features/projects/coordinates';

describe('coordinates', () => {
  it('reads a period or a comma as the decimal mark', () => {
    expect(parseCoordinate(' 41.0151 ')).toBe(41.0151);
    expect(parseCoordinate('41,0151')).toBe(41.0151);
    expect(parseCoordinate('-28.9795')).toBe(-28.9795);
    expect(parseCoordinate('')).toBeNull();
    expect(parseCoordinate('north')).toBeNaN();
    expect(parseCoordinate('1e3')).toBeNaN();
    expect(coordinateDecimals('41,0151234')).toBe(7);
    expect(coordinateDecimals('41')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('splits a pasted pair and leaves single numbers alone', () => {
    expect(splitCoordinatePair('41.0151, 28.9795')).toEqual(['41.0151', '28.9795']);
    expect(splitCoordinatePair('41.0151,28.9795')).toEqual(['41.0151', '28.9795']);
    expect(splitCoordinatePair('41,0151; 28,9795')).toEqual(['41,0151', '28,9795']);
    expect(splitCoordinatePair('-33.8688 151.2093')).toEqual(['-33.8688', '151.2093']);
    expect(splitCoordinatePair('41,0151')).toBeNull();
    expect(splitCoordinatePair('41.0151')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- Modify: `src/features/dialogs/DialogsProvider.tsx`, `src/features/dialogs/DialogHost.tsx`, `src/features/projects/project-schema.ts`, `src/features/projects/project-schema.test.ts`, `src/features/projects/NewProjectWizard.tsx`, `src/features/projects/steps/DetailsStep.tsx`, `src/features/projects/steps/AttributesStep.tsx`, `src/features/projects/steps/StepFields.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5's `useProject`, `useUpdateProject`, `makeUpdateProjectInput`; Task 16's fields; `useWorkspaces`, `canEdit`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // DialogKind adds 'editProject'; it opens with { workspaceId, projectId } (DialogHost renders null without either)
  // project-schema.ts
  //   ProjectFormValues gains keepCover: boolean (true in EMPTY_PROJECT_FORM; in no STEP_FIELDS)
  export function projectFormValues(project: Project): ProjectFormValues;   // nulls → '', coordinates → String(n), keepCover true, coverImage null
  export function toUpdateProjectInput(values: ProjectFormValues, projectId: ID, options: { includeStatusDate: boolean }): UpdateProjectInput;
  //   cover: values.coverImage ?? (values.keepCover ? 'keep' : 'remove'); statusDate only with includeStatusDate
  // NewProjectWizard.tsx
  export type WizardMode = { kind: 'create' } | { kind: 'edit'; project: ProjectSummary; workspace: WorkspaceSummary; readOnly: boolean };
  export function NewProjectWizard({ mode }: { mode?: WizardMode }): ReactElement;
  // EditProjectDialog.tsx
  export function EditProjectDialog({ workspaceId, projectId }: { workspaceId: ID; projectId: ID }): ReactElement;
  ```
- Edit mode: values from `projectFormValues`; every step starts completed and is reachable from the stepper; title "Edit project", description "In {workspace name}"; footer "Cancel" · "Back" · "Next" · primary "Save changes" ("Saving…" while pending) on every step; "Save changes" validates every step (the first invalid field's step opens) and calls `useUpdateProject` with `{ workspaceId: project.workspaceId, input }` → toast "Project updated", the dialog closes; a code equal to the original after trimming and lower-casing skips the availability check; a `conflict` returns to Details as in create mode. With `project.currentSchedule`, "Status date" is disabled with the hint "Follows the current schedule ({name})" and `includeStatusDate` is false (R6). `DetailsStep` shows the current photo with "Remove" (sets `keepCover` false); a chosen file replaces it.
- Read-only mode (viewers, `!canEdit(workspace)`): title "Project details", the same steps with every field read-only (`readOnly` inputs, disabled selects), stepper navigation, footer "Close" only.
- `EditProjectDialog`: while the project or workspaces load, a `DialogShell` titled "Edit project" with a skeleton (`data-testid="edit-project-skeleton"`); a failed read → `SectionError` with Retry inside it; then the wizard in edit or read-only mode.

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';

function openEdit(code: string, dataLayer = createTestDataLayer()) {

  const utils = renderWithProviders(<OpenDialog kind="editProject" options={{ workspaceId: project.workspaceId, projectId: project.id }} />, { dataLayer });

}

describe('EditProjectDialog', () => {

    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Stage'));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('option', { name: 'Construction' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Project updated')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.id === project.id)).toMatchObject({
      name: 'Metro Line Extension', attributes: { stage: 'construction' }, location: { city: 'Ankara', latitude: 39.9334 },
    });
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('none'));
  });

  it("checks another project's ID but not the project's own", async () => {
    const isProjectCodeAvailable = vi.fn(async () => false);
    const { user } = openEdit('002', createTestDataLayer({ overrides: { isProjectCodeAvailable } }));
    const code = await screen.findByLabelText('Project ID');
    await user.clear(code);
    await user.type(code, ' 002 ');
    await act(() => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 400)));
    expect(isProjectCodeAvailable).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await user.clear(code);
    await user.type(code, '001');
    expect(await screen.findByText('This ID is already used in this workspace')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('removes the current photo', async () => {
    const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
    dataLayer.store.write((db) => {
      db.projects.find((p) => p.code === '002')!.coverImageUrl = 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgo=';
    });
    const { user, project } = openEdit('002', dataLayer);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Remove' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Project updated')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.id === project.id)!.coverImageUrl).toBeNull();
  });

  it('keeps the status date with the current schedule (R6)', async () => {
    const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
    const update = vi.spyOn(dataLayer.repository, 'updateProject');
    const { user } = openEdit('003', dataLayer);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /Attributes/ }));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Status date')).toBeDisabled();
    expect(screen.getByText('Follows the current schedule (Update 2)')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Project updated')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(update.mock.calls[0]![0]).not.toHaveProperty('statusDate');
  });

  it('shows viewers the project details, read only', async () => {
    const { user } = openEdit('T-01', createTestDataLayer({ teamRole: 'viewer' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Project details' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByLabelText('Project name')).toHaveAttribute('readonly');
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: /Location/ }));
    expect(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Latitude')).toHaveValue('41.0422');
    await user.click(within(dialog).getAllByRole('button', { name: 'Close' }).at(-1)!);
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('none'));
  });
});
```

### Task 18: List rules, toolbar, status badge and filter chips

**Spec:** §6.1 (`StatusBadge` and its token pairs, `FilterChips`, Formats, the `check-contrast.mjs` pairs), §6.2 (Toolbar row; Tiles row; sort rules; "Filters combine with AND"), §5.4 Matching, §6.6 (toolbar wrap; layout toggle roles), §9 (Home `ProjectCard`; Config `ui.projectsLayout`), D21, D24, D41.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/features/projects/project-list.ts`, `src/features/projects/project-list.test.ts`, `src/components/common/FilterChips.tsx`, `src/components/common/FilterChips.test.tsx`, `src/components/common/StatusBadge.tsx`, `src/components/common/StatusBadge.test.tsx`, `src/features/projects/ProjectsToolbar.tsx`, `src/features/projects/ProjectsToolbar.test.tsx`, `src/hooks/useProjectsLayout.ts`, `src/components/ui/toggle-group.tsx`, `src/components/ui/toggle.tsx`
- Modify: `src/features/home/ProjectCard.tsx`, `src/features/home/ProjectCard.test.tsx`, `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`, `scripts/check-contrast.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `matchesSearch` (Task 9), `compareText`, `compareIds`, `project-options.ts` (Task 16), `readKey` / `writeKey`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // project-list.ts
  export type ProjectSort = 'updated' | 'name' | 'status-date';
  export interface ProjectsView { q: string; status: ProjectStatus | null; stage: ProjectStage | null; sort: ProjectSort }
  export type ProjectsParam = 'q' | 'status' | 'stage' | 'sort';
  export function readProjectsView(params: URLSearchParams): ProjectsView;      // unknown or malformed values → defaults
  export function withViewParam(params: URLSearchParams, key: ProjectsParam, value: string | null): URLSearchParams; // a new object; drops '' / null and sort=updated
  export function withoutFilters(params: URLSearchParams): URLSearchParams;     // drops q, status and stage; keeps sort
  export function activeFilterCount(view: ProjectsView): number;               // status and stage
  export function filterChips(view: ProjectsView): FilterChipItem[];            // ids 'status' | 'stage'; labels "Status", "Stage"; option labels as values
  export function filterProjects<T extends ProjectSummary>(projects: readonly T[], view: ProjectsView): T[]; // q over name and code with matchesSearch; AND
  export function sortProjects<T extends ProjectSummary>(projects: readonly T[], sort: ProjectSort): T[];
  // FilterChips.tsx
  export interface FilterChipItem { id: string; label: string; value: string }
  export function FilterChips(props: { chips: readonly FilterChipItem[]; onRemove(id: string): void; onClear(): void }): ReactElement | null;
  // StatusBadge.tsx
  export function StatusBadge({ status, className }: { status: ProjectStatus; className?: string }): ReactElement;
  // useProjectsLayout.ts
  export type ProjectsLayout = 'tiles' | 'list';
  export function useProjectsLayout(): [ProjectsLayout, (layout: ProjectsLayout) => void]; // 'ui.projectsLayout' through storage.ts; 'tiles' by default
  // ProjectsToolbar.tsx — reads and writes the URL itself (useSearchParams, replace: true)
  export function ProjectsToolbar(props: { layout: ProjectsLayout; onLayoutChange(layout: ProjectsLayout): void }): ReactElement;
  // ProjectCard.tsx
  export function ProjectCard({ project, actions }: { project: ProjectSummary; actions?: ReactNode }): ReactElement;
  ```
- Sort rules (each ends with `compareIds`): updated — `updatedAt` desc; name — `compareText(name)`, then `compareText(code)`; status-date — `statusDate` desc, nulls last, then `compareText(name)`.
- `StatusBadge`: an outer `<span data-status={status}>` with `bg-card` and `rounded-sm`, so it stays opaque; the label `<span>` carries the status classes — Active `text-foreground` with a 6 px `bg-success` dot (`aria-hidden`) before the text; On hold `bg-warning-bg text-warning-foreground border border-warning-border`; Completed `bg-muted text-muted-foreground`; Cancelled `text-destructive border border-destructive`. `check-contrast.mjs` exports `TOKEN_PAIRS` and adds `['--muted-foreground', ['--muted'], TEXT]`, `['--success', ['--card'], NON_TEXT]`, `['--success', ['--background'], NON_TEXT]`.
- `FilterChips`: nothing when `chips` is empty; otherwise one chip per item with the text "{label}: {value}" and a remove button (aria-label "Remove filter {label}"), then the link-button "Clear filters".
- `ProjectsToolbar` (§6.2 copy): `type="search"` input (placeholder "Search projects…", aria-label "Search projects"; `q`); "Filters" button ("Filters · {n}" when active) opening a popover with selects "Status" and "Stage" (first option "Any") and the footer link-button "Clear filters"; "Sort by" select ("Last updated", "Name", "Status date"); a toggle group (aria-label "Layout") with "Tiles" (`LayoutGrid`) and "List" (`List`) that ignores a click leaving none pressed; `FilterChips` under it (its "Clear filters" uses `withoutFilters`). Below 768 px it wraps with the search at full width. Every URL change replaces the history entry. Add the shadcn wrappers with `npx --yes shadcn@latest add toggle-group --yes` and restore any other file the CLI touched.
- `ProjectCard`: a `StatusBadge` in the cover's bottom-left corner when the status isn't `active`; `actions` inside `<div data-card-actions className="absolute top-2 right-2 z-10">`.

`src/features/projects/project-list.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { ProjectSummary } from '@/data/types';
import { activeFilterCount, filterChips, filterProjects, readProjectsView, sortProjects, withoutFilters, withViewParam } from '@/features/projects/project-list';
import { createTestDataLayer } from '@/test/fakes';

const params = (query: string) => new URLSearchParams(query);
const codes = (list: readonly ProjectSummary[]) => list.map((p) => p.code);
async function seeded() {
  const { repository, store } = createTestDataLayer();
  return repository.listProjects(store.read().workspaces[0]!.id);
}

describe('project list rules', () => {
  it('reads the view and ignores values it does not know', () => {
    expect(readProjectsView(params(''))).toEqual({ q: '', status: null, stage: null, sort: 'updated' });
    expect(readProjectsView(params('q=pier&status=on_hold&stage=construction&sort=name'))).toEqual({ q: 'pier', status: 'on_hold', stage: 'construction', sort: 'name' });
    expect(readProjectsView(params('status=paused&stage=x&sort=size&from=2026-02-30'))).toEqual({ q: '', status: null, stage: null, sort: 'updated' });
  });

  it('writes params without defaults or empty values and clears the filters with the search', () => {
    const p = withViewParam(withViewParam(params('sort=name'), 'status', 'completed'), 'sort', 'updated');
    expect(p.toString()).toBe('status=completed');
    expect(withViewParam(p, 'status', null).toString()).toBe('');
    expect(withViewParam(p, 'q', '').toString()).toBe('status=completed');
    expect(withoutFilters(params('sort=name&q=x&status=active&stage=pursuit')).toString()).toBe('sort=name');
  });

  it('counts the filters and names each for its chip', () => {
    const view = readProjectsView(params('q=x&status=on_hold&stage=closeout'));
    expect(activeFilterCount(view)).toBe(2);
    expect(filterChips(view)).toEqual([
      { id: 'status', label: 'Status', value: 'On hold' },
      { id: 'stage', label: 'Stage', value: 'Closeout' },
    ]);
  });

  it('searches name and code with Turkish folding', async () => {
    const projects = await seeded();
    const view = (q: string) => readProjectsView(params(`q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`));
    expect(codes(filterProjects(projects, view('RİVERSİDE')))).toEqual(['001']);
    expect(codes(filterProjects(projects, view('harbor')))).toEqual(['003']);
    expect(codes(filterProjects(projects, view('00')))).toEqual(['001', '002', '003', '004', '005', '006']);
    expect(codes(filterProjects(projects, view('istanbul')))).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('combines status and stage with AND', async () => {
    const projects = await seeded();
    expect(codes(filterProjects(projects, readProjectsView(params('status=active&stage=construction'))))).toEqual(['001', '003']);
    expect(codes(filterProjects(projects, readProjectsView(params('status=on_hold'))))).toEqual(['004']);
  });

  it('sorts by last updated, name and status date with their tie-breaks, nulls last', async () => {
    const projects = await seeded();
    expect(codes(sortProjects(projects, 'updated'))).toEqual(['001', '002', '003', '004', '005', '006']);
    expect(codes(sortProjects(projects, 'name'))).toEqual(['004', '003', '002', '005', '006', '001']);
    expect(codes(sortProjects(projects, 'status-date'))).toEqual(['001', '003', '004', '006', '002', '005']);
    const twins = projects.map((p) => ({ ...p, name: 'Same', updatedAt: projects[0]!.updatedAt, statusDate: null }));
    const byId = [...twins].sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1)).map((p) => p.code);
    expect(codes(sortProjects([...twins].reverse(), 'updated'))).toEqual(byId);
    expect(codes(sortProjects([...twins].reverse(), 'name'))).toEqual(['001', '002', '003', '004', '005', '006']);
    expect(codes(sortProjects([...twins].reverse(), 'status-date'))).toEqual(byId);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/common/StatusBadge';

describe('StatusBadge', () => {
  it.each([
    ['active', 'Active', ['text-foreground']],
    ['on_hold', 'On hold', ['bg-warning-bg', 'text-warning-foreground', 'border-warning-border']],
    ['completed', 'Completed', ['bg-muted', 'text-muted-foreground']],
    ['cancelled', 'Cancelled', ['text-destructive', 'border-destructive']],
  ] as const)('shows %s as "%s" on its tokens, on an opaque chip', (status, label, tokens) => {
    const { container } = render(<StatusBadge status={status} />);
    expect(screen.getByText(label)).toHaveClass(...tokens);
    expect(container.querySelector(`[data-status="${status}"]`)).toHaveClass('bg-card');

});
```

`src/features/projects/ProjectsToolbar.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ProjectsToolbar } from '@/features/projects/ProjectsToolbar';
import { useProjectsLayout } from '@/hooks/useProjectsLayout';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const location = () => screen.getByTestId('location').textContent ?? '';
function renderToolbar(search = '', onLayoutChange = vi.fn()) {
  const utils = renderWithProviders(<ProjectsToolbar layout="tiles" onLayoutChange={onLayoutChange} />, { route: `/w/ws/projects${search}`, path: '/w/:workspaceId/projects' });
  return { ...utils, onLayoutChange };
}
function LayoutProbe() {
  const [layout, setLayout] = useProjectsLayout();
  return <button type="button" onClick={() => setLayout('list')}>{layout}</button>;
}

describe('ProjectsToolbar', () => {
  it('writes the search, filters and sort into the URL and switches the layout', async () => {
    const { user, onLayoutChange } = renderToolbar();
    await user.type(screen.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search projects' }), 'pier');
    expect(location()).toMatch(/\?q=pier$/);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Status'));
    expect((await screen.findAllByRole('option')).map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual(['Any', 'Active', 'On hold', 'Completed', 'Cancelled']);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'On hold' }));
    await user.keyboard('{Escape}');
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Sort by'));
    expect((await screen.findAllByRole('option')).map((o) => o.textContent)).toEqual(['Last updated', 'Name', 'Status date']);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('option', { name: 'Status date' }));
    expect(location()).toContain('status=on_hold');
    expect(location()).toContain('sort=status-date');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'List' }));
    expect(onLayoutChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith('list');
  });

  it('counts the active filters, shows their chips and clears them with the search', async () => {
    const { user } = renderToolbar('?q=x&status=on_hold&stage=construction&sort=name');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters · 2' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Remove filter Status' }));
    expect(location()).not.toContain('status=');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Clear filters' }));
    expect(location()).toMatch(/\?sort=name$/);
    expect(screen.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search projects' })).toHaveValue('');
  });

  it('remembers the layout in ui.projectsLayout', async () => {
    const { user, unmount } = renderWithProviders(<LayoutProbe />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button')).toHaveTextContent('tiles');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button'));
    expect(localStorage.getItem('ui.projectsLayout')).toBe('list');
    unmount();
    renderWithProviders(<LayoutProbe />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('button')).toHaveTextContent('list');
  });
});
```

### Task 19: Projects page

**Spec:** §6.2 (every region, the project menu, "Delete project", empty states, NotFound), §6.1 (`DataTable`, viewer rule, Formats), §6.6 (List columns hidden below 1024 / 640 px; `MENU_ITEM_FOCUS_CLASSES`), §4.3 R12, §9 (Routes and pages), §7 Components (Projects page) and E2E (mock) `projects.spec.ts`.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/components/common/DataTable.tsx`, `src/components/common/DataTable.test.tsx`, `src/features/projects/ProjectMenu.tsx`, `src/features/projects/ProjectMenu.test.tsx`, `src/features/projects/DeleteProjectDialog.tsx`, `src/pages/ProjectsPage.tsx`, `src/pages/ProjectsPage.test.tsx`, `e2e/projects.spec.ts`
- Modify: `src/app/router.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 5–6 hooks, Task 17's `editProject`, Task 18's pieces; `useWorkspaces`, `useWorkspaceProjects`, `useDialogs`, `canEdit`, `isOwner`, `NotFoundPage`, `EmptyState`, `SectionError`, `ProjectCardSkeleton`, `pluralize`, `shortDate`, `MENU_ITEM_FOCUS_CLASSES`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // DataTable.tsx (§6.1)
  export interface DataTableRowLink { to: string; label: string; title: string }
  export interface DataTableProps<TRow> {
    columns: ReadonlyArray<ColumnDef<TRow, unknown>>; data: readonly TRow[]; getRowId(row: TRow): string; caption: string;
    state: 'loading' | 'error' | 'ready'; error?: unknown; onRetry?: () => void; empty: ReactNode; rowLink?: (row: TRow) => DataTableRowLink;
    rowClassName?: (row: TRow) => string | undefined;
  }
  export function DataTable<TRow>(props: DataTableProps<TRow>): ReactElement;
  // module augmentation of @tanstack/react-table: ColumnMeta { className?: string; link?: boolean }
  // ProjectMenu.tsx
  export function ProjectMenu(props: { project: ProjectSummary; workspace: WorkspaceSummary }): ReactElement;
  // DeleteProjectDialog.tsx
  export function DeleteProjectDialog(props: { project: ProjectSummary; open: boolean; onOpenChange(open: boolean): void }): ReactElement;
  // ProjectsPage.tsx — route element of 'w/:workspaceId/projects'
  export function ProjectsPage(): ReactElement;
  ```
- `DataTable`: `useReactTable` with `getCoreRowModel`; a visually hidden `<caption>`; header cells with `SharedSchedulesTable`'s header classes; a column's `meta.className` on its `th` and `td`; with `rowLink`, the cell of the column whose `meta.link` is true (else the first) starts with the stretched row link (`SharedSchedulesTable`'s `ROW_LINK_CLASSES`, `title` from `rowLink`); menus sit above it (`relative z-10`); `loading` → 5 rows `data-testid="data-table-skeleton-row"`; `ready` without data → one row whose cell spans every column and holds `empty`; `error` → that cell holds `<SectionError error onRetry />`.
- `ProjectMenu`: trigger `MoreHorizontal`, aria-label "Actions for {project name}"; items "Open" (link to `/w/:w/p/:p/files`), "Edit project" (editors and owners) or "Project details" (viewers), both opening `editProject` with `{ workspaceId, projectId }`; separator and "Delete project" (owners) → `DeleteProjectDialog`.
- `DeleteProjectDialog` (local `AlertDialog`, §6.2 copy): title "Delete {project}?"; description "This deletes its {pluralize(totalScheduleCount, 'schedule')} and their stored files. It can't be undone." or, with none, "This deletes the project. It can't be undone."; the visible text "Type the project ID to confirm" over an input with aria-label "Project ID"; "Cancel" · "Delete project" (destructive; `aria-disabled="true"` and inert until the input equals the code, ignoring case and outer spaces) → `useDeleteProject` → toast "Project deleted"; when the current path starts with `/w/{ws}/p/{project}/`, the app opens `/w/{ws}/projects`. A `submittingRef` that a success leaves set and only a failure clears makes two quick clicks one request; a failure shows `errorMessage` in a `role="alert"` line inside the dialog.
- The page (§6.2 copy): h1 "Projects" (shown at once); muted line "{workspace} · {pluralize(n, 'project')} · {pluralize(m, 'schedule')}" (`m` sums `scheduleCount`); "Members" (`UserCog`, team workspaces only → `workspaceMembers`) and "+ New project" (primary → `newProject`; for viewers `aria-disabled="true"`, inert, Tooltip "Viewers can't make changes in this workspace", `WelcomeBanner`'s pattern); `ProjectsToolbar`; content filtered and sorted by Task 18's rules in the layout of `useProjectsLayout`. Tiles: `ProjectCard` with `actions={<ProjectMenu …/>}` in `grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4`, 4 `ProjectCardSkeleton`s while loading. List: `DataTable` caption "Projects", columns "Project" (link label = name, `meta.link`; cell = code chip), "Status" (`StatusBadge`), "Stage" (`hidden lg:table-cell`), "Type" (`hidden sm:table-cell`), "Client" (`hidden lg:table-cell`), "City" (`hidden sm:table-cell`), "Status date" (`shortDate`), "Schedules" (`scheduleCount`), and a menu column with the visually hidden header "Actions"; empty values "—"; row link → files page, `title` = name, code, client and city on separate lines (missing ones left out). Empty states and load errors per §6.2. An unknown or inaccessible workspace (not in `useWorkspaces` once loaded) shows `NotFoundPage`.
