```tsx
import type { ColumnDef } from '@tanstack/react-table';
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { DataTable, type DataTableProps } from '@/components/common/DataTable';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

interface Row { id: string; name: string; city: string | null }
const rows: Row[] = [{ id: 'a', name: 'Pier 9', city: 'Izmir' }, { id: 'b', name: 'Dock', city: null }];
const columns: ColumnDef<Row, unknown>[] = [
  { id: 'name', header: 'Name', meta: { link: true }, cell: () => null },
  { id: 'city', header: 'City', meta: { className: 'hidden md:table-cell' }, cell: ({ row }) => row.original.city ?? '—' },
];
function renderTable(props: Partial<DataTableProps<Row>> = {}) {
  return renderWithProviders(
    <DataTable columns={columns} data={rows} getRowId={(row) => row.id} caption="Test rows" state="ready" empty={<p>Nothing here</p>}
      rowLink={(row) => ({ to: `/rows/${row.id}`, label: row.name, title: `${row.name}\n${row.city ?? ''}` })} {...props} />,
  );
}

describe('DataTable', () => {
  it('renders the caption, headers, cells and a row link with its title', () => {
    renderTable();
    expect(screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Test rows' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('columnheader').map((cell) => cell.textContent)).toEqual(['Name', 'City']);
    expect(screen.getByRole('columnheader', { name: 'City' })).toHaveClass('hidden', 'md:table-cell');
    const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Pier 9' });
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('href', '/rows/a');
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('title', 'Pier 9\nIzmir');
    expect(screen.getByText('—')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows five skeleton rows while loading', () => {
    renderTable({ state: 'loading' });
    expect(screen.getAllByTestId('data-table-skeleton-row')).toHaveLength(5);
  });

  it('shows the empty node, and the error with Retry, in one full-width row', async () => {
    const { unmount } = renderTable({ data: [] });
    expect(screen.getByText('Nothing here').closest('td')).toHaveAttribute('colspan', '2');
    unmount();
    const onRetry = vi.fn();
    const { user } = renderTable({ state: 'error', error: new Error('offline'), onRetry });
    expect(screen.getByRole('alert').closest('td')).toHaveAttribute('colspan', '2');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retry' }));
    expect(onRetry).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });
});
```

`src/features/projects/ProjectMenu.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { Role } from '@/data/types';
import { ProjectMenu } from '@/features/projects/ProjectMenu';
import { createTestDataLayer } from '@/test/fakes';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

async function renderMenu(code = '003', teamRole?: Role, route = '/') {
  const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer({ teamRole });
  const workspaceId = dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === code)!.workspaceId;
  const project = (await dataLayer.repository.listProjects(workspaceId)).find((p) => p.code === code)!;
  const workspace = (await dataLayer.repository.listWorkspaces()).find((w) => w.id === workspaceId)!;
  const utils = renderWithProviders(<ProjectMenu project={project} workspace={workspace} />, { dataLayer, route: route.replace(':p', project.id).replace(':w', workspaceId) });
  return { ...utils, project };
}
const items = () => screen.getAllByRole('menuitem').map((item) => item.textContent);

describe('ProjectMenu', () => {
  it('offers each role the actions it can take', async () => {
    const owner = await renderMenu();
    await owner.user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Harbor Logistics Hub' }));
    expect(items()).toEqual(['Open', 'Edit project', 'Delete project']);
    owner.unmount();
    const editor = await renderMenu('T-01', 'editor');
    await editor.user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Tunnel Ventilation Upgrade' }));
    expect(items()).toEqual(['Open', 'Edit project']);
    editor.unmount();
    const viewer = await renderMenu('T-01', 'viewer');
    await viewer.user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Tunnel Ventilation Upgrade' }));
    expect(items()).toEqual(['Open', 'Project details']);
  });

  it('opens the project and its edit dialog', async () => {
    const { user, project } = await renderMenu();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Harbor Logistics Hub' }));
    expect(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Open' })).toHaveAttribute('href', `/w/${project.workspaceId}/p/${project.id}/files`);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Edit project' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('editProject');
  });

  it('deletes the project once its ID is typed, with one request for two quick clicks, and leaves its pages', async () => {
    const { user, dataLayer, project } = await renderMenu('003', undefined, '/w/:w/p/:p/files');
    const deleteProject = vi.spyOn(dataLayer.repository, 'deleteProject');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Harbor Logistics Hub' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Delete project' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('alertdialog', { name: 'Delete Harbor Logistics Hub?' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveAccessibleDescription("This deletes its 3 schedules and their stored files. It can't be undone.");
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Type the project ID to confirm')).toBeInTheDocument();
    const confirm = within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete project' });
    expect(confirm).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    await user.click(confirm);
    expect(deleteProject).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await user.type(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Project ID'), ' 003 ');
    expect(confirm).not.toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    act(() => {
      confirm.click();
      confirm.click();
    });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Project deleted')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(deleteProject).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('location')).toHaveTextContent(`/w/${project.workspaceId}/projects`);
  });
});
```

`src/pages/ProjectsPage.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RepositoryError } from '@/data/errors';
import { ProjectsPage } from '@/pages/ProjectsPage';
import { createTestDataLayer, type TestDataLayer } from '@/test/fakes';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

async function renderProjects(options: { dataLayer?: TestDataLayer; team?: boolean; search?: string } = {}) {
  const dataLayer = options.dataLayer ?? createTestDataLayer();
  const workspace = dataLayer.store.read().workspaces.find((w) => w.kind === (options.team ? 'team' : 'personal'))!;
  const utils = renderWithProviders(<ProjectsPage />, { route: `/w/${workspace.id}/projects${options.search ?? ''}`, path: '/w/:workspaceId/projects', dataLayer });
  await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Projects' });
  return { ...utils, workspace };
}
const tileNames = () => screen.getAllByRole('article').map((tile) => within(tile).getAllByRole('link')[0]!.textContent);
const search = () => screen.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search projects' });

describe('ProjectsPage', () => {
  it("shows the workspace's counts and its projects as tiles, last updated first", async () => {
    const { user } = await renderProjects();
    expect(await screen.findByText('My workspace · 6 projects · 7 schedules')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await screen.findAllByRole('article');
    expect(tileNames()).toEqual(['Riverside Office Tower', 'Metro Line Extension', 'Harbor Logistics Hub', 'Campus Library Retrofit', 'Northgate Bridge Repair', 'Old Mill Housing']);
    expect(within(screen.getAllByRole('article')[3]!).getByText('On hold')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New project' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('newProject');
  });

  it('shows Not found for a workspace that is not mine', async () => {
    renderWithProviders(<ProjectsPage />, { route: '/w/unknown/projects', path: '/w/:workspaceId/projects' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Page not found' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows skeletons while the projects load and Retry when they fail', async () => {
    const { unmount } = await renderProjects({ dataLayer: createTestDataLayer({ overrides: { listProjects: () => new Promise(() => {}) } }) });
    expect(await screen.findAllByTestId('project-card-skeleton')).toHaveLength(4);
    unmount();
    await renderProjects({ dataLayer: createTestDataLayer({ overrides: { listProjects: () => Promise.reject(new RepositoryError('network', 'offline')) } }) });
    expect(await screen.findByText("Can't reach the server. Check your connection and try again.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retry' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('searches with Turkish folding and keeps the search in the URL', async () => {
    const { user } = await renderProjects();
    await screen.findAllByRole('article');
    await user.type(search(), 'RİVERSİDE');
    await waitFor(() => expect(tileNames()).toEqual(['Riverside Office Tower']));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('location').textContent).toMatch(/\?q=/);
  });

  it('filters by status and stage from the URL and counts the filters', async () => {
    await renderProjects({ search: '?status=active&stage=construction' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(tileNames()).toEqual(['Riverside Office Tower', 'Harbor Logistics Hub']));
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters · 2' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Stage: Construction')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('sorts by name and by status date, nulls last', async () => {
    const { unmount } = await renderProjects({ search: '?sort=name' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(tileNames()[0]).toBe('Campus Library Retrofit'));
    unmount();
    await renderProjects({ search: '?sort=status-date' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(tileNames()).toEqual(['Riverside Office Tower', 'Harbor Logistics Hub', 'Campus Library Retrofit', 'Old Mill Housing', 'Metro Line Extension', 'Northgate Bridge Repair']));
  });

  it('switches to the list layout with a menu per row, and remembers it', async () => {
    const { user, unmount, dataLayer, workspace } = await renderProjects();
    await screen.findAllByRole('article');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'List' }));
    const table = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Projects' });
    expect(within(table).getAllByRole('columnheader').map((h) => h.textContent)).toEqual(['Project', 'Status', 'Stage', 'Type', 'Client', 'City', 'Status date', 'Schedules', 'Actions']);
    const riverside = dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === '001')!;
    const link = within(table).getByRole('link', { name: 'Riverside Office Tower' });
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('href', `/w/${workspace.id}/p/${riverside.id}/files`);
    expect(link).toHaveAttribute('title', 'Riverside Office Tower\n001\nRiverside Holding\nIstanbul');
    const millRow = within(table).getByRole('link', { name: 'Old Mill Housing' }).closest('tr')!;
    expect(within(millRow).getByText('—')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(millRow).getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Old Mill Housing' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(table).not.toHaveTextContent('null');
    unmount();
    await renderProjects({ dataLayer });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Projects' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the empty workspace to editors and viewers, and clears a search that matches nothing', async () => {
    const { unmount } = await renderProjects({ dataLayer: createTestDataLayer({ scenario: 'empty' }) });
    expect(await screen.findByText('No projects yet')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Create a project to organize its schedules.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    unmount();
    const viewer = createTestDataLayer({ teamRole: 'viewer' });
    viewer.store.write((db) => {
      db.projects = db.projects.filter((p) => p.code !== 'T-01');
    });
    const second = await renderProjects({ dataLayer: viewer, team: true });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Projects in this workspace will appear here.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    second.unmount();
    const { user } = await renderProjects();
    await screen.findAllByRole('article');
    await user.type(search(), 'zzz');
    expect(await screen.findByText('No matching projects')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Try other words or clear the filters.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: 'Clear filters' }).at(-1)!);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('article')).toHaveLength(6);
  });

  it('keeps viewers from creating and tells them why', async () => {
    const { user } = await renderProjects({ dataLayer: createTestDataLayer({ teamRole: 'viewer' }), team: true });
    const newProject = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'New project' });
    expect(newProject).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    await user.hover(newProject);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('tooltip')).toHaveTextContent("Viewers can't make changes in this workspace");
    await user.click(newProject);
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('none');
  });

  it('opens the members of a team workspace and has no Members button in a personal one', async () => {
    const team = await renderProjects({ team: true });
    await team.user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Members' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('workspaceMembers');
    team.unmount();
    await renderProjects();
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Members' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`e2e/projects.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/home');
  await page.getByRole('navigation', { name: 'Primary' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Projects' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Projects' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('article').first()).toBeVisible();
});

test('the list layout survives a reload', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('radio', { name: 'List' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Projects' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Projects' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('search and filters stay in the URL across a reload', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Search projects' }).fill('harbor');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Filters' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Status', { exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByRole('option', { name: 'Active' }).click();
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/q=harbor/);
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole('article')).toHaveCount(1);
  await expect(page.getByText('Status: Active')).toBeVisible();
});

test("a project's attributes can be edited", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Metro Line Extension' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Edit project' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /Attributes/ }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Status', { exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByRole('option', { name: 'On hold' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save changes' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Project updated')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('article').filter({ hasText: 'Metro Line Extension' }).getByText('On hold')).toBeVisible();
});

test('a project can be deleted after typing its ID', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Old Mill Housing' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Delete project' }).click();
  const dialog = page.getByRole('alertdialog', { name: 'Delete Old Mill Housing?' });
  await dialog.getByLabel('Project ID').fill('006');
  await dialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete project' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Project deleted')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('article')).toHaveCount(5);
});
```

### Task 20: Files page

**Spec:** §6.3 (every region, NotFound rules, loading, empty states), §6.1 (`TagBadges`, viewer rule, "Editor not available", Formats), §6.4 (New schedule row: the suggestion follows the chosen project; §6.5 Schedule code "already used" against the cached list), §6.6 (Files columns hidden below 1024 / 768 px), §9 (Routes and pages; `ProjectPlaceholderPage`; `router.test.tsx`).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/pages/ProjectFilesPage.tsx`, `src/pages/ProjectFilesPage.test.tsx`, `src/features/files/SchedulesTable.tsx`, `src/components/common/TagBadges.tsx`, `src/components/common/TagBadges.test.tsx`
- Modify: `src/app/router.tsx`, `src/app/router.test.tsx` ('names the project on project pages' uses `/dashboard` and the heading "Riverside Office Tower · Dashboard"), `src/components/common/ProjectPlaceholderPage.tsx` (`section: 'Dashboard' | 'Schedule'`), `src/components/common/ProjectPlaceholderPage.test.tsx` (its `/files` routes become `/dashboard`), `src/features/schedules/NewScheduleDialog.tsx`, `src/features/schedules/NewScheduleDialog.test.tsx` (in Task 3's 'suggests a code, checks it and shows a taken one under Code' the taken code now reads the form copy "This code is already used in this project", with no request sent)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `useProject`, `useWorkspaces`, `useProjectSchedules`, `DataTable`, `StatusBadge`, `EmptyState`, `coverFor`, `relativeTime`, `useNow`, `shortDate`, `canEdit`, Tasks 3 and 7's `codes.ts` and `makeNewScheduleSchema`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  export function ProjectFilesPage(): ReactElement;   // route element of 'w/:workspaceId/p/:projectId/files'
  export function SchedulesTable(props: { project: ProjectSummary; workspace: WorkspaceSummary; showArchived: boolean;
    renderMenu?: (schedule: Schedule, schedules: readonly Schedule[]) => ReactNode }): ReactElement;   // Task 21 passes the menu
  export function TagBadges({ schedule }: { schedule: Pick<Schedule, 'isCurrent' | 'baseline'> }): ReactElement | null; // "Current", then "Primary baseline" or "Baseline"
  ```
- Page (§6.3 copy): `NotFoundPage` when the project is unknown, its `workspaceId` differs from `:workspaceId`, or that workspace isn't in `useWorkspaces` (a share-only project); while loading, the h1 holds a skeleton and the visually hidden text "Loading project…". Header: cover thumbnail 96×64 (photo or `coverFor`); h1 "{project name}" + code chip + `StatusBadge`; muted "{client} · {city}" (missing parts left out); "Edit project" (`Pencil`; viewers "Project details") → `editProject` `{ workspaceId, projectId }`; "Import schedule" (`FileUp`) → `importSchedule` `{ projectId }`; "+ New schedule" (primary) → `newSchedule` `{ projectId }`; for viewers the last two get `aria-disabled="true"`, do nothing and show the Tooltip "Viewers can't make changes in this workspace". Counters, in a `<section aria-label="Project summary">` holding a `<dl>` (labels in `<dt>`, values in the next `<dd>`): "Status date" (`shortDate`, "—"), "Schedules" (`scheduleCount`), "Current schedule" (name or "None") + "Open current schedule" (hidden without one; `aria-disabled="true"` with the Tooltip "The schedule editor isn't available yet."). Toolbar: checkbox "Show archived" bound to URL `archived=1` (replace).
- `SchedulesTable`: `DataTable` caption "Schedules", no row link, columns "Name" (plain text with `title` = name, code and the notes' first 200 characters on separate lines; archived rows `text-muted-foreground` with an "Archived" badge after the name), "Code" (`hidden md:table-cell`), "Tags" (`TagBadges`), "Status date", "Update" ("Update {n}" or "—"; `hidden md:table-cell`), "Last modified" (`relativeTime`; `hidden md:table-cell`), "Activities" (`hidden lg:table-cell`), "Source" ("Imported · {file name}" or "Blank"; `hidden lg:table-cell`), "Notes" (`StickyNote` + the first line, truncated; empty without notes; `hidden lg:table-cell`), and the menu column with the visually hidden header "Actions"; "—" for every other empty cell; order as `listSchedules`; archived rows only with `showArchived`. Empty states (§6.3): editors `ChartGantt`, "No schedules yet", "Create a blank schedule or import one from your planning tool." + "New schedule" and "Import schedule"; viewers "No schedules yet" / "Schedules in this project will appear here.".
- New schedule: Code follows `suggestNewScheduleCode(codes of the chosen project)` (from `useProjectSchedules`) until the user edits it; the schema is `makeNewScheduleSchema(those codes)`, so a taken code reads "This code is already used in this project" before any request.

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { TagBadges } from '@/components/common/TagBadges';

describe('TagBadges', () => {
  it('names the current schedule and the baselines, and nothing else', () => {
    const { rerender, container } = render(<TagBadges schedule={{ isCurrent: true, baseline: 'primary' }} />);
    expect([...container.querySelectorAll('span')].map((s) => s.textContent)).toEqual(['Current', 'Primary baseline']);
    rerender(<TagBadges schedule={{ isCurrent: false, baseline: 'baseline' }} />);
    expect(screen.getByText('Baseline')).toBeInTheDocument();
    rerender(<TagBadges schedule={{ isCurrent: false, baseline: 'none' }} />);
    expect(container).toBeEmptyDOMElement();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ProjectFilesPage } from '@/pages/ProjectFilesPage';
import { createTestDataLayer, type TestDataLayer } from '@/test/fakes';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

function renderFiles(code: string, options: { dataLayer?: TestDataLayer; search?: string; workspaceId?: string } = {}) {
  const dataLayer = options.dataLayer ?? createTestDataLayer();
  const project = dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === code)!;
  const utils = renderWithProviders(<ProjectFilesPage />, {
    route: `/w/${options.workspaceId ?? project.workspaceId}/p/${project.id}/files${options.search ?? ''}`,
    path: '/w/:workspaceId/p/:projectId/files',
    dataLayer,
  });
  return { ...utils, project };
}
const rowNames = (table: HTMLElement) => within(table).getAllByRole('row').slice(1).map((row) => within(row).getAllByRole('cell')[0]!.textContent);

describe('ProjectFilesPage', () => {
  it("shows Not found for an unknown project, another workspace's and a share-only one", async () => {
    const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
    const { unmount } = renderFiles('001', { dataLayer, workspaceId: dataLayer.store.read().workspaces[1]!.id });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Page not found' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    unmount();
    const shared = createTestDataLayer();
    shared.store.write((db) => {
      const mert = db.users.find((u) => u.firstName === 'Mert')!;
      const other = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: 'Northline Contractors', kind: 'team' as const, createdAt: db.seededAt };
      db.workspaces.push(other);
      db.members.push({ workspaceId: other.id, userId: mert.id, role: 'owner', joinedAt: db.seededAt });
      db.projects.push({ ...db.projects[0]!, id: crypto.randomUUID(), workspaceId: other.id, code: 'N-01', name: 'Coastal Road Widening' });
    });
    renderFiles('N-01', { dataLayer: shared });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Page not found' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows a skeleton with hidden text while the project loads', () => {
    renderFiles('001', { dataLayer: createTestDataLayer({ overrides: { getProject: () => new Promise(() => {}) } }) });
    expect(screen.getByText('Loading project…')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the header and opens the dialogs for this project', async () => {
    const { user, project } = renderFiles('003');
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: /Harbor Logistics Hub/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Aegean Ports · Izmir')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'New schedule' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('newSchedule');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Cancel' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Edit project' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('editProject');
    expect(project.code).toBe('003');
  });

  it('shows the counters and a disabled "Open current schedule" with its note', async () => {
    const { user } = renderFiles('004');
    const counters = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Project summary' });
    expect(within(counters).getByText('Schedules').nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent('2');
    expect(within(counters).getByText('Current schedule').nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent('Recovery plan');
    const open = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open current schedule' });
    expect(open).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    await user.hover(open);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('tooltip')).toHaveTextContent("The schedule editor isn't available yet.");
  });

  it('lists the schedules with their columns and shows archived ones when asked', async () => {
    const { user } = renderFiles('004');
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Schedules' });
    expect(within(table).getAllByRole('columnheader').map((h) => h.textContent)).toEqual([
      'Name', 'Code', 'Tags', 'Status date', 'Update', 'Last modified', 'Activities', 'Source', 'Notes', 'Actions',
    ]);
    await waitFor(() => expect(rowNames(table)).toEqual(['Recovery plan', 'Baseline']));
    expect(within(table).getByText('Recovery plan').closest('[title]')).toHaveAttribute('title', 'Recovery plan\nRP-01\nAgreed with the client on site.');
    expect(within(within(table).getAllByRole('row')[1]!).getByText('Current')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Show archived' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('location')).toHaveTextContent('archived=1');
    await waitFor(() => expect(rowNames(table)).toEqual(['Recovery plan', 'Recovery plan draftArchived', 'Baseline']));
  });

  it('shows "—" for missing values, never "null", for a P2-era schedule', async () => {
    const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
    dataLayer.store.write((db) => {
      const library = db.projects.find((p) => p.code === '004')!;
      db.schedules.push({ ...db.schedules.find((s) => s.projectId === library.id)!, id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: 'Old import', code: null, statusDate: null, notes: null, isCurrent: false, baseline: 'none', updateNumber: null, source: 'import', sourceFileName: 'old.xer', sourceFormat: 'xer' });
    });
    renderFiles('004', { dataLayer });
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Schedules' });
    const row = (await within(table).findByText('Old import')).closest('tr')!;
    expect(within(row).getAllByText('—').length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
    expect(within(row).getByText('Imported · old.xer')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(table).not.toHaveTextContent('null');
  });

  it('shows the empty state to editors and viewers', async () => {
    const { unmount } = renderFiles('002');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Create a blank schedule or import one from your planning tool.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button', { name: 'Import schedule' })).toHaveLength(2);
    unmount();
    const viewer = createTestDataLayer({ teamRole: 'viewer' });
    viewer.store.write((db) => {
      const tunnel = db.projects.find((p) => p.code === 'T-01')!;
      db.schedules = db.schedules.filter((s) => s.projectId !== tunnel.id);
    });
    renderFiles('T-01', { dataLayer: viewer });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Schedules in this project will appear here.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('keeps viewers from writing and tells them why', async () => {
    const { user } = renderFiles('T-01', { dataLayer: createTestDataLayer({ teamRole: 'viewer' }) });
    const newSchedule = await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'New schedule' });
    expect(newSchedule).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import schedule' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    await user.hover(newSchedule);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('tooltip')).toHaveTextContent("Viewers can't make changes in this workspace");
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Project details' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('editProject');
  });
});
```

### Task 21: Schedule menu, details and deletion

**Spec:** §6.3 (Row menu row, "Delete schedule"), §6.1 (viewer rule, state rule D11, "Editor not available"), §6.4 (Schedule details row; `DialogKind` and `DialogOptions`; D39), §6.5 (Schedule details rows), §6.6 (radio groups, `MENU_ITEM_FOCUS_CLASSES`), §4.3 R2–R8, §7 Components and E2E (mock) `files.spec.ts`.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/features/files/ScheduleMenu.tsx`, `src/features/files/ScheduleMenu.test.tsx`, `src/features/files/ScheduleDetailsDialog.tsx`, `src/features/files/ScheduleDetailsDialog.test.tsx`, `src/features/files/DeleteScheduleDialog.tsx`, `src/features/files/details-schema.ts`, `src/components/ui/radio-group.tsx` (shadcn), `e2e/files.spec.ts`
- Modify: `src/features/files/codes.ts`, `src/features/files/codes.test.ts`, `src/features/dialogs/DialogsProvider.tsx`, `src/features/dialogs/DialogHost.tsx`, `src/pages/ProjectFilesPage.tsx` (passes `renderMenu`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 7–8 and 15 hooks (`useSetCurrentSchedule`, `useSetSchedulesArchived`, `useDeleteSchedules`, `useUpdateScheduleDetails`, `useDownloadSourceFile`), `suggestCodeFor`, `isCodeTaken`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // DialogKind adds 'scheduleDetails' and 'importReport' (Task 23 renders the second); DialogOptions gains scheduleId?: ID;
  //   both open with { projectId, scheduleId }
  // codes.ts
  export function suggestUpdateNumber(schedules: ReadonlyArray<Pick<Schedule, 'id' | 'statusDate' | 'updateNumber'>>, scheduleId: ID, statusDate: ISODate | null): number;
  // ScheduleMenu.tsx
  export function ScheduleMenu(props: { schedule: Schedule; schedules: readonly Schedule[]; project: ProjectSummary; workspace: WorkspaceSummary }): ReactElement;
  // ScheduleDetailsDialog.tsx
  export function ScheduleDetailsDialog({ projectId, scheduleId }: { projectId: ID; scheduleId: ID }): ReactElement;
  // DeleteScheduleDialog.tsx
  export function DeleteScheduleDialog(props: { schedule: Schedule; open: boolean; onOpenChange(open: boolean): void }): ReactElement;
  ```
- `suggestUpdateNumber` (§6.4): 1 + the number of the project's other numbered schedules whose status date is on or before `statusDate`, or the highest number + 1 when `statusDate` is null; when taken, the next free number above it.
- `ScheduleMenu` (trigger `MoreHorizontal`, aria-label "Actions for {name}"), items in this order: "Open" (disabled, with the muted second line "The schedule editor isn't available yet." linked by `aria-describedby`); "Make current" (editors; not on the current or an archived schedule); "Edit details" (editors) → `scheduleDetails`; "Archive" (editors; not on the current schedule) or "Restore" (editors; archived); "Download original file" (`source === 'import'`, P2-era imports included) → `useDownloadSourceFile`; "View import report" (`hasImportReport`) → `importReport`; separator; "Delete" (owners; not on the current schedule while the project has other schedules) → `DeleteScheduleDialog`. Toasts "Schedule is now current", "Schedule archived", "Schedule restored"; a failure toasts `errorMessage`.
- `DeleteScheduleDialog`: `AlertDialog` "Delete {name}?" / "This can't be undone. Stored original files are deleted too." / "Cancel" · "Delete" → toast "Schedule deleted"; a `submittingRef` makes two quick clicks one request.
- `ScheduleDetailsDialog` (`FormDialog` width `lg`): reads the schedule from `useProjectSchedules(projectId)`; title "Schedule details"; fields "Name", "Code" (required; hint "Letters, numbers, ., - or _ · up to 24 characters"; a schedule without a code starts with `suggestCodeFor`), "Status date", "Baseline" (radio group "Not a baseline", "Baseline", "Primary baseline"), "Update number" (a text input with `inputMode="numeric"`; hint "Leave empty if this isn't a status update."; when empty, the link-button "Use {n}" with `suggestUpdateNumber`), "Notes" (counter "{n}/2000"); read-only lines "Source" ("Imported from {file name}" or "Blank"), "Created {date}" (`shortDate`), "Last modified {relative}"; "Cancel" · "Save" / "Saving…"; success → toast "Details saved". Muted hints: choosing "Primary baseline" while another schedule is primary → "Comparisons against the primary baseline will use this schedule instead of {name}."; leaving "Primary baseline" on the primary one → "The project will have no primary baseline until you choose one.". `details-schema.ts`: name and code as `schedule-schema.ts` (code checked against the project's other codes), status date "Enter a valid date", update number "Enter a whole number from 1 to 999", notes "Use 2000 characters or fewer". A `conflict` puts its message under "Code" or "Update number" (by its copy); other failures toast.

```tsx
import { act, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { Role } from '@/data/types';
import { ScheduleMenu } from '@/features/files/ScheduleMenu';
import { saveFile } from '@/lib/download';
import { createTestDataLayer, type TestDataLayer } from '@/test/fakes';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

vi.mock('@/lib/download', () => ({ saveFile: vi.fn() }));

async function renderMenu(projectCode: string, scheduleCode: string, options: { teamRole?: Role; dataLayer?: TestDataLayer } = {}) {
  const dataLayer = options.dataLayer ?? createTestDataLayer({ teamRole: options.teamRole });
  const db = dataLayer.store.read();
  const projectId = db.projects.find((p) => p.code === projectCode)!.id;
  const project = await dataLayer.repository.getProject(projectId);
  const workspace = (await dataLayer.repository.listWorkspaces()).find((w) => w.id === project.workspaceId)!;
  const schedules = await dataLayer.repository.listSchedules(projectId);
  const schedule = schedules.find((s) => s.code === scheduleCode)!;
  const utils = renderWithProviders(<ScheduleMenu schedule={schedule} schedules={schedules} project={project} workspace={workspace} />, { dataLayer });
  await utils.user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `Actions for ${schedule.name}` }));
  return { ...utils, schedule, dataLayer };
}
const items = () => screen.getAllByRole('menuitem').map((item) => item.textContent);

describe('ScheduleMenu', () => {
  it('offers what the role and the schedule state allow', async () => {
    const current = await renderMenu('004', 'RP-01');
    expect(items()).toEqual(['OpenThe schedule editor isn\'t available yet.', 'Edit details']);
    current.unmount();
    const baseline = await renderMenu('004', 'BL-01');
    expect(items()).toEqual(['OpenThe schedule editor isn\'t available yet.', 'Make current', 'Edit details', 'Archive', 'Delete']);
    baseline.unmount();
    const draft = await renderMenu('004', 'RP-00');
    expect(items()).toEqual(['OpenThe schedule editor isn\'t available yet.', 'Edit details', 'Restore', 'Delete']);
    draft.unmount();
    await renderMenu('T-01', 'BL-03', { teamRole: 'viewer' });
    expect(items()).toEqual(['OpenThe schedule editor isn\'t available yet.']);
  });

  it('keeps "Open" disabled and describes why', async () => {
    await renderMenu('004', 'BL-01');
    const open = screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: /^Open/ });
    expect(open).toHaveAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    expect(open).toHaveAccessibleDescription("The schedule editor isn't available yet.");
  });

  it('makes a schedule current and archives another, with their toasts', async () => {
    const first = await renderMenu('003', 'UP-01');
    await first.user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Make current' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Schedule is now current')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(first.dataLayer.store.read().schedules.find((s) => s.code === 'UP-01' && s.isCurrent)).toBeDefined();
    first.unmount();
    const second = await renderMenu('003', 'BL-01');
    await second.user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Archive' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Schedule archived')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('deletes a schedule after confirming, once for two quick clicks', async () => {
    const { user, dataLayer } = await renderMenu('004', 'BL-01');
    const remove = vi.spyOn(dataLayer.repository, 'deleteSchedules');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Delete' }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('alertdialog', { name: 'Delete Baseline?' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveAccessibleDescription("This can't be undone. Stored original files are deleted too.");
    const confirm = within(dialog).getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete' });
    act(() => {
      confirm.click();
      confirm.click();
    });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Schedule deleted')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(remove).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("downloads an import's original and opens its report", async () => {
    const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
    const { makeImportScheduleInput } = await import('@/test/fakes');
    const metro = dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === '002')!;
    await dataLayer.repository.importSchedule(await makeImportScheduleInput(metro.id));
    const { user } = await renderMenu('002', 'NTH-A', { dataLayer });
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Download original file' }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(vi.mocked(saveFile)).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.any(File), 'two-projects.xer'));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for North Plant' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'View import report' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('importReport');
  });
});
```

`src/features/files/ScheduleDetailsDialog.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createTestDataLayer, type TestDataLayer } from '@/test/fakes';
import { OpenDialog, renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

async function openDetails(projectCode: string, scheduleCode: string | null, dataLayer: TestDataLayer = createTestDataLayer(), name?: string) {
  const db = dataLayer.store.read();
  const project = db.projects.find((p) => p.code === projectCode)!;
  const schedule = db.schedules.find((s) => s.projectId === project.id && (name ? s.name === name : s.code === scheduleCode))!;
  const utils = renderWithProviders(<OpenDialog kind="scheduleDetails" options={{ projectId: project.id, scheduleId: schedule.id }} />, { dataLayer });
  await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Schedule details' });
  return { ...utils, schedule, dataLayer };
}

describe('ScheduleDetailsDialog', () => {
  it('saves the details of a schedule', async () => {
    const { user, schedule, dataLayer } = await openDetails('003', 'UP-01');
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Name')).toHaveValue('Update 1');
    expect(screen.getByText('Blank')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Notes'), 'Crane moved to the east gate.');
    expect(screen.getByText('29/2000')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Baseline' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Details saved')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(dataLayer.store.read().schedules.find((s) => s.id === schedule.id)).toMatchObject({ notes: 'Crane moved to the east gate.', baseline: 'baseline' });
  });

  it('starts a schedule without a code on the suggestion', async () => {
    const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
    dataLayer.store.write((db) => {
      const s = db.schedules.find((x) => x.code === 'UP-01')!;
      Object.assign(s, { code: null, source: 'import', sourceFileName: 'Rev7.XER', sourceFormat: 'xer' });
    });
    await openDetails('003', null, dataLayer, 'Update 1');
    expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Code')).toHaveValue('Rev7');
    expect(screen.getByText('Imported from Rev7.XER')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('offers the next free update number and shows a taken one under the field', async () => {
    const { user } = await openDetails('003', 'BL-01');
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Use 3' }));
    const number = screen.getByLabelText('Update number');
    expect(number).toHaveValue('3');
    await user.clear(number);
    await user.type(number, '1');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('This update number is already used in this project.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('explains what a baseline change does', async () => {
    const first = await openDetails('003', 'UP-01');
    await first.user.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Primary baseline' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('Comparisons against the primary baseline will use this schedule instead of Baseline.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    first.unmount();
    const second = await openDetails('003', 'BL-01');
    await second.user.click(screen.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Not a baseline' }));
    expect(screen.getByText('The project will have no primary baseline until you choose one.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('checks the fields and keeps the form open', async () => {
    const { user } = await openDetails('003', 'UP-02');
    const code = screen.getByLabelText('Code');
    await user.clear(code);
    await user.type(code, 'up-01');
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Update number'), '0');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('This code is already used in this project')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Enter a whole number from 1 to 999')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(code).toHaveFocus());
  });
});
```

(BL-01's status date is 60 days back, before both updates, so the rule gives 1; UP-01 holds 1 and UP-02 holds 2, so the next free number is 3. Typing 1 reaches the mock's R7 conflict, shown under the field.)

`e2e/files.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';

async function openHarbor(page: Page) {
  await page.goto('/home');
  await page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recent projects' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Harbor Logistics Hub' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: /Harbor Logistics Hub/ })).toBeVisible();
}
// The row whose text starts with the schedule's name ("Baseline" must not match "Primary baseline").
const row = (page: Page, name: string) => page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Schedules' }).getByRole('row').filter({ hasText: new RegExp(`^${name}`) });

test('another schedule becomes current and gets its details', async ({ page }) => {
  await openHarbor(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Update 1' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Make current' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Schedule is now current')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(row(page, 'Update 1')).toContainText('Current');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Update 1' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Edit details' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Primary baseline' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Notes').fill('Revised crane plan');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Details saved')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(row(page, 'Update 1')).toContainText('Primary baseline');
  await expect(row(page, 'Baseline')).not.toContainText('Primary baseline');
});

test('a schedule is archived, shown with Show archived, and restored', async ({ page }) => {
  await openHarbor(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Baseline' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Archive' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Schedule archived')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(row(page, 'Baseline')).toHaveCount(0);
  await page.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'Show archived' }).check();
  await expect(row(page, 'Baseline')).toContainText('Archived');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Baseline' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Restore' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Schedule restored')).toBeVisible();
});

test('a schedule is deleted after confirming', async ({ page }) => {
  await openHarbor(page);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for Baseline' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Delete' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('alertdialog', { name: 'Delete Baseline?' }).getByRole('button', { name: 'Delete' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Schedule deleted')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(row(page, 'Baseline')).toHaveCount(0);
});
```

**Spec:** §5.1 (steps 1–3), §5.2 (refusal copy), §5.7 (options, fields, preview), §6.4 (Import schedule row: title, project line, stepper, File and Columns), §6.5 (Import · File, Import · Columns), §5.9 "Copy details", §6.6 (radio and select roles), §9 (Schedules and import: `SCHEDULE_EXTENSIONS`, `FileDropzone`).

**Files:**
- Create: `src/features/import/ImportScheduleDialog.tsx`, `src/features/import/FileStep.tsx`, `src/features/import/ColumnsStep.tsx`, `src/features/import/import-schema.ts`, `src/features/import/copy-details.ts`, `src/features/import/copy-details.test.ts`, `src/features/import/ImportScheduleDialog.test.tsx`
- Modify: `src/components/common/FormDialog.tsx` (widths), `src/components/common/FileDropzone.tsx` (the xlsx MIME type), `src/features/schedules/schedule-schema.ts`, `src/features/schedules/schedule-schema.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 14's `useImportParser`, `ImportClientFactoryContext` and re-exported types; `ProjectField`; `useCurrentContext`; `FileDropzone`.
- Produces:
  ```ts
  // FormDialog: width gains '2xl' (760 px) and '3xl' (880 px)
  // schedule-schema.ts
  export const IMPORT_EXTENSIONS: readonly ['.xer', '.xml', '.xlsx', '.csv'];
  export const REFUSED_EXTENSIONS: Readonly<Record<'.mpp' | '.xls', string>>;   // §5.2 MPP and .xls messages
  export const importFileSchema: ZodType;   // "Choose a file to import" · "Use a .xer, .xml, .xlsx or .csv file" · "File must be 20 MB or smaller" · the .mpp / .xls copy
  // copy-details.ts
  export function copyDetailsText(details: { message: string; code: string | null; fileName: string; size: number; format: string | null; parserVersion: string | null }): string;
  // import-schema.ts
  export const IMPORT_FIELD_LABELS: Record<ImportField, string>;   // Activity code … Notes, §6.4 order
  export function checkColumns(table: TableMapping): Partial<Record<ImportField | 'headerRow' | 'hoursPerDay', string>>; // §6.5 Columns copy
  // ImportScheduleDialog.tsx — registered in Task 23
  export function ImportScheduleDialog({ projectId }: { projectId?: ID }): ReactElement;
  ```
- `copyDetailsText` lines: `Message: {message}`, `Code: {code}` (left out when null), `File: {fileName} ({fileSize(size)})`, `Detected format: {format ?? 'none'}`, `Parser version: {parserVersion ?? 'unknown'}`, joined by `\n`.
- Dialog (§6.4 copy): `FormDialog` title "Import schedule"; width `xl` on File, `2xl` on Columns, `3xl` on Review; the line "Into {workspace} › {code} {project}" on every step once the project is known; stepper "File" · "Columns" · "Review" ("Columns" only for xlsx and CSV); descriptions File "Upload a schedule exported from your planning tool.", Columns "Match the file's columns to schedule fields.", Review "Check what will be imported.". One `useImportParser()` per dialog.
- File step: "Project" (`ProjectField`) when opened without `projectId`, else the read-only project line; `FileDropzone` label "Schedule file", accepting the four extensions plus `.mpp`, `.xls` and the xlsx MIME type, hint "Primavera P6 (.xer), MS Project XML (.xml), Excel (.xlsx) or CSV · up to 20 MB"; a chosen file passing `importFileSchema` is inspected: status "Reading…", then the format name ("Primavera P6 XER {version}", "Microsoft Project XML", "Excel workbook", "CSV file") or the refusal message with the button "Copy details" (clipboard + toast "Details copied"); only the answer for the latest file counts (a request counter drops older ones); primary "Next" / "Reading file…" goes to Columns (tables) or Review (XER, MSPDI).
- Columns step (§6.4 copy): title line "Columns · {file name}"; the options "Sheet" (xlsx), "Header row", "Text encoding" (CSV: "UTF-8", "Turkish (Windows-1254)", "Western European (Windows-1252)" with the muted "Detected: {label}"; with a byte-order mark the read-only line "UTF-8 (byte-order mark)", "UTF-16 LE (byte-order mark)" or "UTF-16 BE (byte-order mark)"), "Delimiter" ("Comma", "Semicolon", "Tab"), "Decimal separator" ("Comma (2,5)", "Period (2.5)"), "Date format", "Duration unit" ("Days", "Hours"; hint "For durations without a unit"), "Hours per day", "Predecessors refer to" ("Activity code", "Task ID"), "Rows with problems" ("Import them, leaving bad cells empty", "Skip them"); the section "Columns" with one select per field ("Not used" + "{column letter} · {header}" for each non-empty header); the section "Preview" (a table captioned "First 10 rows") from `parser.preview`, mapped columns emphasized and invalid cells marked; every option change runs `preview` again (latest answer wins). Footer "Back" · "Next" / "Checking…"; "Next" runs `checkColumns`, shows each message under its control and focuses the first, else moves on (Task 23 analyzes there).

```ts
import { copyDetailsText } from '@/features/import/copy-details';

describe('copyDetailsText', () => {
  it('lists the message, code, file, format and parser version', () => {
    expect(copyDetailsText({ message: 'The file is empty.', code: 'FILE_EMPTY', fileName: 'a.xer', size: 2048, format: 'Primavera P6 XER', parserVersion: '1.0.0' })).toBe(

      'Message: x\nFile: b.db (10 B)\nDetected format: none\nParser version: unknown',
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
it('accepts the import extensions and refuses MPP and XLS with their messages', () => {
  const issues = (name: string) => {
    const result = importFileSchema.safeParse(new File(['x'], name));
    return result.success ? [] : result.error.issues.map((i) => i.message);
  };
  expect(['a.XER', 'b.xml', 'c.xlsx', 'd.CSV'].flatMap(issues)).toEqual([]);
  expect(issues('plan.pdf')).toEqual(['Use a .xer, .xml, .xlsx or .csv file']);
  expect(issues('plan.mpp')).toEqual(["MPP files can't be imported. In Microsoft Project, save the file as XML and import that."]);
  expect(issues('old.xls')).toEqual(["Older Excel workbooks (.xls) can't be imported. In Excel, save the file as .xlsx and import that."]);
});
```

`src/features/import/ImportScheduleDialog.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import turkish from '@/import/__fixtures__/turkish-1254.csv?bytes';
import xer from '@/import/__fixtures__/two-projects.xer?bytes';
import { createImportClient } from '@/import/client';
import { ImportScheduleDialog } from '@/features/import/ImportScheduleDialog';
import { ImportClientFactoryContext, type ImportClient, type InspectOutcome } from '@/hooks/useImportParser';
import { createTestDataLayer } from '@/test/fakes';
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

const realClient = () => createImportClient({ createWorker: () => null });
function renderImport(options: { projectCode?: string; client?: () => ImportClient } = {}) {
  const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
  const project = options.projectCode ? dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === options.projectCode)! : undefined;
  const utils = renderWithProviders(
    <ImportClientFactoryContext value={options.client ?? realClient}>
      <ImportScheduleDialog projectId={project?.id} />
    </ImportClientFactoryContext>,
    { route: '/home', path: '/home', dataLayer },
  );
  return { ...utils, project, dataLayer };
}
const dropzone = () => screen.getByLabelText('Schedule file', { selector: 'input' });
function deferred<T>() {
  let resolve!: (value: T) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<T>((r) => { resolve = r; });
  return { promise, resolve };
}

describe('ImportScheduleDialog — File and Columns', () => {
  it('asks for the project first from Home and shows where the import goes', async () => {
    renderImport();
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Import schedule' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveAccessibleDescription('Upload a schedule exported from your planning tool.');
    await waitFor(() => expect(within(dialog).getByLabelText('Project')).toHaveTextContent('Riverside Office Tower'));
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Into My workspace › 001 Riverside Office Tower')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByText('Primavera P6 (.xer), MS Project XML (.xml), Excel (.xlsx) or CSV · up to 20 MB')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('reads an XER and shows its format', async () => {
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File([xer], 'north.xer'));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Primavera P6 XER 20.12')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText('Columns')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('refuses a file by its name, its size or its content, and copies the details', async () => {
    const refused: InspectOutcome = { kind: 'refused', message: 'This is a database file, not a schedule export. Export the project as XER from P6 or as XML from Microsoft Project.', fileName: 'db.xer', size: 16, parserVersion: '1.0.0' };
    const client = { inspect: vi.fn(async () => refused), dispose: vi.fn() } as unknown as ImportClient;
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003', client: () => client });
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File(['x'], 'plan.mpp'));
    expect(await screen.findByText("MPP files can't be imported. In Microsoft Project, save the file as XML and import that.")).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File(['SQLite format 3'], 'db.xer'));
    expect(await screen.findByText(refused.message)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Copy details' }));
    expect(await navigator.clipboard.readText()).toContain('Message: This is a database file');
    expect(await screen.findByText('Details copied')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('drops a stale answer when another file is chosen, and disposes the parser on close', async () => {
    const first = deferred<InspectOutcome>();
    const client = {
      inspect: vi.fn().mockReturnValueOnce(first.promise).mockResolvedValueOnce({ kind: 'refused', message: 'Second answer', fileName: 'b.xer', size: 1, parserVersion: '1.0.0' }),
      dispose: vi.fn(),
    } as unknown as ImportClient;
    const { user, unmount } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003', client: () => client });
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File(['a'], 'a.xer'));
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File(['b'], 'b.xer'));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Second answer')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await act(async () => first.resolve({ kind: 'refused', message: 'First answer', fileName: 'a.xer', size: 1, parserVersion: '1.0.0' }));
    expect(screen.queryByText('First answer')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    unmount();
    expect(client.dispose).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  });

  it('goes to Columns for a CSV with its detected options, mapping and preview', async () => {
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File([turkish], 'liste.csv'));
    expect(await screen.findByText('CSV file')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Columns · liste.csv')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Import schedule' })).toHaveAccessibleDescription("Match the file's columns to schedule fields.");
    expect(screen.getByText('Detected: Turkish (Windows-1254)')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Delimiter')).toHaveTextContent('Semicolon');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Decimal separator')).toHaveTextContent('Comma (2,5)');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Activity code')).toHaveTextContent('A · Aktivite Kodu');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Task ID')).toHaveTextContent('Not used');
    const preview = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'First 10 rows' });
    expect(within(preview).getByText('Şantiye kurulumu')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('checks the Columns step before moving on', async () => {
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File([turkish], 'liste.csv'));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }));
    await screen.findByText('Columns · liste.csv');
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Activity code'));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('option', { name: 'Not used' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Notes'));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('option', { name: 'B · Aktivite Adı' }));
    const header = screen.getByLabelText('Header row');
    await user.clear(header);
    await user.type(header, '0');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Map a column to Activity code')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByText('Each column can be used once').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(screen.getByText('Enter a row number from 1 to 50')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('reads the preview again when an option changes', async () => {
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await user.upload(dropzone(), new File([turkish], 'liste.csv'));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }));
    await screen.findByText('Columns · liste.csv');
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Text encoding'));
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('option', { name: 'Western European (Windows-1252)' }));
    const preview = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'First 10 rows' });
    expect(await within(preview).findByText('Kazý iþleri')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

(Bytes 0xFD and 0xFE read as `ý` and `þ` in Windows-1252, so "Kazı işleri" turns into "Kazý iþleri".)

- `ImportScheduleInput`'s `stored`, `statusDate`, `activityCount`, `sourceProjectCode`, `mapping` and `report` become required; both adapters drop the interim branch (Supabase: the extension-derived format and its own gzip; mock: the extension-derived `sourceFormat`). Every `importSchedule` call in tests passes `makeImportScheduleInput(...)`; the contract case 'records imported schedules and rejects unknown projects' asserts `hasImportReport: true, hasImportMapping: true` on its import. `DialogHost` renders the new `ImportScheduleDialog` for `importSchedule`.
- Review step (§6.4 copy; Columns → Review and File → Review run `analyze` first, "Checking…" on the Columns button): for an XER of several projects, the table "Projects in this file" with one row per project, each analyzed with its `sourceProjectId`: radio (aria-label "Use {code} · {name}"), "Source" ("{file name} · {project code}"), "Activities", "Issues" ("{e} errors · {w} warnings"); the first row without errors starts chosen, flagged projects first, each group in file order (D15); a row with errors can't be chosen and shows "Fix the errors before importing. This schedule can't be imported as it is." with "Copy details" (its first error's message and code, `copyDetailsText`). Then the fields "Name" (the file name without extension, or the XER project's name), "Code" (`suggestImportCode` with the project's codes and the XER short name), "Status date" (hint "From the file", "Not in the file. The earliest task start is filled in." or "Not in the file. Leave it empty to use the project's status date."; optional, a value that isn't a real date reads "Enter a valid date"; a valid change runs `analyze` again with `{ statusDate: field || project.statusDate }`, D37); then `ReportView` in `review` mode: "File" ({name} · {size}), "Format" ("Primavera P6 XER {version}", "Microsoft Project XML", "Excel workbook", "CSV file"; "(read from the content)" when `formatFromContent`), "Activity codes" (MSPDI select "Unique ID", "ID", "Text1"…; with "ID" the hint "IDs change when rows are inserted, so later updates may not match."), "Text encoding" (XER select with "Detected: {label}"; CSV a line; a BOM the read-only line), "First activities" (XER, CSV), "Dates" ("{start} – {finish}"), "Scheduling settings" ("{setting}: {value}"); the counts table with the visually hidden caption "Counts" (rows "Calendars", "WBS elements", "Activities", "Relationships"; columns "Read", "Accepted", "Adjusted", "Skipped"); "Summary" ("Activities by type", "Started", "Completed", "Relationships by type", "Constraints", "Calendar exceptions"); "Checks" as a `<dl>` (each of the nine names of §6.4 in a `<dt>`, "No findings" or "{n} findings" in the next `<dd>`); "What happened to each part of the file" (three lists, "{element} ({n})"); the muted note "The original file is kept with the schedule."; "Issues" with "{e} errors · {w} warnings · {i} notes" and the groups by code (title, count, expanding to message and location; the first 50 issues, then "Show all {n}"); link-buttons "Download CSV" · "Download JSON" (`useDownloadImportReport`, name from the Name field). Footer "Back" · "Import" / "Importing…". "Import" with no row chosen → the alert "Choose at least one schedule to import." (focused); with the only schedule in error → the alert "Fix the errors before importing. This schedule can't be imported as it is." (focused). Commit: `pack` → `importSchedule({ projectId, name, code, file, stored, statusDate: field || null, activityCount, sourceProjectCode, mapping, report })`; a `conflict` puts its message under "Code"; success → toast "Schedule imported" with the action "View report" (opens `importReport` for the new schedule) and the app opens the project's files page; a `submittingRef` makes repeated clicks one import. Below 768 px the projects table becomes a list of cards.
- Import report dialog (§6.4 copy): title "Import report"; description "{file name} · imported {relativeTime(createdAt)}" (the schedule from `useProjectSchedules(projectId)`, D39); `ReportView` in `stored` mode (selects become lines; "Activity codes" from `activityCodeField`; no "First activities"); when the stored issues are fewer than the totals, "Showing {k} of {n} issues. The totals count every issue."; "Download CSV" · "Download JSON"; footer "Close".

```tsx
describe('ImportScheduleDialog — Review and import', () => {
  async function toReview(user: ReturnType<typeof renderImport>['user'], file: File) {
    await user.upload(dropzone(), file);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }));
    if (file.name.endsWith('.csv')) await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }));
    return screen.findByText('Check what will be imported.');
  }

  it('lists the projects of a two-project XER and starts on the flagged one', async () => {
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await toReview(user, new File([xer], 'north.xer'));
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Projects in this file' });
    expect(within(table).getByRole('radio', { name: 'Use NTH-A · North Plant' })).toBeChecked();
    expect(within(table).getByText('north.xer · NTH-B')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Name')).toHaveValue('North Plant');
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Code')).toHaveValue('NTH-A');
    await user.click(within(table).getByRole('radio', { name: 'Use NTH-B · North Annex' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Code')).toHaveValue('NTH-B'));
  });

  it('shows the report view of the chosen schedule', async () => {
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await toReview(user, new File([xer], 'north.xer'));
    const counts = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Counts' });
    expect(within(counts).getAllByRole('row').map((r) => r.textContent)).toEqual([
      'ReadAcceptedAdjustedSkipped', 'Calendars3310', 'WBS elements2200', 'Activities6610', 'Relationships6511',
    ]);
    expect(screen.getByText('Primavera P6 XER 20.12')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Links to other projects or files').nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent('1 findings');
    expect(screen.getByText('Progress without an actual start').nextElementSibling).toHaveTextContent('No findings');
    expect(screen.getByText('0 errors · 4 warnings · 3 notes')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('The original file is kept with the schedule.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Mobilisation')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('runs the checks again with the status date it will store', async () => {
    const client = realClient();
    const analyze = vi.spyOn(client, 'analyze');
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003', client: () => client });
    await toReview(user, new File([xer], 'north.xer'));
    const date = screen.getByLabelText('Status date');
    expect(date).toHaveValue('2026-10-15');
    expect(screen.getByText('From the file')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.clear(date);
    await user.type(date, '2026-09-01');
    await waitFor(() => expect(analyze).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ sourceProjectId: expect.any(String) }), { statusDate: '2026-09-01' }));
  });

  it('imports once, lands on the files page and offers the report', async () => {
    const { user, dataLayer, project } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    const importSchedule = vi.spyOn(dataLayer.repository, 'importSchedule');
    await toReview(user, new File([xer], 'north.xer'));
    const button = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import' });
    act(() => {
      button.click();
      button.click();
    });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Schedule imported')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(importSchedule).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(importSchedule.mock.calls[0]![0]).toMatchObject({ name: 'North Plant', code: 'NTH-A', statusDate: '2026-10-15', activityCount: 6, sourceProjectCode: 'NTH-A', mapping: { sourceProjectId: '101' } });
    expect(screen.getByTestId('location')).toHaveTextContent(`/w/${project!.workspaceId}/p/${project!.id}/files`);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'View report' }));
    expect(screen.getByTestId('dialog')).toHaveTextContent('importReport');
  });

  it('shows a taken code under Code', async () => {
    const { user, dataLayer } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    dataLayer.repository.importSchedule = () => Promise.reject(new RepositoryError('conflict', 'This code is already used in this project.'));
    await toReview(user, new File([xer], 'north.xer'));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('This code is already used in this project.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Code')).toHaveFocus());
  });

  it('names what blocks Import when every schedule has errors', async () => {
    const broken = new TextEncoder().encode('ERMHDR\t20.12\n%T\tPROJECT\n%F\tproj_id\tproj_short_name\n%R\t1\tP1\n%R\t2\tP2\n%E\n');
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await toReview(user, new File([broken], 'empty.xer'));
    expect(screen.getAllByText("Fix the errors before importing. This schedule can't be imported as it is.").length).toBe(2);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('Choose at least one schedule to import.');
  });

  it('refuses to import a single schedule with errors', async () => {
    const duplicate = new TextEncoder().encode('Code,Name\nA1,One\nA1,Two\n');
    const { user } = renderImport({ projectCode: '003' });
    await toReview(user, new File([duplicate], 'dup.csv'));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent("Fix the errors before importing. This schedule can't be imported as it is.");
  });
});
```

(Each of `empty.xer`'s two projects has no activities, so both rows carry `FILE_NO_ACTIVITIES` and neither starts chosen. Import `RepositoryError` from `@/data/errors`.)

`src/features/import/ImportReportDialog.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { saveFile } from '@/lib/download';
import { createTestDataLayer, makeImportScheduleInput } from '@/test/fakes';
import { OpenDialog, renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render';

vi.mock('@/lib/download', () => ({ saveFile: vi.fn() }));

async function openReport(patchIssues?: (count: number) => void) {
  const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
  const metro = dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === '002')!;
  const schedule = await dataLayer.repository.importSchedule(await makeImportScheduleInput(metro.id));
  patchIssues?.(0);
  const utils = renderWithProviders(<OpenDialog kind="importReport" options={{ projectId: metro.id, scheduleId: schedule.id }} />, { dataLayer });
  return { ...utils, dataLayer, schedule };
}

describe('ImportReportDialog', () => {
  it('shows the stored report read-only with the import time', async () => {
    await openReport();
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Import report' });
    expect(dialog).toHaveAccessibleDescription(/^two-projects\.xer · imported /);
    expect(within(dialog).queryByRole('combobox')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).queryByText('First activities')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(dialog).getByRole('table', { name: 'Counts' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('says when the stored list is shorter than the totals', async () => {
    const dataLayer = createTestDataLayer();
    const metro = dataLayer.store.read().projects.find((p) => p.code === '002')!;
    const input = await makeImportScheduleInput(metro.id);
    const report = { ...input.report!, issueTotals: { ...input.report!.issueTotals, warning: 900 } };
    const schedule = await dataLayer.repository.importSchedule({ ...input, report });
    renderWithProviders(<OpenDialog kind="importReport" options={{ projectId: metro.id, scheduleId: schedule.id }} />, { dataLayer });
    expect(await screen.findByText(`Showing ${report.issues.length} of ${900 + report.issueTotals.info} issues. The totals count every issue.`)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('downloads the report as CSV and as JSON', async () => {
    const { user } = await openReport();
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Download CSV' }));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Download JSON' }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(vi.mocked(saveFile).mock.calls.map(([, name]) => name)).toEqual(['North Plant - import report.csv', 'North Plant - import report.json']));
  });
});
```

`e2e/import.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';

const fixture = (name: string) => `src/import/__fixtures__/${name}`;

test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/home');
  await page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Recent projects' }).getByRole('link', { name: 'Metro Line Extension' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: /Metro Line Extension/ })).toBeVisible();
});

test('a Windows-1254 CSV is imported with its column mapping', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import schedule' }).first().click();
  await page.locator('input[type="file"]').setInputFiles(fixture('turkish-1254.csv'));
  await expect(page.getByText('CSV file')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Detected: Turkish (Windows-1254)')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'First 10 rows' })).toContainText('Şantiye kurulumu');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Code', { exact: true }).fill('CSV-1');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Schedule imported')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Schedules' })).toContainText('turkish-1254');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for turkish-1254' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'View import report' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Import report' })).toContainText('turkish-1254.csv');
});

test("the second project of a two-project XER is imported and its report opens from the row", async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import schedule' }).first().click();
  await page.locator('input[type="file"]').setInputFiles(fixture('two-projects.xer'));
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('radio', { name: 'Use NTH-B · North Annex' }).check();
  await expect(page.getByLabel('Code', { exact: true })).toHaveValue('NTH-B');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import', exact: true }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Schedule imported')).toBeVisible();
  const row = page.getByRole('table', { name: 'Schedules' }).getByRole('row').filter({ hasText: 'North Annex' });
  await expect(row).toContainText('Current');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Actions for North Annex' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'View import report' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Import report' })).toContainText('two-projects.xer');
});

test('the report downloads as CSV', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Import schedule' }).first().click();
  await page.locator('input[type="file"]').setInputFiles(fixture('two-projects.xer'));
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Next' }).click();
  const download = page.waitForEvent('download');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Download CSV' }).click();
  expect((await download).suggestedFilename()).toBe('North Plant - import report.csv');
});
```
